TL;DR: 本文发现基于采样和潜在世界模型的控制器存在"提案过度生成"问题：增加候选动作序列数量反而会导致选择可行性下降。对此提出相邻集合动作重构(ASAR)方法，通过从低成本提案中测量标准化早期动作前缀的密度并重构完整序列，显著提升了任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示并解决基于采样和潜在世界模型的控制器中存在的反直觉失败模式——增加候选序列数量反而降低选择可行性（称为"提案过度生成"），并对此提出有效的改进方法。

Method: 提出相邻集合动作重构(ASAR)方法：在低成本提案中，基于标准化早期动作前缀测量密度，从相邻集合中重构完整序列，同时使用最小成本序列的轻量锚点辅助。包含Kernel ASAR变体。

Result: 在Cube候选执行审计中，提案预算从72增至288时，最小潜在成本选择的可行性从0.375降至0.062（位置目标）和从0.344降至0.031（位置+偏航目标）。在Carry and Release评估集的75个查询上，Kernel ASAR在72/144/288提案下分别比匹配选择提升28.0/24.0/18.7个百分点（潜在成本）和18.7/20.0/17.3个百分点（轨迹可达性成本）。

Conclusion: 提案过度生成是采样控制器的固有风险，ASAR通过密度测量和相邻集合重构有效缓解了这一问题。对有限提案池的分析从下尾分布、半径支持统计量和显式局部可行性条件下的序列包含性三个维度刻画了选择风险。

Abstract: Controllers based on sampling and latent world models assign a predicted terminal cost to each candidate action sequence, choose the minimum, execute its first action block, and replan. This rule can fail even when the terminal cost perfectly and accurately reflects the true task objective in the physical world. Residual prediction error can give an infeasible sequence an anomalously low cost, and a larger proposal pool gives such errors more chances to outrank feasible alternatives. We call this conditional failure proposal overgeneration. In Cube candidate execution audits, increasing the total proposal budget from 72 to 288 reduces the feasibility of selection by minimum latent cost from .375 to .062 for position targets and from .344 to .031 for targets defined by position and yaw, although every larger pool contains a feasible sequence. We introduce Adjacent Set Action Reconstruction (ASAR). Among proposals with low cost, ASAR measures density from standardized early action prefixes and reconstructs a full sequence from an adjacent set with a light anchor from the sequence with minimum cost. On a Carry and Release evaluation set of 75 queries, Kernel ASAR improves event completion success over matching selection by 28.0, 24.0, and 18.7 percentage points under latent cost and by 18.7, 20.0, and 17.3 points under a trajectory reachability cost at 72, 144, and 288 proposals. Analysis of finite proposal pools characterizes selection risk from the lower tail, separation by a related radius support statistic, and sequence containment under an explicit local feasibility condition.

</details>


### [109] [Towards Ultrafast Depth Sensing Via Active Event-based Stereo Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23684)
*Jianing Li,Yunjian Zhang,Haiqian Han,Kangyao Huang,Xiangyang Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出主动事件立体视觉（active event-based stereo vision）这一新问题设定，通过融合双目事件相机与红外2D图案投影仪实现高速密集深度感知。构建了真实与合成数据集，并设计了轻量级事件立体匹配网络ActiveEventNet+，其包含轻量块、动态交互代价体以及时序一致性架构三项创新，在高速场景下优于传统帧基相机，原型系统可达150 FPS实时处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统帧基成像在快速运动场景中面临重大挑战，亟需一种新型范式实现超高速深度感知。

Method: 1) 构建双目事件相机+红外2D图案投影仪的主动事件立体原型系统；2) 建立真实数据集（21.5k标签@15Hz）与合成数据集（23.8k标签@20Hz）；3) 提出ActiveEventNet+网络，包含轻量块、动态交互代价体、时序一致性架构三大创新。

Result: ActiveEventNet+在性能上超越现有最先进方法，同时显著降低计算复杂度；轻量版本使原型系统实现高达150 FPS的实时处理速度。

Conclusion: 所提出的主动事件立体视觉范式为未来高速深度传感相机系统设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Conventional frame-based imaging for active stereo systems has encountered major challenges in fast-motion scenarios. However, how to design a novel paradigm for ultrafast depth sensing remains an open issue. In this paper, we propose a novel problem setting, namely active event-based stereo vision, which attempts to integrate binocular event cameras and an infrared 2D pattern projector for high-speed dense depth sensing. Technically, we first build a stereo camera prototype system and present a real-world dataset with over 21.5k spatiotemporal synchronized labels at 15 Hz, while also establishing a realistic synthetic dataset with stereo event streams and 23.8k synchronized labels at 20 Hz. Then, we propose ActiveEventNet+, a lightweight yet effective event-based stereo matching neural network that learns to generate high-quality dense disparity maps from stereo event streams with low latency. Our ActiveEventNet+ mainly involves three innovations: incorporating lightweight blocks into event-based stereo matching frameworks, designing a novel cost volume with dynamic interactions between stereo pairs, and presenting an effective temporal consistency architecture to fully use rich temporal cues in event streams. The results show that our ActiveEventNet+ outperforms state-of-the-art methods while significantly reducing computational complexity. Our solution offers superior depth sensing performance compared to conventional frame-based stereo cameras in high-speed scenes. In particular, the lightweight ActiveEventNet enables the prototype system to achieve real-time processing at speeds up to 150 FPS. We believe that this novel active event-based stereo vision paradigm can provide new insights into the design of future high-speed depth sensing camera systems. Our dataset and code can be available at https://github.com/jianing-li/active_event_based_stereo.

</details>


### [110] [Try Once, Then Optimal: De-Redundified Procedure Memory for Cross-Episode Exploration Amortization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23702)
*Haizhou Ge,Haochen Ouyang,Zhixing Chen,Yufei Jia,Yue Li,Lu Shi,Lei Han,Guyue Zhou,Ruqi Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出实例导向记忆(IOM)框架，通过单次交互记录物体操作流程并基于视觉特征识别，避免重复探索隐藏状态，在多种任务中减少16-30%的操作步骤且不降低成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作具有隐藏内部状态的物体（如带门闩的微波炉）时需要反复探测，但现有跨实例记忆按状态而非物体组织经验，导致同一物体被重复探索，效率低下。

Method: 提出Instance-Oriented Memory (IOM)框架：从单次交互中提取操作流程，以物体可识别特征为键值存储，作为软偏置注入策略网络；利用现成VLM解析交互过程生成流程描述，无需任务特定训练。

Result: 在模拟（微波炉、门）和实物（瓶子、柜子）四类任务中，理想流程记忆比重复探索减少16-30%操作步骤且成功率不降；VLM实现可恢复69-88%的效率提升；即使约12%的门实例存在错误记忆，成功率仍保持。

Conclusion: IOM框架通过物体级别的经验重用显著提升操作效率，错误记忆可通过反馈驱动策略自动纠正，在所有任务中成功率从未下降，实物场景甚至有所提升。

Abstract: Manipulating objects with hidden internal state, such as a latched microwave, forces a robot to probe before it can act. Yet a robot that has solved an instance once re-runs the same probes whenever it encounters that instance again, because existing cross-episode memories target task success and organize reuse around states, not the object or the cost of re-exploring it. We present Instance-Oriented Memory (IOM), an object-centric framework that amortizes this exploration: from a single encounter that uncovers the hidden state, whether or not it succeeds, IOM records a short procedure for manipulating that instance, keys it on the object's identifiable features, and injects it as a soft bias on a procedure-conditioned policy. A later encounter recognizes the object and recalls its procedure instead of re-exploring. We instantiate this distillation with an off-the-shelf vision-language model (VLM) that parses each encounter into the procedure without task-specific training. Across four articulated-object tasks, two in simulation (microwave, door) and two on a real robot (bottle, cabinet), an oracle procedure memory cuts manipulation operations by 16-30% over re-exploration at non-regressing success, and the VLM instantiation recovers 69-88% of that saving out of the box. Because the procedure is a soft bias on a feedback-driven policy, an incorrect memory is recovered from rather than obeyed: success holds even when a retrieved procedure is wrong, as for $\approx$12% of door instances. Across all tasks the benefit is purely one of efficiency: success never regresses, and on the real robot even improves. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [111] [LabRobFail: A Benchmark for Robotic Failure Analysis in Chemical Self-driving Laboratories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23704)
*Haobo Wang,Baoli Sun,Anqi Zou,Dongsheng Huang,Zelin Lv,Ning Wang,Rui Li,Dongzhan Zhou,Weiyu Guo,Zhihui Wang,Wanli Ouyang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了LabRobFail框架，包含仿真环境(注入三类可控故障构建超2万条轨迹)、评测基准(六项能力)和专用视觉语言模型(LabRobFail-VLM)，用于化学实验室机器人故障分析与闭环恢复，在故障检测(92.58%)和时间定位(85.58%)上显著优于通用VLM，并提升下游任务成功率10-20个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自驱动实验室中具身智能体的部署受限于化学实验的不可逆性和安全关键性，且故障数据稀缺、缺乏细粒度评测协议，亟需一个面向故障的框架来学习和评估机器人故障分析能力。

Method: 构建LabRobFail-Sim仿真环境，在控制层、物理层和语义层注入可控故障，生成包含20,000+条轨迹、70+任务场景、5类故障类别和11种细粒度故障类型的数据集LabRobFail-Data；设计LabRobFail-Bench评测六项能力；开发领域专用视觉语言模型LabRobFail-VLM，生成结构化故障诊断与恢复指令。

Result: LabRobFail-VLM在已知环境上故障检测准确率92.58%，时间定位准确率85.58%，显著优于通用VLM；作为实时监督器时，下游VLA任务成功率提升10-20个百分点。

Conclusion: 细粒度故障理解对闭环恢复和可靠的实验室自主性具有重要价值，所提框架为化学实验室机器人故障分析与自主恢复提供了有效方案。

Abstract: The deployment of embodied agents in self-driving laboratories could accelerate scientific discovery, yet their reliability is constrained by the irreversible and safety-critical nature of chemical experiments. Progress is further hindered by scarce failure data and the lack of fine-grained evaluation protocols. To address these challenges, we introduce LabRobFail, a failure-centric framework for learning and evaluating robotic failure analysis in chemical laboratories. LabRobFail-Sim injects controllable failures at the control, physics, and semantic levels, enabling the construction of LabRobFail-Data, which contains over 20,000 trajectories across 70+ task scenarios, five failure categories, and 11 fine-grained failure types. LabRobFail-Bench evaluates six capabilities spanning task understanding, failure detection, temporal localization, severity assessment, failure classification, and actionable correction. We further develop LabRobFail-VLM, a domain-specialized vision-language model that generates structured failure diagnoses and recovery instructions. On seen environments, it achieves 92.58% failure-detection accuracy and 85.58% temporal-localization accuracy, substantially outperforming general-purpose VLMs. When integrated as a real-time supervisor, it improves downstream VLA task success rates by 10-20 percentage points, demonstrating the value of fine-grained failure understanding for closed-loop recovery and reliable laboratory autonomy. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/Su-ISE-2001/SciRobo

</details>


### [112] [Hierarchical Soft Actor-Critic for Sparse-Reward Long-Horizon Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23726)
*Zahra Abdalla Elashaal,Afef Hfaiedh,Nahla Khraief,Issmail Ellabib,Giansalvo Cirrincione*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种双层分层强化学习框架（HRL-SAC），上层负责高层策略规划，下层使用连续控制SAC算法，通过熵正则化策略优化解决稀疏奖励长时域任务，在SAR-2数据集上表现优于普通SAC基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏奖励和长时域任务中探索困难，现有强化学习方法难以有效应对延迟奖励和连续控制带来的挑战。

Method: 构建双层分层强化学习框架：高层进行战略规划，低层使用Soft Actor-Critic (SAC)连续控制算法，并采用熵正则化策略优化来促进探索。

Result: 在Search-and-Rescue-2 (SAR-2)数据集上，HRL-SAC在成功率、覆盖效率和收敛速度方面均优于平面SAC基线。

Conclusion: 分层熵正则化策略是解决长时域稀疏奖励强化学习任务的有效方案。

Abstract: Exploration in sparse-reward long-horizon tasks poses significant challenges for reinforcement learning. To address these challenges, we propose a two-level Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning (HRL) framework. The first level handles high-level strategic planning, while the low-level uses the continuous-control Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) algorithm, and they utilize entropy-regularized policy optimization. The proposed framework was trained and evaluated using the Search-and-Rescue-2 (SAR-2) dataset. HRL-SAC effectively addresses sparse-reward long-horizon search problems characterized by delayed rewards and continuous control, and its outperforming the flat SAC baseline reinforcement learning in terms of success rates, coverage efficiency, and convergence. These findings indicate that hierarchical entropy-regularized policies are a promising solution to tackle long-horizon sparse-reward reinforcement learning tasks.

</details>


### [113] [Learning Traversability-Aware Global Planners for Long Horizon Off-Road Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23743)
*Kasi Viswanath,Jason M. Gregory,Shaunak Kolhe,Srikanth Saripalli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用卫星图像、航空LiDAR和矢量地图等地理空间数据，结合人类驾驶GPS轨迹和自监督几何先验，学习连续可通行性地图的方法，用于大规模越野自主导航。在299个场景（约1244平方公里）和1130公里人类驾驶数据上训练，实车测试表明该方法轨迹长度仅比人类驾驶长3.66%，且干预次数减少约85%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越野环境中自主导航面临传感器感知范围有限的瓶颈，无法获取远距离地形特征来规划安全高效的路径。现有方法难以从地理空间数据中学习可通行性，因为缺乏大规模密集标注数据，且多依赖短距离感知。

Method: 提出一种高效的学习框架，从卫星影像、航空LiDAR等地理空间数据中学习连续的可通行性地图。训练数据采用人类驾驶的GPS轨迹作为监督信号，并结合LiDAR自监督几何先验进行约束。同时发布了包含299个场景、约1244平方公里多样地形和1130公里人类驾驶数据的公开数据集。

Result: 在Clearpath Warthog平台上，跨越两个地点七条路线的实地测试中，该方法生成的轨迹长度仅为人类驾驶路径长度的3.66%，并将操作员干预次数比仅使用局部规划器的自主系统减少了约85%。

Conclusion: 利用地理空间数据结合人类驾驶轨迹和自监督学习，能够有效学习远距离的越野可通行性，显著提升自主导航的效率和安全性，减少人工干预。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation across large off-road environments remains a challenging problem. Onboard sensors perceive only the immediate surroundings, yet safe and efficient routes depend on terrain features that extend well beyond the sensor horizon. Geo-spatial data sources such as satellite imagery, aerial LiDAR, and vector maps can close this gap, but learning traversability from them is difficult: dense labels are unavailable at scale, and existing methods rely on short-range sensing. We propose an efficient formulation that learns a continuous traversability map from overhead data, supervised directly by human-driven GPS trajectories and shaped by self-supervised geometric priors from LiDAR. Alongside the model, we release a public dataset of 299 scenes spanning $\sim\!1{,}244\,\mathrm{km}^{2}$ of diverse terrain, paired with $1{,}130\,\mathrm{km}$ of human driving. In field trials on a Clearpath Warthog across seven routes at two sites, our method achieves trajectories within $3.66\%$ of human path length and reduces operator interventions by $\sim\!85\%$ compared to local-planner-only autonomy.

</details>


### [114] [$N_0$-VTLA: Scaling Vision-Tactile-Language-Action Model with Latent Tactile Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23782)
*NeoteAI Team,Fudan TEAI Team*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出N₀-VTLA，一种视觉-触觉-语言-动作基础模型，通过大规模触觉预训练、触觉通路逐步集成和优势条件离线策略改进，在接触丰富的操作任务上大幅超越基线方法，在9项真实机器人任务和20项仿真任务中分别达到全胜和63.8%的平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有操作策略多依赖视觉信息，缺乏对触觉感知的有效利用，难以处理需要精细接触力控制的任务。作者旨在构建一个能够融合触觉感知的大规模基础模型，实现精细接触操作和离线策略改进。

Method: (1) 视觉-触觉预训练：利用大规模触觉数据集NeoData学习广泛的接触先验；(2) 阶段性触觉通路集成：通过知识蒸馏将预训练的接触模式迁移到精细运动调整中；(3) ALTER优势条件离线强化学习方法：将相对进度和轨迹事件比较转化为二值优势标签，用于固定部署语料上的策略训练。

Result: 在所有9项真实机器人NeoReal任务中全胜；在20项仿真任务套件中达到63.8%平均成功率（最强基线为44.0%）；结合ALTER后在3项长时域真实机器人任务上达到75-95%的成功率。

Conclusion: N₀-VTLA验证了大规模触觉数据预训练和触觉通路集成对接触丰富操作任务的有效性，为通用触觉驱动操作策略奠定了基础。

Abstract: We present $N_0$-VTLA, a vision-tactile-language-action (VTLA) foundation model capable of (1) fine-grained contact-rich manipulation with tactile perception and tactile-feedback control, and (2) offline policy improvement from stored deployment data. Building on current vision-based backbones, we propose a training recipe for tactile integration consisting of visuo-tactile pre-training, staged tactile-pathway integration, and advantage-conditioned offline policy improvement. During pre-training, the policy learns broad contact priors from NeoData, our large-scale visuo-tactile robot dataset; to our knowledge, $N_0$-VTLA is the first VTLA model pretrained on tactile data at scale. During post-training, we augment the policy with a predictive tactile pathway that distills the contact patterns learned at scale into the fine motion adjustments required by downstream tactile-centric manipulation. For offline policy improvement, we introduce ALTER, an advantage-conditioned offline reinforcement learning method that converts relative progress and trajectory-event comparisons into binary advantage labels for policy training on a fixed deployment corpus, further improving task-specific learning on contact-rich skills such as deformable object manipulation. Across contact-rich benchmarks, $N_0$-VTLA outperforms strong baselines by wide margins: it wins all nine real-robot NeoReal tasks and reaches 63.8% mean success on a twenty-task simulation suite, against 44.0% for the strongest baseline. $N_0$-VTLA policies trained with ALTER reach 75-95% success on three long-horizon real-robot tasks. These results lay a foundation for versatile tactile-driven manipulation policies.

</details>


### [115] [$N_0$-TWAM: Scaling Tactile-Native World-Action Model for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23783)
*NeoteAI Team,Fudan TEAI Team*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了$N_0$-TWAM，一种触觉原生的世界-动作模型，用于接触密集操作。通过在六个体现和450个任务的大规模触觉丰富演示上进行视觉-触觉联合训练，结合统一力触觉表示NeoForce和触觉接触事件任务分段，以及非对称混合Transformer架构，该模型能够同时预测未来视觉、触觉和动作，在接触密集任务上展现出强大的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型主要依赖视觉信息，缺乏触觉模态的融入，限制了在接触密集操作任务中的精细控制能力。本文旨在构建一个大规模、触觉原生的世界-动作模型，赋予机器人预测未来视觉、触觉和动作的能力，以支撑细粒度接触密集操作。

Method: 1) 使用NeoForce统一力触觉表示，提供物理可解释的接触信号来条件化动作生成；2) 在大规模多体现数据上（6个体现、450个任务）进行视觉-触觉联合预训练；3) 引入触觉接触事件进行任务分段，在执行中推进阶段；4) 采用非对称混合Transformer架构，用全宽度专家进行视频预测，用精简专家进行动作和触觉预测。

Result: 在真实和仿真基准上，$N_0$-TWAM在多种接触密集任务上展现了强大能力，数据规模扩展对精确触觉和动作预测有明显益处。

Conclusion: $N_0$-TWAM构建了第一个大规模训练的触觉世界-动作模型，赋予系统预测视觉、触觉和动作的能力，为开放接触密集任务上的精细操作奠定了坚实基础。代码和模型将开源以促进触觉机器人操作研究。

Abstract: We present $N_0$-TWAM, a tactile-native world-action model for contact-rich manipulation that predicts both future vision and future contact. To our knowledge, it is the first tactile world-action model trained at large scale, and it shows strong capability on contact-rich tasks. We pre-train $N_0$-TWAM at large scale with visuo-tactile joint training over tactile-rich demonstrations spanning six embodiments and 450 tasks. We use NeoForce, a unified force-based tactile representation, to form a physically grounded contact signal that conditions action generation. To improve long-horizon and multi-stage manipulation, we introduce tactile contact events for task staging and advance through them during execution. For real-time efficiency, we adopt an asymmetric Mixture-of-Transformers architecture that pairs a full-width expert for video prediction with slim experts for downstream action and tactile prediction. Evaluations on both real and simulated benchmarks justify the capabilities of $N_0$-TWAM across a range of contact-rich tasks, and demonstrate the benefit of data scaling for precise tactile and action prediction. In summary, $N_0$-TWAM endows a world-action model with predictive capabilities to foresee vision, touch and action, building a solid foundation for fine-grained manipulation on open contact-rich tasks. The codebase and model checkpoints will be made publicly available to foster further research and development in tactile-enabled robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [116] [A Few Words Go a Long Way: Language Guided Robot Policy Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23784)
*Daphne Chen,Archit Ritesh Jain,Eric Goossen,Emma Romig,Michael Murray,Nick Walker,Maya Cakmak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ARCHITECT框架，将机器人策略获取转化为交互式程序合成任务，利用LLM编码智能体合成模块化机器人程序，通过人类自然语言纠正和技能库积累，在Franka Panda机器人上优于现有VLA模型和程序合成基线，实现可解释、可操控、数据高效的机器人学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在机器人操控中表现出零样本能力，但本质上是黑箱策略，难以解释、适应或在失败时修正，亟需一种可解释、可局部化反馈的替代方案。

Method: 将机器人策略获取视为交互式程序合成任务，利用LLM编码智能体合成模块化程序，通过执行轨迹将人类自然语言纠正确植于策略代码中，并蒸馏为持久技能库实现长期上下文学习。

Result: 在Franka Panda机器人上，对复杂长时域任务（包括铰接物体操作和布料折叠）的基准评估中，ARCHITECT优于最先进的VLA模型和程序合成基线，且技能库使系统能以递减的人类干预迁移到新任务。

Conclusion: 模块化架构允许用户隔离失败并定位反馈，提供了可操控、数据高效的黑箱机器人学习替代方案。

Abstract: While vision-language-action models have demonstrated impressive zero-shot manipulation capabilities, they remain fundamentally black box policies that are difficult to interpret, adapt, or correct when they inevitably fail. In this work, we propose ARCHITECT, a framework that treats robot policy acquisition as an interactive program synthesis task. ARCHITECT leverages the reasoning capabilities of LLM coding agents to synthesize modular robot programs that utilize a suite of perception and control tools. Unlike end-to-end models where distribution shift leads to unpredictable, cascading failures, our modular architecture allows users to isolate failures and localize feedback at the level of abstraction required. We introduce an iterative process where a human supervisor provides natural language corrections to steer the policy. These corrections are grounded in the policy code by program execution traces and distilled into a persistent skill library, a form of long-term in-context learning which enables the agent to accumulate a repertoire of reusable, interpretable behaviors. In a benchmark evaluation on a Franka Panda robot, ARCHITECT outperforms state-of-the-art VLA models and program synthesis baselines on complex, long-horizon tasks, including articulated object manipulation and cloth folding. Our results demonstrate that the synthesized skill library enables the system to transfer to novel tasks with decreasing human intervention, providing a steerable and data-efficient alternative to black-box robot learning. Website: https://robo-architect.github.io/

</details>


### [117] [Memory for Attention: Language-Conditioned Re-Perception with a Vision--Language--Motion Map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23797)
*Dibyendu Ghosh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究携带持久性行为标注地图的机器人所面临的两类规划问题。在空间导航中，基于VLMM的行为感知规划器在离线优化中降低35%成本，但闭环执行时收益降至4%，与按需VLM性能相当。而在资源分配问题（有限感知预算下的重观察调度）中，地图记忆（变化历史或最近观测时间）能匹配oracle性能，远超无记忆的VLM先验。记忆的优势集中于重要物体（约1.6倍均值），下游取物任务验证了更少无效行程；增益随实例异质性增长，符合Cauchy-Schwarz界预测。在语言条件化任务中，VLMM通过运动通道跟踪开放词汇物体的变化，优于相关性加权基线（+2.5%）和按需VLM（+8.9%）。结论：地图的核心价值不在于指导机器人如何行走，而在于告诉机器人该关注什么。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究携带持久性行为标注地图的机器人在空间导航和资源分配两类规划问题中的表现差异，探究地图记忆的实际价值所在。

Method: 基于VLMM（视觉-语言-运动地图）构建行为感知规划器，在AI2-THOR场景中分别测试空间导航和资源分配任务。空间导航采用闭环执行对比离线优化；资源分配框架将重感知视为注意力决策，对比记忆型调度（变化历史、最近观测时间）与无记忆VLM先验；语言条件化任务测试VLMM的开放词汇物体跟踪能力。

Result: 空间导航中离线优化成本降低约35%，闭环执行仅约4%；资源分配中记忆型调度匹配oracle，VLM先验表现差；语言条件化中VLMM超越相关性加权基线（+2.5%）和按需VLM（+8.9%）。

Conclusion: 持久性行为标注地图的核心价值不在于优化空间导航，而在于指导机器人如何根据记忆和语言条件分配有限的感知注意力资源。

Abstract: A robot carrying a persistent, behavior-annotated map faces two planning questions, and its memory answers only one well. The \emph{spatial-navigation} question -- how to walk around a room -- we address first and report a negative: building on Vision--Language--Motion Maps (VLMM), a behavior-aware planner cost cuts a planning-time objective by $\sim$35\% over 28 AI2-THOR scenes, but under closed-loop execution the real benefit nearly vanishes ($\sim$4\%) and an on-demand vision--language model (VLM) does as well. The \emph{resource-allocation} question differs: under a limited perception budget, what should the robot re-observe now to keep its map fresh? Framing re-perception as this attention decision, we show a persistent map's memory (change-history, or even just recency of last sighting) yields the best schedule (held-out), matching an oracle, while the memoryless VLM prior is poor. Because the schedule reallocates budget toward what matters, memory's benefit concentrates on the important objects ($\sim$1.6$\times$ the mean), and a downstream fetch task confirms fewer wasted trips; the gain grows with per-instance heterogeneity exactly as a Cauchy--Schwarz bound predicts -- it equals $\mathrm{Var}(\sqrtλ)$, the variance of root-volatility. With a real CLIP prior on rendered objects the advantage is $+21$--$26\%$. The map's distinctive value appears when the task is \emph{language-conditioned}: told what to track, VLMM grounds the relevant objects (open-vocabulary) and tracks their change (memory), beating even a strong relevance-weighted recency baseline ($+2.5\%$) -- so its motion channel adds value beyond a last-seen timestamp -- and an on-demand VLM ($+8.9\%$); neither language nor dynamics alone suffices. The map earns its keep not by telling the robot how to walk around a room, but by telling it what to pay attention to.

</details>


### [118] [BC-NMPC: Battery-Constrained NMPC with Propulsion Prediction and Replanning for High-Speed Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23867)
*Parakh M. Gupta,Matej Mihulka,Matej Novosad,Robert Penicka,Martin Saska*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将电池与推进系统模型集成到非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）框架中的方法，实时预测无人机电压、电流、功率和最大可用推力，从而在高速敏捷飞行中补偿因电池放电导致的推力衰减，结合轨迹重规划算法，实现碰撞规避并显著提升轨迹跟踪性能（RMSE降低6倍，飞行距离增加46%，飞行时间增加100%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在高速敏捷飞行中，电池放电导致最大可用推力下降，引起轨迹跟踪误差，甚至碰撞障碍物导致任务失败。现有方法未能在控制层实时考虑电池动态对推力上限的影响。

Method: 1) 将电池和推进系统模型集成到NMPC框架中，实时预测电压、电流、功率及最大可用推力；2) 基于动态推力约束，在飞行中通过轨迹规划算法重规划轨迹，以适应推力衰减。

Result: 实物飞行实验验证了模型精度；仿真实验表明：相比未补偿方法，跟踪RMSE降低6倍，飞行距离增加46%，飞行时间增加100%，且实现无碰撞飞行。

Conclusion: 将电池动态模型融入NMPC并配合在线轨迹重规划，能有效补偿推力衰减，显著提升无人机在障碍密集环境下的高速飞行安全性和轨迹跟踪精度。

Abstract: Trajectory tracking performance of Uncrewed Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) degrades during high-speed and agile flight due to the depletion of the battery and subsequent loss of maximum available thrust. In applications such as drone racing, the consequent trajectory tracking error leads to a collision with obstacles and a subsequent failure to complete the race. In this paper, we present a novel method for integrating battery and propulsion system models into a Nonlinear Model Predictive Controller (NMPC) framework to enable real-time prediction of the voltage, consumed current, power, and maximum available thrust of the platform. This enables our approach to account for the dynamic variations in the maximum available thrust of the UAV caused by battery discharge, allowing it to plan for the depleting thrust and improve trajectory tracking performance. A trajectory planning algorithm is implemented to replan the trajectory in-flight based on evolving thrust limits. The accuracy of the proposed model is verified in real-world flight experiments, while the effectiveness of the replanning algorithm is evaluated in simulation. Compared to an uncompensated flight, our novel approach demonstrates achieves a collision-free flight to achieve a 6-fold decrease in tracking Root Mean Square Error (RMSE), a 46 % increase in flight distance, and a 100 % increase in flight time in an obstacle-ridden environment.

</details>


### [119] [Embodied GPT-5.1: Evidence of a World Model?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23899)
*Roberto Spinelli,Thiago C. Martins*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探究了无任何具身经验、无仿真训练的多模态大模型GPT-5.1能否作为实体移动机器人的高层控制器。仅凭低分辨率第一人称图像和离散动作集，模型在导航和目标物体定位接触任务中展现了空间记忆、物理推理和连贯动作序列等涌现能力，但也存在效率低下和感知局限。结果表明，尽管缺乏具身训练，GPT-5.1仍表现出类似世界模型的行为，挑战了"物理身体是智能前提"的传统观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验大规模多模态语言模型（GPT-5.1）在完全没有具身经验、仿真训练或传感器运动经验的前提下，能否作为实体机器人的高层控制器，从而挑战认知科学和机器人学中"物理身体是发展空间推理和物理理解能力的必要条件"这一长期观点。

Method: 采用探索性研究方法，将GPT-5.1连接至实体移动机器人，仅提供低分辨率第一人称视角图像和离散动作集（如前进、转向、停止等），令模型完成导航和物体导向行为任务（定位并接触目标玩具），在多次试验中观察其表现。

Result: GPT-5.1展现出持续跟踪已离开视野的物体位置（短期记忆）、推断自身移动的物理后果、执行连贯动作序列（如碰撞物体后倒车以目视验证结果）等能力。但同时也表现出对准策略不精确、偶尔误识别远处干扰物等效率低下和感知局限。

Conclusion: GPT-5.1在无任何具身训练的条件下展现出类似世界模型的行为，这一发现挑战了"物理身体是空间推理和物理理解必要前提"的传统观点，并为深入探究大语言模型中物理理解的涌现机制、边界和鲁棒性提供了动因。

Abstract: This exploratory study examines whether a large multimodal language model, GPT-5.1, can serve as the high-level controller of a physical mobile robot despite having no prior embodiment, no training in simulated environments, and no exposure to sensorimotor experience. Using only low-resolution first-person images and a discrete action set, the model was tasked with navigation and object-directed behaviors such as locating and contacting a target toy. Across multiple trials, GPT-5.1 demonstrated emergent capabilities that suggest elements of spatial reasoning and physical understanding. These included maintaining short-term memory of object locations after they left the camera frame, inferring the physical consequences of its own movements, and executing coherent action sequences such as colliding with an object and reversing to visually verify the outcome. At the same time, the model displayed inefficiencies and perceptual limitations, including imprecise alignment strategies and occasional misidentification of distant distractors. Overall, the results indicate that GPT-5.1 exhibits signs of world-model-like behavior in an embodied setting, despite the absence of any embodiment-related training, a finding that challenges long-standing views in cognitive science and robotics which hold that a physical body is a necessary prerequisite for developing such forms of intelligence. The findings motivate deeper investigation into the emergence, limits, and robustness of physical understanding in large language models.

</details>


### [120] [LeapBot-WA: World-Anchor Action Models via Predictive Latent Alignments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23969)
*Pei Liu,Nan Zheng,Lang Zhang,Daojie Peng,Yanan Zhang,Feilong Kong,Mingyue Feng,Jiachao Liu,Yaonong Wang,Qifeng Chen,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LeapBot-WA提出了一种基于预测-潜变量范式（Predictive-Latent paradigm）的世界动作模型（WAM），通过联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）替代传统的像素级视频生成，配合各向同性语义自编码器（ISAE）和非对称混合Transformer（MoT）架构，在LIBERO和RoboTwin 2.0上达到SOTA性能，且无需大规模预训练，展现出优异的零样本鲁棒性和真实世界迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WAMs过度依赖像素级视频生成，导致模型不得不重建与任务无关的视觉细节，既浪费了表征容量，又使策略容易受到视觉干扰物的影响，亟需一种更高效、鲁棒的潜变量中心范式。

Method: 1) 将JEPA作为World-Anchor，将世界模型的核心从视觉合成转向预测语义对齐（Predictive Semantic Alignment），在潜变量基础空间中提取抽象物理动态。2) 提出各向同性语义自编码器（ISAE），重塑anchor的潜空间为扩散友好的流形，防止离流形漂移。3) 设计非对称混合Transformer（MoT）架构：训练时Anchor Diffusion Transformer作为特权动力学专家指导Action Diffusion Transformer，推理时剪除该分支实现零开销执行。

Result: 在LIBERO上达到预测模型中的SOTA，在RoboTwin 2.0上匹配顶级生成式WAMs，无需大规模轨迹预训练。展现出对未见环境的优越零样本鲁棒性，并成功实现真实世界迁移。

Conclusion: LeapBot-WA建立了一种高效、鲁棒的潜变量中心范式，为可扩展的机器人控制提供了新的方向，证明了摆脱像素级生成、转向潜空间语义对齐在具身智能中的有效性。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for embodied intelligence, yet the prevailing reliance on pixel-level video generation creates a fundamental bottleneck. Forcing models to reconstruct task-irrelevant visual details dissipates representational capacity and renders policies vulnerable to visual distractors. In this paper, we propose LeapBot-WA, which establishes a novel Predictive-Latent paradigm for WAMs by operationalizing the Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) as a World-Anchor. Departing from the traditional reliance on visual synthesis, LeapBot-WA shifts the core of world modeling to Predictive Semantic Alignment, extracting abstract physical dynamics directly within a latent foundation space. To bridge the modality gap between non-Gaussian predictive features and diffusion priors, we introduce the Isotropic Semantic Autoencoder (ISAE), which reshapes the anchor's latent space into a diffusion-friendly manifold to prevent off-manifold drift. Furthermore, we design an Asymmetric Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) architecture. During training, an Anchor Diffusion Transformer acts as a privileged dynamics expert to guide the Action Diffusion Transformer; at inference, this heavy dynamics branch is pruned, enabling zero-overhead execution. LeapBot-WA achieves state-of-the-art performance among predictive models on LIBERO and matches top-tier generative WAMs on RoboTwin 2.0 without requiring large-scale trajectory pre-training. It further demonstrates superior zero-shot robustness to unseen environments and successful real-world transfer, establishing a highly efficient and robust latent-centric paradigm for scalable robotic control. Code: https://github.com/LeapWM/leapbot-wa.

</details>


### [121] [Co-planning of Flight Corridors and Communication Infrastructure for Urban Drone Logistics Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23989)
*Yingjie He,Yikang Wang,Zhenyu Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CR-CMAB框架，通过信道互导引导的组合多臂老虎机算法联合优化基站部署与UAV飞行走廊，在满足通信质量约束下最小化基础设施投资与飞行距离。实验表明该方法能更优地定位基站并缩短飞行走廊。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常独立优化通信基础设施与UAV飞行路径，导致不必要的基站部署或过度的飞行绕路，缺乏联合优化的视角。

Method: 提出CR-CMAB（信道互导引导的组合多臂老虎机）框架，包含三部分：利用3D射线追踪构建高保真无线电地图、通过覆盖感知的CMAB搜索选择基站组合、利用信道互导性动态扩展搜索空间以识别有前景的基站位置。

Result: 在详细案例研究中，CR-CMAB在适中的计算时间内优于基线方法，实现了更优策略定位的基站和更短的飞行走廊。

Conclusion: 该研究为未来智慧城市中成本效益高且通信可靠的UAM部署提供了实用的规划视角。

Abstract: Reliable wireless connectivity is essential for urban air mobility (UAM) networks in dense urban environments. It is therefore imperative to carefully plan the supporting communication infrastructure for UAM flight corridors. Most existing works optimize communication infrastructure and UAV flight paths independently, often leading to unnecessary base station (BS) deployment or excessive flight detours. This paper studies the joint optimization of BS deployment and UAV flight corridors in complex urban environments, aiming to minimize both infrastructure investment and flight distance while satisfying communication quality constraints. We propose CR-CMAB, a channel reciprocity-guided combinatorial multi-armed bandit framework. The framework constructs high-fidelity radio maps using 3D ray tracing, selects BS combinations via coverage-aware CMAB search, and dynamically expands the search space by identifying promising BS locations through channel reciprocity. Experimental results from a detailed case study demonstrate that CR-CMAB outperforms baseline methods with moderate computational time, yielding more strategically positioned BSs and shorter flight corridors. This study offers a practical planning perspective for cost-effective and communication-reliable UAM deployment in future smart cities.

</details>


### [122] [FutureRTC: Real-Time Robot Execution with Anticipatory-Conditioned Action Chunking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24008)
*Hai Jiang,Yixian Zou,Binbin Liang,Boqian Liu,Fanman Meng,Shuaicheng Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FutureRTC，一种即插即用的适配框架，通过预测执行时刻的观测和状态来解决VLA策略异步执行中的预测-执行错位和块间不连续问题，无需修改底层策略，在仿真和真实环境中实现了更平滑的轨迹、更快的执行速度和更高的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略的实时部署需要异步执行，即当前动作块执行的同时计算后续动作块，这导致预测与执行错位，表现为块间不连续。现有方法仅表面平滑块边界、需要昂贵的策略优化，或只能前向预测本体感受状态而忽略关键的视觉观测。

Method: FutureRTC包含三个关键组件：1) 状态校正模块，补偿前向传播与执行时刻实际本体感受状态之间的差异；2) 观测预测模块，利用机器人运动作为显式物理先验，通过运动感知特征传输与重建预测执行时刻的视觉表示；3) 策略一致性损失，对齐从预测上下文生成的动作块与在VLA策略期望的执行时刻输入下产生的动作块。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中的大量实验表明，FutureRTC对推理延迟具有优越的鲁棒性，实现了更平滑的轨迹、更快的执行速度和持续更高的任务成功率。

Conclusion: FutureRTC作为一种即插即用的适配框架，无需修改底层VLA策略即可有效解决异步执行中的预测-执行错位问题，为VLA策略的实时部署提供了一种实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Real-time deployment of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies necessitates asynchronous execution, wherein subsequent action chunks are computed concurrently with the execution of the current chunk, leading to prediction-execution misalignment and manifesting as inter-chunk discontinuities. Existing methods either superficially smooth chunk boundaries, require costly policy optimization, or exclusively forward-predict proprioceptive states yet neglect critical visual observations. In this paper, we propose \textbf{FutureRTC}, a plug-and-play adaptation framework that predicts execution-time observations and states for asynchronous VLA control without modifying the underlying policy. Specifically, FutureRTC features a state correction module to compensate for the discrepancy between rolled-forward and actual execution-time proprioceptive states and an observation prediction module that forecasts execution-time visual representations by leveraging robot motion as an explicit physical prior through motion-aware feature transport and reconstruction. Furthermore, we introduce a policy consistency loss to align the action chunks generated from predicted contexts with those produced under the expected execution-time inputs of the VLA policy. Extensive experiments across simulated and real-world environments demonstrate that FutureRTC achieves superior robustness to inference delays, resulting in smoother trajectories, faster execution, and consistently higher task success rates.

</details>


### [123] [Moving-Horizon Estimation and Nonlinear Model Predictive Control of Cable-Driven Soft Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24029)
*Lingxiao Xun,Haihong Li,Gang Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于降阶Cosserat动力学模型的移动时域估计（MHE）和非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）框架，用于绳驱动软体机械臂的精确控制。通过平滑的绳长驱动建模公式近似绳张力与松弛的互补关系，实现无需直接张力传感的绳长控制。仿真和四绳原型实验验证了该方法在末端执行器位姿跟踪中的实时性与有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机械臂的精确控制因难以建立准确且计算可行的模型而具有挑战性，现有方法缺乏物理一致性与实时控制兼容性的结合。

Method: 基于降阶Cosserat杆模型，提出平滑绳长驱动公式以近似绳张力-松弛互补关系；结合MHE（从末端位姿和绳长信息估计状态）与NMPC（在绳长和绳速约束下实现任务空间控制）。

Result: 仿真验证了估计器和控制器在多绳软体机械臂上对位姿和应变调节的有效性；四绳原型实验表明该方案可实时实现，并通过绳长控制实现精确的末端位置跟踪。

Conclusion: 所提出的MHE-NMPC框架基于降阶Cosserat模型，能在无需直接张力传感的条件下实现绳驱动软体机械臂的实时精确控制，兼具物理可解释性与计算可行性。

Abstract: Precise control of soft manipulators remains challenging due to the difficulty of developing accurate yet computationally tractable models for model-based estimation and control. Reduced Cosserat-rod models provide a physics-based and control-oriented description of soft-robot dynamics, offering an explicit alternative to purely data-driven input-output representations. In this paper, we propose a moving-horizon estimation (MHE) and nonlinear model predictive control (NMPC) framework for cable-driven soft manipulators based on reduced Cosserat dynamics. A smooth cable-length-driven modeling formulation is developed by approximating the complementarity relationship between cable tension and cable slackness, enabling cable-length control without direct tension sensing. Based on this formulation, an MHE method is introduced to estimate the reduced state and reconstruct the manipulator configuration from end-effector pose measurements and cable-length information. An NMPC controller is then formulated to achieve task-space control under cable-length and cable-rate constraints. The proposed framework is validated through numerical simulations and experiments. Simulation results demonstrate the effectiveness of the estimator and controller for pose and strain-related regulation on a multi-cable soft manipulator. Experimental results on a four-cable prototype further show that the proposed MHE-NMPC scheme can be implemented in real time and enables accurate end-effector position tracking through cable-length control.

</details>


### [124] [WARL: Wrench-Augmented Reinforcement Learning for Task-Agnostic Learning in Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24036)
*Keita Yoneda,Kento Kawaharazuka,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出Wrench-Augmented Reinforcement Learning (WARL)方法，通过在动作空间引入力/力矩（wrench）来扩展四足机器人的探索能力，并结合基于成功率的课程机制逐步消除wrench，最终实现仅基于关节控制的行为获取。实验表明该方法在多样地形和电机任务中具有鲁棒性，但也发现引入wrench可能鼓励与物理结构不一致的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于关节空间的强化学习限制了四足机器人的探索能力，导致学习效率低下，需要针对不同地形设计复杂的奖励函数或课程机制。

Method: 提出WARL方法：1) 在动作空间中引入wrench（力和力矩）以增强探索；2) 设计基于成功率的Switching Curriculum机制，逐步消除wrench，最终过渡到纯关节控制。

Result: 在四足机器人上，WARL在多种地形和电机任务中均能鲁棒学习，无需地形特定的奖励调整或复杂课程设计。消融实验验证了Switching Curriculum的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于wrench的探索增强能有效提升学习效率，但需注意wrench的设计需与机器人物理结构一致，这是未来关键挑战。

Abstract: While reinforcement learning for legged robots has achieved high motor performance, it has been constrained by the limited exploration capability of actions confined to the joint space. To address this issue, this study proposes a new method, Wrench-Augmented Reinforcement Learning (WARL), which introduces a wrenche (force and torque) into the action space. The proposed method combines wrench-guided exploration with a success rate-based curriculum mechanism to expand exploration capabilities in the early stages of learning, with the ultimate goal of acquiring behaviors based solely on joint control. Experiments using a quadruped robot demonstrated that WARL can learn robustly across diverse terrains and motor tasks without requiring terrain-specific reward adjustments or complex curriculum designs. Furthermore, an ablation study verified the effectiveness of the Switching Curriculum, which gradually eliminates the wrench. On the other hand, we also show that introducing a wrench can encourage behaviors that do not sufficiently exploit the robot's physical embodiment. These findings suggest that while wrench-based exploration enhancement is effective for improving learning efficiency, designing it in a way that is consistent with the robot's physical structure is a critical future challenge.

</details>


### [125] [Effective Parameters, Real Behavior: Renormalization for Robotics -- From Infinite Electron Mass to Sim-to-Real Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24079)
*Youran Sun,Jiaxuan Guo,Xingyu Ren,Chugang Yi,Haizhao Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出利用重正化（renormalization）方法，通过使用与分辨率相关的有效参数来吸收模拟器忽略的细节，从而弥合机器人的仿真到现实（sim-to-real）差距，而非依赖构建更精确的模拟器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥合机器人的sim-to-real差距是核心问题，传统方法依赖于构建越来越精确的模拟器，但该方法在复杂场景下成本高且难以实现。

Method: 提出基于重正化的方法：使用依赖于分辨率的有效参数来吸收模拟器遗漏的细节，从而复现真实行为。通过PD控制在有限仿真频率下的解析分析、动态绳索操作和水下游泳的实例进行验证。

Result: 解析证明了在有限仿真频率的PD控制中，比例反馈会改变有效微分增益，微分反馈会改变有效惯性；并将该视角成功应用于动态绳索操作和水下游泳场景。

Conclusion: 重正化为机器人学提供了一条补充路径：通过有效参数而非更精确的模拟器，实现从仿真到现实的跨越。

Abstract: Bridging the sim-to-real gap is a central problem in robotics, and the prevailing approach is to build increasingly accurate simulators. Here, we propose another approach based on renormalization: using effective, resolution-dependent parameters to absorb details omitted by the simulator and reproduce real behavior. These parameters may differ from measured physical values because they compensate for what the simulator leaves out. We demonstrate this mechanism analytically for proportional--derivative (PD) control at finite simulation frequency, where proportional feedback changes the effective derivative gain and derivative feedback changes the effective inertia. We then interpret dynamic rope manipulation and underwater swimming through the same perspective. Finally, we present a practical procedure for choosing observables, identifying omitted physics, and determining effective parameters. Renormalization offers robotics a complementary path across the sim-to-real gap: effective parameters, real behavior.

</details>


### [126] [A Case Study on the Acceptance of a Humanoid Robotic Head Employed in Three Public Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24113)
*Marcel Heisler,Luca Randecker,Christian Becker-Asano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了一款具备多语言自然语言处理能力和情感模拟功能的人形机器人头部，在三个不同公共场所（旅游信息中心、市立图书馆、建筑管理局）连续运行多日的用户接受度。基于TAM2问卷结果显示，用户普遍认为该机器人有用且易用，但公共场所比办公环境更适合部署，且20%的用户认为响应速度过慢影响对话流畅性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究人形机器人在不同真实公共场所（而非实验室环境）中的用户接受度差异，验证多语言交互和情感表达对用户接受度的影响，并识别当前机器人交互系统在实际部署中存在的瓶颈。

Method: 1) 构建具备自然语言处理、情感模拟后端和多模态言语响应（整合情感表达）的机器人系统；2) 在三个不同公共场所（旅游信息中心、市立图书馆、建筑管理局）连续运行数天；3) 采用TAM2（技术接受模型扩展版）问卷评估用户的感知有用性、感知易用性和使用动机。

Result: 1) 用户平均认为机器人有用、易用且有使用动机，不受地点显著影响；2) 旅游信息中心和市立图书馆比建筑管理局更适合部署，后者交互意愿较低；3) 多语言响应功能受到用户高度赞赏；4) 约20%的用户认为响应时间过慢，影响了对话流畅性。

Conclusion: 人形机器人在公共场所（如旅游信息中心、图书馆）的接受度高于办公环境（如建筑管理局），多语言能力是重要优势，但响应延迟是当前系统的主要短板，需要在未来工作中优化以改善对话流畅性。

Abstract: Previous research has shown that a human-like robot's acceptance heavily depends on the setting in which it operates and its ability to perform relevant tasks. This paper, first, reports on how our robot processes natural language to generate a multimodal, verbal response integrating emotional expressions based on an emotion simulation backend. Then, it describes how visitors were invited to speak with our robot in their own language at three different, public locations, where the robot was running continuously for several days. The TAM2 questionnaire results reveal that on average users were motivated to use the robot and found it rather useful and easy to use regardless of the specific location. However, public spaces like the tourist information and the city library seem to be a better fit for our interactive, robotic head than an office environment such as the building authority, where the willingness to interact was lower. Overall, the robot's multi-lingual responses were very much appreciated, but every fifth user found the response time too slow impeding the dialog flow, which remains to be improved in future work.

</details>


### [127] [DeVA: Decoupled Video-Action Model with physical guidance for robot policy learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24159)
*Mengqi Zhang,Sahil Khose,Simar Kareer,Yuchen Song,Unnat Jain,Judy Hoffman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DeVA是一种解耦的视频-动作模型，通过专门化的视频和动作专家模块、多层次特征传递和物理显著性引导（如可操作性/深度信息），在有限数据下实现更快的收敛和更强的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型的静态预训练目标对物理动态和时间因果关系的监督有限，控制相关知识需从下游机器人演示中学习；而现有视频-动作模型要么将视频和动作预测耦合在共享骨干中导致策略优化困难，要么未能充分利用视频信息指导动作分支。

Method: 提出DeVA（解耦视频-动作模型），包含：(1) 专门的视频专家和动作专家模块；(2) 多层次特征传递机制，从视频各层向动作专家传递表示；(3) 物理显著性引导（可操作性/深度）监督中间视频特征和动作流。

Result: 在仿真基准和真实场景部署中，DeVA在有限数据下表现优异，相比统一架构收敛更快，物理引导带来显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 解耦视频-动作专家架构结合多层次特征传递和物理显著性引导，能在数据有限的情况下实现更高效的策略学习，验证了物理先验知识对机器人操作任务的重要性。

Abstract: Generalizable robot manipulation requires policies that can anticipate how visual scenes evolve while executing language instructions. While recent Vision-Language-Action models benefit from large-scale pretraining, their predominantly static pretraining objectives provide limited supervision for physical dynamics and temporal causality, leaving control-relevant knowledge to be learned from downstream robot demonstrations. Video generative models offer a promising foundation by encoding rich spatiotemporal priors through future predictions. However, existing Video-Action Models either couple video and action prediction in a shared backbone, making policy adaptation harder to optimize, or under-utilize video information when guiding the action branch. In this work, we introduce DeVA, a Decoupled Video-Action model with specialized video and action experts, multi-level feature transfer, and physically salient guidance. DeVA transfers representations from multiple video layers to the action expert, enabling rich information exchange while making policy learning more tractable. It further supervises intermediate video features and the action stream with physically salient guidance (affordance/depth). Experiments on both simulation benchmarks and real-world deployment demonstrate strong performance with limited data, faster convergence than a unified architecture, and clear performance gains from physical guidance.

</details>


### [128] [Not Forgotten: Implementation and Evaluation of a Personalized Episodic Memory for the Humanoid Robot Head Kim](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24190)
*Steve Aschenbrenner,Marcel Heisler,Thomas Sievers,Christian Becker-Asano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个轻量级 episodic memory 模块，将基于向量的语义检索与 LLM 控制的对话系统集成到人形机器人 Kim 上，使机器人能在跨会话中记住并检索相关历史交互。在线视频研究(N=43)表明，该模块显著提升了感知社交性、信任度和温暖感，且未引发隐私不适或恐怖谷效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 依赖大语言模型的社交机器人无法跨会话保留信息，这种记忆缺失违反了社交期待，阻碍了持久人际关系的形成。

Method: 设计了一个轻量级 episodic memory 模块，采用混合评分函数（余弦相似度 + 记忆强度指标）进行向量语义检索，从历史交互中召回上下文相关的记忆并注入到 LLM 生成提示中。部署于双足人形机器人头 Kim 上。

Result: 在线视频研究(N=43)使用 HRIES 量表评估，episodic memory 显著提升了感知社交性(d=0.60, p<.001)，信任度(d=0.62)和温暖感(d=0.56)效果最强，感知干扰无变化(d=0.00)。

Conclusion: Episodic memory 在具身人机交互中作为社交润滑剂发挥作用，提升关系质量而不引发负面情感反应。

Abstract: Social robots that rely on large language models for conversation are unable to retain information across sessions. This absence of memory violates social expectations, potentially preventing the formation of persistent relationships. This paper presents a lightweight episodic memory module that integrates vector-based semantic retrieval with an LLM-controlled dialog system, deployed on the humanoid robot head Kim. The module employs a hybrid scoring function combining cosine similarity with a memory strength metric to retrieve contextually relevant past interactions and inject them into the generation prompt. The system was evaluated in a within-subjects video-based online study (N = 43) using the Human-Robot Interaction Evaluation Scale (HRIES). Results show that episodic memory significantly increased perceived sociability (d = 0.60, p < .001), with the strongest effects on perceived trustworthiness (d = 0.62) and warmth (d = 0.56). Perceived disturbance remained unchanged (d = 0.00), indicating that the implemented approach to personalized recall did not trigger privacy-related discomfort or uncanny valley effects. These findings suggest that episodic memory serves as a social lubricant in embodied Human-Robot Interaction, enhancing relational quality without eliciting negative affective responses.

</details>


### [129] [Surgical Re-enactment for Operating Room Workflow Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24206)
*Jana Nina Friedrich,Andrea Karin Maria Ross,Angelo Henriques,Mario Peter Martin Weisser,Ling Zhang,Mohammad Ali Nasseri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在重建手术室中重演完整手术流程的方法，用于生成可重复、可标注的工作流数据集，以支持手术活动识别、场景图生成和手术过程建模，并已在机器人辅助眼科手术中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现互联智能手术室需要对手术工作流有深入理解，而获取真实手术室中的多视角数据集面临伦理审批、空间限制和无菌规范等重重困难。

Method: 采用专家咨询、结构化工作流形式化、手术室重建、角色扮演训练、真实手术室观察、迭代录制与事后复盘相结合的分步方法论。

Result: 成功在重建手术室中实现了完整手术流程的可重复重演，生成了可用于训练活动识别模型、生成场景图和形式化手术过程模型的数据集。

Conclusion: 该方法可被其他研究团体无缝采用到各自的手术领域，为手术工作流研究提供高质量数据支持。

Abstract: The introduction of new technologies, such as surgical robots, is driving the vision of a connected, smart operating room (OR). However, realizing this vision requires a deep understanding of surgical workflows, which relies on realistic datasets capturing the actions of all OR personnel from both full room and surgical field perspectives. Acquiring such data in real ORs is prohibitively challenging due to factors such as ethics committee approvals, limited space for camera installation, and sterility regulations preventing the use of tracking markers. We present a step-by-step methodology for re-enacting complete surgical procedures in a reconstructed OR. This approach enables the creation of repeatable and annotatable workflow datasets for training activity recognition models, generating scene graphs, and formalizing surgical process models. Developed for robot-assisted ophthalmic surgery, our methodology combines expert consultation, structured workflow formalization, OR reconstruction, role-based training, real OR observation, and iterative recording with post-take debriefing. We provide concrete recommendations to allow other research groups to seamlessly adopt this methodology for their own surgical domains.

</details>


### [130] [FloAff-Kitchen: Bridging Navigation and Manipulation via Canonical and Progressive Floor Affordance Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24207)
*Ping Zhong,Manling Teng,Tao Wu,Bolei Chen,Jiazhi Xia,Jianxin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向机器人移动操作的地面可供性（FloAff）预测统一框架，包含规范表示学习（CFAR）和渐进式可供性先验学习（PFAL），通过消除无关空间变化和跨技能知识迁移，在多个基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地面可供性预测方法存在两大问题：1）无关空间上下文和任意物体朝向导致表示歧义；2）异构操作技能中共享知识与任务特定知识相互纠缠，难以有效迁移。

Method: 提出统一框架：1）规范地面可供性表示（CFAR）——通过保留可供性相关的局部结构同时消除无关空间变化，学习规范交互几何；2）渐进式地面可供性学习（PFAL）——从基础操作任务中学习可迁移的FloAff先验，并逐步适配到异构下游操作技能。

Result: 在跨场景、多视图的FloAff-Kitchen基准上，方法在三个基准设置下均一致优于强基线方法，消融实验验证了各组件的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过规范表示消除空间歧义和渐进式先验迁移学习，有效提升了机器人从自我中心多模态感知中预测地面可供性的能力。

Abstract: Mobile manipulation requires robots to identify Floor Affordance (FloAff) that maximizes downstream manipulation success rather than merely ensuring navigation feasibility. FloAff prediction is a target-conditioned local spatial reasoning problem, yet existing methods suffer from representation ambiguity caused by irrelevant spatial context and arbitrary object orientations, while entangling shared and task-specific knowledge across heterogeneous manipulation skills. To address these challenges, we propose a unified framework for FloAff prediction from egocentric multimodal perception, consisting of canonical representation learning and progressive affordance prior learning. Specifically, we introduce a Canonical Floor Affordance Representation (CFAR), which learns canonical interaction geometry by preserving affordance-relevant local structure while eliminating nuisance spatial variations unrelated to robot base placement. We further propose Progressive Floor Affordance Learning (PFAL), which learns transferable FloAff priors from a foundation manipulation task and progressively adapts them to heterogeneous downstream manipulation skills. To facilitate systematic evaluation, we establish the first cross-scene, multi-view FloAff-Kitchen benchmark covering diverse manipulation skills, scene layouts, furniture styles, and viewpoints. Extensive experiments on three benchmark settings demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms strong baselines, while ablation studies validate the contribution of each proposed component. Project page: https://csu-hero-lab.github.io/FloAff-Kitchen_Web/

</details>


### [131] [Quality-Adaptive Multi-UAV 3D Reconstruction with Sparse Workload Redistribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24233)
*Benjamin Sportich,Kenza Boubakri,Olivier Simonin,Alessandro Renzaglia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种质量自适应的多无人机协同3D重建策略，通过TSDF置信度引导视点生成、两级协调机制（局部散度惩罚+全局不平衡校正），在提升路径效率的同时实现高保真度重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空中平台的计算和能源能力有限，多无人机在线协同规划3D重建路径仍是一个重大挑战。

Method: 1) 基于TSDF置信度的质量导向视点生成与信息增益估计；2) 视点评价中的惩罚因子促进局部散度；3) 基于正则化聚类与最优任务分配的全局不平衡校正机制（仅在不平衡配置被检测到时触发）。

Result: 相比最先进的多无人机探索方法，该方法在路径效率、重建覆盖率和精度方面均表现更优。

Conclusion: 所提质量自适应分散决策策略能有效实现多无人机协同3D地图构建，满足用户自定义保真度目标，且代码已开源。

Abstract: 3D reconstruction of unknown environments is a key application in robotics but is severely limited by the computational and energy capabilities of current aerial platforms. Deploying multiple UAVs and providing efficient and scalable path planning strategies are common approaches, but effective online coordination among UAVs remains a significant challenge. To address this problem, we propose a quality-adaptive decentralized decision-making strategy to build a 3D map with user-defined degrees of fidelity. The approach integrates a quality-oriented criterion based on TSDF confidence into view generation and information gain estimation to produce viewpoints consistent with the desired fidelity target. Additionally, we employ two levels of coordination: a penalty factor in the viewpoint evaluation to encourage local dispersion among the UAVs and a global imbalance correction mechanism. The latter, based on regularized clustering and optimal task assignment, is only triggered when an unbalanced configuration relative to high-information regions is detected. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed method improves path efficiency compared to state-of-the-art multi-UAV exploration approaches, while also achieving higher-fidelity reconstructions in terms of coverage and accuracy. We make our code publicly available to the community.

</details>


### [132] [FeelWorld: Visuo-Tactile World Model for Hierarchical Contact Prediction and Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24267)
*Wenxuan Ma,Chaofan Zhang,Chao Xue,Yinghao Cai,Guocai Yao,Shaowei Cui,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FeelWorld是一个层次化的视觉-触觉世界模型，通过联合预测未来视觉潜在表示和三种触觉状态（接触状态、3D触觉潜在表示、滑动状态），并引入接触门控非对称注意力机制和自回归训练，显著提升了接触密集任务中的视觉预测质量和零样本规划成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉世界模型主要捕捉外观动态，忽略了控制接触交互的触觉状态，导致预测的未来虽然在视觉上合理但违反物理动力学。因此需要将触觉感知融入世界模型以提高物理真实性。

Method: 1) 层次化的视觉-触觉世界模型，联合预测未来视觉潜在表示和三种触觉状态（接触状态、3D触觉潜在表示、滑动状态）；2) 共享潜在动力学模型的显式监督；3) 接触门控非对称注意力机制：接触前保持纯视觉预测路径，接触时进行联合视觉-触觉动力学预测；4) 自回归展开训练和上下文噪声注入提高鲁棒性；5) 利用预测的接触和滑动状态进行接触感知的CEM规划。

Result: 在芯片抓取、水果抓取和USB插入任务中，FeelWorld将10步LPIPS从0.084降至0.058；在80步自回归展开后LPIPS比视觉基线低61%；平均零样本规划成功率达81.7%。

Conclusion: FeelWorld为将触觉感知融入世界模型提供了一种有效方法，在接触密集任务中显著提升了视觉预测的质量和规划成功率。

Abstract: Humans plan physical interactions by imagining the possible outcomes of candidate actions. However, existing visual world models primarily capture appearance dynamics while overlooking the tactile states that govern contact-rich interactions, potentially producing imagined futures that appear visually plausible but violate physical dynamics. We introduce FeelWorld, a hierarchical visuo-tactile world model that jointly predicts future visual latents and three tactile states. FeelWorld organizes these states hierarchically as contact state, a 3D tactile latent that encodes force-related information, and slip state. These states are jointly predicted by a shared latent dynamics model with explicit supervision. To prevent irrelevant tactile signals during free-space motion from degrading visual prediction, we introduce a contact-gated asymmetric attention mechanism that maintains a visual-only prediction pathway before contact and enables joint visuo-tactile dynamics prediction during contact. The model is further trained with autoregressive rollouts and context noise injection to improve robustness to compounding errors. The predicted contact and slip states also support contact-aware CEM planning. Experiments on chip grasping, fruit grasping, and USB insertion show that FeelWorld reduces 10-step LPIPS from 0.084 to 0.058 and maintains an LPIPS that is 61% lower than that of the visual baseline after an 80-step autoregressive rollout. FeelWorld also achieves an average zero-shot planning success rate of 81.7%, providing an effective approach for incorporating tactile sensing into world models.

</details>


### [133] [Learning Adaptive Multi-Task Guidance, Navigation, and Control via Hypernetworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24292)
*Ricard Marsal I Castan,Aman Arora,Antoine Richard,Andrej Orsula,Cédric Pradalier,Miguel A. Olivares-Méndez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HYPER-GNC框架，利用超网络将物理信息任务嵌入映射到共享策略网络权重，使单个控制器掌握四种GNC任务，并在连续任务空间中实现零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法为每个任务维护独立策略导致架构脆弱且缺乏灵活性，需要一个既通用又资源高效的统一控制器。

Method: 超网络将物理信息任务嵌入映射到共享Actor-Critic策略的权重，通过多任务强化学习训练单个紧凑控制器。

Result: 样本效率与单任务专家相当，在惯性扰动和外力干扰下保持稳定性，在物理卫星模拟器上成功验证了仿真到现实的迁移。

Conclusion: HYPER-GNC实现了多任务GNC控制的通用性，支持零样本适应新任务配置，代码和模型已开源。

Abstract: Autonomous free-flying robots in orbital environments require controllers that are both versatile and resource-efficient, yet maintaining a separate, task-specific policy for each mission profile is architecturally brittle and limits operational flexibility as requirements evolve. We introduce HYPER-GNC, a multi-task reinforcement learning framework in which a hypernetwork maps physics-informed task embeddings to the weights of a shared actor-critic policy, enabling a single compact controller to master four distinct GNC tasks: velocity tracking, docking, inspection, and navigation with obstacle avoidance. The continuous embedding space allows the controller to generalize to novel mission configurations at deployment time without any retraining. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HYPER-GNC achieves sample efficiency comparable to single-task specialists while maintaining stability under significant inertial perturbations and external body wrenches. We further validate the framework on a physical satellite emulator, successfully bridging the simulation-to-reality gap across all mission profiles. Code, trained models, and deployment scripts are made publicly available to support reproducibility.

</details>


### [134] [PAC-DP: PAC-Bayesian Diffusion Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24296)
*Mohammad Hasan Yeganegi,Dian Yu,Andrea Del Prete,Majid Khadiv,Matteo Saveriano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PAC-DP通过将扩散策略建模为贝叶斯神经网络并引入PAC-Bayes泛化界正则化项，提出了一种新的训练目标，在有限数据条件下提升了机器人操作任务的性能，尤其在低数据场景和复杂任务中改进最为显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准扩散策略通过最小化去噪目标进行训练，在机器人领域常见的有穷数据场景下对泛化能力的控制有限，需要一种理论上更严谨的正则化方法来提升性能。

Method: 将扩散策略建模为贝叶斯神经网络，定义PAC-Bayes泛化界，推导出新的训练目标，在标准去噪损失基础上添加后验与先验参数分布之间的KL散度正则化项。

Result: 实验结果表明PAC-DP在多个机器人操作基准上提升了去噪性能、降低了变分负对数似然、并获得了更高的任务成功率，尤其在低数据训练场景和复杂任务中改进最大。

Conclusion: PAC-DP提供了一种理论上严谨的机器人策略学习框架，在不显著增加训练时间的前提下有效正则化扩散策略训练，为有限数据场景下的机器人操作学习提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion Policies (DPs) are able to perform complex manipulation tasks. However, DPs are typically trained by minimizing a denoising objective, which provides limited control over generalization in the finite-data regimes common in robotics. In this letter, we propose PAC-DP, an approach that increases the performance of DPs in robotic manipulation tasks. By modeling the DP as a Bayesian neural network, and defining a PAC-Bayes generalization bound, we derive a novel training objective that augments the standard denoising loss with a Kullback-Leibler divergence regularizer between the posterior and prior parameter distributions. From the theoretical perspective, our approach provides a principled approach to regularize the training of DPs without significantly increasing the training time. From the practical point of view, experimental results demonstrate improved denoising performance, lower variational negative log-likelihood, and higher success rates across multiple robotic manipulation benchmarks. Crucially, the largest improvements are observed in low-data training regimes and complex tasks, establishing PAC-DP as a theoretically grounded framework for robot policy learning.

</details>


### [135] [A note on the motion representation and configuration update in time stepping schemes for the constrained rigid body](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24317)
*A. Müller*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文从几何视角证明了受约束刚体动力学建模中，配置空间的选择直接影响约束满足情况。当约束构成配置空间的子群时，数值积分方案能精确满足约束。SE(3)作为半直积李群，其子群对应机械系统中的低副运动副，因此是受约束刚体DAE建模的适当配置空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察到使用不同李群（SO(3)×R³与SE(3)）作为配置空间时，约束满足情况存在差异，需要从几何角度解释这一现象并确定更优的配置空间。

Method: 采用几何视角分析约束满足的代数条件，研究约束与配置空间子群之间的关系，并分析SE(3)子群在机械系统建模中的意义。

Result: 约束若能定义配置空间的子群，则数值积分方案可精确满足约束；SE(3)的子群与低副运动副（Reuleaux pairs）对应，是受约束刚体DAE建模的适当配置空间。

Conclusion: SE(3)是受约束刚体数值DAE建模的合适配置空间，但该结论不能直接推广到多体系统。

Abstract: The dynamics of a holonomically constrained rigid body can be modeled by Newton-Euler equations subjected to geometric constraints. This is frequently formulated as a differential-algebraic equation (DAE) system of index 1. Inmultibody system (MBS) dynamics it is common (1) to numerically solve this system by means of integration schemes for ordinary differential equations, and (2) to treat the rigid body motion on the direct product Lie group SO (3)R3, although rigid body motions form the semidirect product Lie group SE (3). It is has been observed that the constraint satisfaction depends on which Lie group is used as configuration space (c-space). In this paper the problem is considered from a geometric perspective. It is shown that the constraints are exactly satisfied by a numerical integration scheme if they define a subgroup of the c-space. The subgroups of SE (3) have a significance for modeling mechanical systems, including lower kinematic (Reuleaux) pairs and are implicitly used in MBS modeling. It is concluded that SE (3) is the appropriate cspace for numerical DAE modeling of a constrained rigid body. This result does not immediately apply to MBS, however.

</details>


### [136] [Continual-RL for Generalization in Autonomous Racing on the RoboRacer Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24320)
*Joel Siegert,Edoardo Ghignone,Michele Magno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于持续反向传播的持续强化学习框架，能在仅使用真实世界数据的情况下，在赛道上训练通用策略，并在15分钟内微调以超越经典控制器，同时提出了基于离线RL的对比方法并分析了可塑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器人面临适应动态环境的挑战，尤其是在仿真无法涵盖所有真实世界配置时，需要在物理世界中进行强化学习。然而持续强化学习的框架和方法尚未得到充分探索，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出基于持续反向传播（Continual Backpropagation）的持续RL框架，先在多个赛道上训练通用策略，再在15分钟内进行微调；同时提出基于离线RL的对比方法，并对方法的可塑性（plasticity）进行仿真分析。

Result: 该框架仅使用真实世界数据即可训练出通用策略，并在15分钟微调后超越经典控制器。离线RL对比方法也被提出，可塑性分析在仿真环境中完成。

Conclusion: 持续反向传播框架能有效解决真实世界中机器人适应新环境的持续学习问题，在自动驾驶赛车场景中展现了快速微调并超越经典方法的潜力。

Abstract: A key challenge in modern robotics is to adapt to changing environments, a challenge that is exacerbated when simulations cannot encompass every possible real-world configuration, and therefore Reinforcement Learning (RL) in the physical world becomes necessary. Continual Reinforcement Learning provides the tools to address this challenge; however, both the frameworks and the methods remain underexplored. Autonomous Racing and in particular the RoboRacer competition provide a testing ground for such methods, as learning to drive on a new track-floor combination with the least amount of new experience naturally frames a continual learning problem. This work tries to address this gap by proposing a continual RL framework based on Continual Backpropagation that is able, with only real-world data, to train a generalistic policy on a set of tracks and then fine- tune it within 15 minutes to outperform classical controllers. Furthermore, a comparison method based on offline RL is proposed, and a simulation analysis of the plasticity properties of the methods is conducted.

</details>


### [137] [Model Predictive Planner for UAV Navigation in Non-Convex Air Corridors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24369)
*Henrique Silva,Marcelo A. Santos,Guilherme V. Raffo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于混合整数跟踪模型预测控制的无人机运动规划框架，用于非凸城市空中走廊导航。该框架通过在单一优化问题中强制满足走廊可行性和动态一致性，并嵌入基于最短路径的偏移成本来保证收敛并避免局部最小值，数值仿真验证了其无需外部全局规划即可生成满足约束且收敛到目标的动态有效轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理非凸城市空中走廊的无人机导航时，常面临局部极小值问题，且依赖外部全局规划阶段，导致系统复杂度和计算负担增加。亟需一种能够在单一优化问题中同时保证走廊可行性、动态一致性及目标收敛性的规划框架。

Method: 采用混合整数跟踪模型预测控制（MI-TMPC）作为核心规划器，将走廊可行性和动态一致性约束整合到同一个优化问题中。为应对非凸几何引起的局部极小值，引入基于最短路径的偏移成本并嵌入可行性约束，以引导无人机向目标收敛。

Result: 数值仿真结果表明，所提方法能够生成动态有效的无人机轨迹，满足走廊约束条件，并成功收敛到目标位置，且无需依赖外部全局规划模块。

Conclusion: 所提出的混合整数跟踪模型预测控制框架能够有效解决非凸城市空中走廊中的无人机运动规划问题，避免了外部全局规划的依赖，具有良好的可行性与收敛性。

Abstract: This work presents a motion planning framework for UAV navigation in non-convex urban air corridors. The planner is based on a mixed-integer tracking model predictive control formulation that enforces corridor feasibility and dynamic consistency within a single optimization problem. To guarantee convergence to the target and mitigate the occurrence of local minima induced by non-convex geometry, a shortest-path-based offset cost with feasibility constraints is embedded directly into the planning problem. Numerical simulations show that the proposed formulation generates dynamically valid trajectories that satisfy the corridor constraints and converge to the target without relying on external global planning stages.

</details>


### [138] [ArmnetBench v0.1: Parallel Real-World Evaluation of Manipulation Policies on a Low-Cost Arm Farm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24481)
*Praveen Selvaraj,Lorenzo Uttini,Ville Kuosmanen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ArmnetBench v0.1是一个在低成本SO-101机械臂集群上运行的基准测试，包含2,518次策略部署和600条参考演示，所有3,118条轨迹均标注为成功、次优或失败三类。该基准在轻量现场监督下评估了7种策略在12个任务上的表现（含单臂和双臂配置），并提供质量标注的轨迹数据供下游学习使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的评估是开发通用机器人操作策略的瓶颈，因为每次部署都需要物理硬件和操作员进行设置、重置和评分。作者旨在构建一个可在轻量监督下大规模运行的基准测试，以解决评估效率低下的问题。

Method: 在低成本的SO-101机械臂集群（arm farm）上运行基准测试，每个策略在每个任务上使用50条示范进行训练或微调，总计3,118条轨迹（含2,518次策略部署和600条参考示范），所有轨迹都经过人工标注为成功、次优或失败三类。

Result: 端到端验证了机械臂集群的有效性，比较了7种策略在12个任务上的表现（含单臂和双臂配置），发布了包含3,118条核心轨迹的数据集，支持LeRobot v3.0和RoboMeter格式。

Conclusion: 质量标注的轨迹不仅用于评估，还能支持下游学习任务（如奖励建模、预测世界模型、基于混合质量数据训练策略），并为社区提供了一个共享预算下的初始比较排行榜。

Abstract: Real-world evaluation is a bottleneck in developing generalist robot manipulation policies. Each rollout requires physical hardware and an operator to set up, reset, and score it. We introduce ArmnetBench v0.1, a benchmark run on a fleet of low-cost SO-101 cells under light on-site supervision. v0.1 validates this arm farm end to end and compares 7 policies across 12 tasks with both single-arm and bimanual configurations. Each policy is trained or fine-tuned on 50 demonstrations per task; the benchmark contains 2,518 policy rollouts and 600 reference demonstrations. All 3,118 episodes carry a three-way label (successful, suboptimal, or failure). Policy rollouts are human-scored, while demonstrations are successful by construction. Beyond evaluation, its quality-labelled trajectories support downstream learning, from reward and predictive world models to policies trained on mixed-quality data. The leaderboard is an initial comparison under this shared budget. We release the 3,118 core episodes in LeRobot v3.0 and RoboMeter formats.

</details>


### [139] [Distributed Coordination for Resilient Multi-UAV Remote Sensing: A Photovoltaic Inspection Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24482)
*Guillermo GP-Lenza,Miguel Fernandez-Cortizas,Martin Molina,Pascual Campoy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SwarmLink——一种无人机群非侵入式点对点通信基础设施，可将分布式拍卖机制集成到现有飞行框架中，统一处理目标分配、平台故障恢复和数据质量触发重分配三种中断场景，无需地面站干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集中式地面站协调器存在单点故障风险，链路或站点故障会导致传感覆盖缺口，需要操作员干预才能填补。

Method: 设计SwarmLink通信基础设施，非侵入式地扩展任何现有空中框架，赋予其对等协调能力；在Aerostack2上应用分布式拍卖机制，将初始目标分配、平台故障恢复和数据质量触发重分配统一为同一运行时机制。

Result: 三种中断场景（初始分配、平台故障、数据质量问题）均可简化为对剩余目标和活跃平台的重新拍卖，无需修改Aerostack2核心，也无需地面站参与。

Conclusion: SwarmLink通过分布式拍卖统一处理多类中断，消除了对集中式地面站的依赖，提升了无人机群的鲁棒性和自主性。

Abstract: Deploying multiple UAVs for remote sensing enables proportional reductions in mission time, but realizing these benefits requires the fleet to coordinate at runtime: distributing sensing targets, responding to platform failures, and recovering from degraded data quality. In inspection campaigns, where mission value depends on complete coverage and the usability of every capture, a centralized ground-station coordinator is a single point of failure: a lost link or station fault leaves sensing gaps that cannot be filled without operator intervention. We propose the \textbf{SwarmLink}, an inter-agent communication infrastructure that non-invasively extends any existing aerial framework with peer-to-peer coordination capability, without modifying the host system. We apply it to photovoltaic plant inspection as a representative large-scale sensing campaign, extending Aerostack2 with a distributed auction that unifies initial sensing-target allocation, platform-failure recovery, and data-quality-triggered reassignment into a single runtime mechanism. All three disruption scenarios reduce to the same re-auction over remaining targets and active platforms, requiring zero modifications to the Aerostack2 core and no ground-station involvement during the mission.

</details>


### [140] [τ: Learning Touch-Augmented Vision-Language-Action Models from Future Visual Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24485)
*Ning Cheng,Jinan Xu,Wanlin Li,Yangzhi Chen,Jing Gao,Yiqun Wang,Kelan Peng,Wenjuan Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出τ框架，通过JEPA启发的未来视觉监督学习动作条件化时空触觉表征，并融合视觉-语言特征用于动作生成，在有限数据下提升操作性能；同时引入TacAura多模态触觉数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在将触觉表征适配到预训练VLA模型时面临数据和建模两大挑战：数据层面，任务特定演示有限制约表征质量，大规模预训练成本高昂；建模层面，现有方法要么关注瞬时接触状态，要么用6D wrench序列建模时序交互，高维触觉信号未被充分探索。

Method: 提出τ框架，采用Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA)思想，利用未来视觉监督在潜在空间中学习动作条件化的时空触觉表征，再与视觉-语言特征融合进行动作生成。该监督仅在训练时使用，不增加部署开销。同时构建TacAura数据集，包含同步的视觉、本体感知和基于视觉的触觉信号。

Result: τ在接触丰富的操作任务上优于现有模型，并能泛化到未见过的物体和场景，实现了更好的操作性能和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 利用未来视觉监督学习触觉表征并与VLA模型融合，是提升接触丰富操作任务性能的有效途径，且具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Learning the informative tactile representation while effectively adapting it to pretrained Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models remains challenging at both the data and modeling levels. At the data level, limited task-specific demonstrations constrain representation quality, whereas large-scale pretraining incurs substantial costs. At the modeling level, existing methods either focus on instantaneous contact states or model temporal interaction dynamics using 6D wrench sequences, leaving high-dimensional tactile signals underexplored. To address these challenges, we present τ, a touch-augmented VLA framework that learns an action-conditioned spatiotemporal tactile representation from future visual supervision inspired by the Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA), and fuses it with vision-language features for action generation under limited data. This supervision operates in latent space and is used only during training, adding no deployment overhead. We also introduce TacAura, a dataset of synchronized vision, proprioception, and vision-based tactile signals across four representative contact-rich manipulation tasks. Experiments show that τ outperforms existing models and generalizes to unseen objects and scenes, delivering improved manipulation performance and robustness

</details>


### [141] [KAI: A Kinematic-Aware Interface for Data-Efficient Articulated Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24493)
*Yaping Li,Zhaxizhuoma,Qiaojun Yu,Jia Zeng,Dahua Lin,Jiangmiao Pang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为KAI的中间表示方法，通过嵌入几何和运动学先验知识，显著提升了铰接物体操作策略的样本效率和泛化能力，在仅使用一半演示数据的情况下达到82.9%的平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铰接物体的操作需要理解运动学结构，但仅靠机器人演示学习这种结构既困难又昂贵，现有方法样本效率低，难以在低数据场景下有效学习。

Method: 提出Kinematic-Aware Articulation Interface (KAI)，一种结构化中间表示，显式编码铰接物体的几何与运动学先验，为策略学习提供强归纳偏置；设计为动作无关的表示，支持联合训练人类交互视频。

Result: 在六个仿真任务中，仅用一半演示数据达到82.9%平均成功率，匹配或超越基线性能；对未见背景和视觉干扰物表现出鲁棒泛化能力；结合视频联合训练在多样视觉干扰下达到70%以上成功率。

Conclusion: KAI通过显式运动学建模有效提升了样本效率和泛化性，其动作无关设计使其能够利用非专家的人类视频数据进行训练，为铰接物体操作提供了一种高效、鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Articulated object manipulation requires an understanding of kinematic structure that is difficult and costly to learn from robot demonstrations alone. We introduce the Kinematic-Aware Articulation Interface (KAI), a structured intermediate representation that captures the kinematic structure of articulated objects. By embedding interpretable geometric and kinematic priors into policy learning, KAI provides a strong inductive bias aligned with the underlying structure of articulated motion. This design effectively improves sample efficiency, with gains particularly pronounced in low-data regimes: across six simulation tasks, our method achieves an average success rate of 82.9%, matching or surpassing baseline performance while using only half the demonstration data. Our method also exhibits robust generalization to unseen backgrounds and visual distractors, transferring from a single clean training environment to cluttered real-world scenes. KAI's action-agnostic design further enables co-training with human interaction videos to enhance real-world robustness: under diverse visual distractions, our method with video co-training achieves over 70% average success rate.

</details>


### [142] [NEO: NeRF It Once, Edit It Many Times for Continuous Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24538)
*Mikołaj Zieliński,David Hall,Dominik Belter,Peyman Moghadam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NEO是一个统一的框架，通过语言引导的NeRF编辑实现机器人操作，包括物体移除、权重编辑方法以及首个用于定量评估机器人操作场景编辑的基准数据集，在物体移除和抓取放置实验中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NeRF场景编辑方法在机器人操作任务中存在视觉不一致、几何伪影等问题，且缺乏针对机器人操作的定量评估基准，因此亟需一个统一、高效且适用于机器人操作的NeRF编辑框架。

Method: (i) 语言引导的物体移除：结合神经场重采样与多视图一致的渐进式修复；(ii) 直接NeRF权重编辑：利用知识蒸馏，通过教师-学生模型组合原始与编辑后的NeRF，在机器人执行动作前对未来场景状态进行连贯建模；(iii) 构建首个定量评估基准数据集NEO-Dataset。

Result: 在物体移除和抓取放置机器人实验中，NEO在视觉连贯性和几何一致性方面均优于现有方法，并减少了先前方法常见的伪影。

Conclusion: NEO框架通过语言引导的NeRF编辑有效提升了机器人操作场景的编辑质量，验证了其在机器人任务中的实用性和优越性。

Abstract: In this paper, we present NEO, a unified framework providing language-guided NeRF editing for robotic manipulation. Our paper introduces (i) a language-guided object removal that combines neural field resampling with multiview-consistent progressive inpainting, (ii) a direct NeRF weight editing method utilizing knowledge distillation, composing original and edited NeRFs via a teacher-student model, enabling coherent modeling of future scene states before a robot executes an action, and (iii) the first benchmark (NEO-Dataset) for quantitatively evaluating NeRF scene editing methods suitable for robot manipulation. We show that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in scene editing tasks, including object removal and pick-and-place robotic experiments, yielding visually coherent and geometrically consistent edits that reduce artifacts commonly introduced by prior methods.

</details>


### [143] [Development of a Handheld Actuation Mechanism for a Tendon-driven Robotically Steered Guidewire](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24629)
*Saima Chavenet,Timothy A. Brumfiel,Revanth Konda,Jaydev P. Desai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种手持式驱动装置，用于腱驱动机器人导丝，通过紧凑的绕线机制实现电机控制的导丝转向，同时允许手动旋转和推进。该装置支持临床医生通过手柄上的摇杆和开关执行导丝的送进、旋转和弯曲运动，最大操作长度达1.5米，并在解剖学上准确的主动脉模型中进行了导航验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动导丝导航因血管迂曲、导丝尖端转向性差等因素具有挑战性，易导致血管损伤、穿孔、夹层、闭塞及术后并发症（如血栓形成），因此需要开发更可控的驱动装置。

Method: 将紧凑绕线机制集成到手持设备中，临床医生可通过摇杆和瞬时开关控制1.5米长的机器人可转向导丝进行送进、旋转和弯曲运动，同时保留手动操作能力。

Result: 该装置在解剖学上准确的主动脉模型幻影中成功完成了导航演示。

Conclusion: 该手持式驱动装置结合了电机驱动转向与手动操作灵活性，有望提高血管内介入手术中导丝导航的安全性和可控性。

Abstract: An endovascular intervention begins with a skilled clinician manually navigating a long, slender wire, called a guidewire, to the target location within the vasculature. Due to factors, such as vessel tortuosity and lack of steerability at the guidewire tip, manual navigation of a guidewire could be challenging, potentially resulting in vessel damage, perforation, dissection, and occlusion, as well as postsurgical complications, such as thrombosis. This work details the development of a handheld actuation mechanism for tendon-driven robotic guidewires by utilizing the aforementioned spooling mechanism. Incorporating the compact spooling mechanism into a handheld device enables clinicians to leverage both motorized guidewire steering and manual rotation and translation, if desired. The proposed device is designed such that, while holding the device, the clinician can execute feeding, rotation, and bending motions for up to 1.5 m of the robotically steerable guidewire using a joystick and momentary switch on the handle. Furthermore, the proposed device is demonstrated through navigation in an anatomically accurate phantom aorta model.

</details>


### [144] [Data Pyramid for Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24744)
*Yifan Ye,Yankai Fu,Yaoxu Lv,Bohan Hou,Jun Cen,Lingdong Kong,Duo Zheng,Tianxing Chen,Jiaming Liu,Ziang Cao,Yunfan Lou,Wei Chow,Xian Sun,Yingshuo Wang,Kuangzhi Ge,Xiaowei Chi,Xidong Zhang,Zhibo Pang,Yiwu Zhong,Sirui Han,Zhihe Lu,Weihao Yuan,Qifeng Chen,Michael Yu Wang,Yao Mu,Ziwei Liu,Jianfei Yang,Ping Luo,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统性地构建了具身数据生态的"金字塔"框架，涵盖五种互补数据源（真实机器人数据、UMI风格数据、自我中心/外部中心数据、仿真数据、通用视觉语言数据），并分析了这些数据源在可扩展性与机器人对齐之间的权衡，以及它们如何被用于训练具身基础模型（具身大脑模型、视觉-语言-动作模型、世界-动作模型）。最后讨论了六个开放性挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体无法像多模态基础模型那样通过消费互联网数据获得捷径，因为它们需要将观测与物理状态和动作耦合的数据。现有数据来源多样但缺乏系统性的组织和理解，亟需一个统一的框架来梳理具身数据生态。

Method: 提出具身数据"金字塔"框架，按数据源的可扩展性与机器人对齐程度进行层次化组织；对每种数据源从质量、多样性、可复用性和物理保真度四个维度进行刻画；通过数据配方的视角分析现有具身基础模型，考察不同数据源如何被选择、对齐和混合。

Result: 构建了包含五种数据源（真实机器人数据、UMI风格数据、自我中心/外部中心数据、仿真数据、通用视觉语言数据）的具身数据金字塔；揭示了不同数据源在可扩展性与机器人对齐之间的张力关系；分析了三类具身基础模型（具身大脑模型、视觉-语言-动作模型、世界-动作模型）的数据配方与其能力（感知、推理、规划、动作生成、世界预测）之间的关联。

Conclusion: 指出了六个开放性挑战：构建大规模触觉数据集、收集失败与恢复数据、开发可扩展的数据采集流程、跨具身形态的动作对齐、利用自我中心数据进行灵巧操作、设计原则性的机器人学习数据配方。这些挑战的解决将为下一代具身系统的设计奠定基础。

Abstract: Multimodal foundation models learned to see and to speak by consuming the whole internet. Embodied agents admit no such shortcut, since they require data that couple observations with physical states and actions. These signals can be provided, to varying degrees, by multiple data sources. In this work, we organize the embodied data ecosystem as a "pyramid" spanning five complementary sources: real-robot data, UMI-style data, egocentric and exocentric data, simulation data, and general vision-language data. We organize the pyramid around the tension between scalability and robot alignment, and further characterize each source in terms of data quality, diversity, reusability, and physical fidelity. We then analyze recent embodied foundation models through the lens of their data recipes, examining how different sources are selected, aligned, and mixed during pretraining. For embodied brain models, vision-language-action models, and world-action models alike, we relate data composition to capabilities in perception, reasoning, planning, action generation, and world prediction. We close by discussing six open challenges: building large-scale tactile datasets, collecting failure and recovery data, developing scalable data-collection pipelines, aligning actions across embodiments, leveraging egocentric data for dexterous manipulation, and designing principled data recipes for robot learning. We hope this work paves the foundation for the design of next-generation embodied systems.
