</details>


<div id='cs.CG'></div>

# cs.CG [[Back]](#toc)

### [145] [Exact Reachability by Positive Vertex-Centroid Moves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22981)
*Yongjie Guan*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文解决了The Open Projects Project的第60个问题，证明了在选定顶点在总质心处固定的自然约定下，非共线的平面n顶点配置可通过有限次顶点-质心移动精确变换为正n边形。该结论在更一般的配置空间中成立，并推广到高维情形，给出了基于秩一扰动和逆函数定理的存在性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决The Open Problems Project中第60个问题，探究平面n顶点配置通过顶点-质心移动变换为正n边形的充要条件，并推广到更一般的配置空间和高维情形。

Method: 将配置表示为坐标矩阵，将顶点-质心移动转化为单位矩阵的秩一扰动；构造显式的单步移动曲线和三步移动共轭词，得到具有可逆变微商的有限词端点映射；利用逆函数定理证明局部精确可达性，再通过连通性推广为全局性；对简单多边形方向类采用耳式缩减参数论证建立连通性；用代数方法确定正移动群的结构。

Result: 循环标记的平面n顶点配置（n≥3）可经有限次顶点-质心移动精确变为正n边形当且仅当其非共线。在更一般的连通开子集上，任意两个仿射张成配置均可通过正移动相连。对简单标记n边形，在保持简单性的前提下，每个方向类内部均可精确可达。在R^d中，n≥d+2时所有满维配置相互可达，n=d+1时方向是唯一障碍。确定了正移动生成的群结构。

Conclusion: 顶点-质心移动系统具有丰富的可达性结构：非共线是平面正多边形可达的充要条件；高维情形下维度与点数关系决定了可达性；方向是简单多边形保持简单性的关键障碍。该证明是存在性和非定量的。

Abstract: We resolve Problem 60 of The Open Problems Project under the natural convention that a selected vertex at the total centroid is immobile. A cyclically labeled planar $n$-vertex configuration, $n\ge3$, can be transformed exactly into a regular $n$-gon by finitely many vertex--centroid moves if and only if it is noncollinear. The affirmative direction is independent of this convention, and all moves may be chosen positive, so the selected vertex never crosses the centroid of the other vertices. More generally, any two configurations in a connected open subset of the affinely spanning configuration space can be joined by positive moves whose entire continuous execution remains in that subset. For simple labeled $n$-gons, allowing straight-angle vertices, this yields exact reachability while preserving simplicity precisely within each orientation class. In $\mathbb{R}^d$, it yields mutual reachability of all full-dimensional labeled $n$-point configurations when $n\ge d+2$, while orientation is the only obstruction when $n=d+1$. Writing configurations as coordinate matrices, each move becomes a rank-one perturbation of the identity. Explicit one-move curves and three-move conjugation words yield a finite-word endpoint map with invertible differential at every affinely spanning configuration. The inverse function theorem gives exact local reachability with all intermediate states confined to a prescribed neighborhood, and connectedness makes it global. An elementary ear-reduction argument establishes the required connectivity of simple-polygon orientation classes. The same algebra determines exactly the group generated by positive moves. The proof is existential and nonquantitative.

</details>


### [146] [Node Labeling in Line Diagrams of Ordered Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23283)
*Marcel Nöhre,Gerd Stumme*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 提出了一种两阶段算法，用于有序集线图的标签布局。第一阶段基于可读性标准过滤固定位置模型，第二阶段对溢出标签先通过稀疏网格代价函数初始化，再使用力导向模型微调，以最小化标签与节点的距离同时避免与节点、线条或其他标签交叉。算法还展示了在形式概念分析中对概念格进行双标签放置的灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线图作为有序集的可视化表示，其边缘近乎垂直的特性尚未被充分利用于标签布局优化。现有方法未针对性解决标签与节点、线条的交叉问题，且缺乏兼顾计算效率与布局质量的方案。

Method: 两阶段算法：(1) 过滤阶段，基于线图特定可读性标准筛选固定位置模型；(2) 溢出标签处理阶段，先通过稀疏网格上的代价函数进行初始布局，再用力导向模型微调。对形式概念分析场景，通过第一阶段预过滤及代价函数与力导向模型的微小调整，实现概念格的双标签放置需求。

Result: 所提算法能有效避免标签与节点、线条及其他标签的交叉，同时最小化节点与标签之间的距离。在形式概念分析中的应用表明，仅需第一阶段的预过滤和代价函数与力导向模型的少量调整即可满足双标签放置要求。

Conclusion: 该两阶段算法结合了基于可读性准则的过滤与力导向优化，在保证布局质量的同时兼顾了计算效率，且具有良好的灵活性和可扩展性，能够适应不同领域（如形式概念分析）的特殊标签约束。

Abstract: We propose a flexible, two-phase algorithm for labeling line diagrams of ordered sets, in which the nodes of direct neighbors in the order relation are connected by a straight, upward-pointing line. In contrast to the labeling of diagrams of arbitrary graphs, we benefit from the fact that all edges in line diagrams of ordered sets are more or less vertical. In this paper, we study the placement of all labels such that they do not intersect with any nodes, lines, or other labels while minimizing the distances between the nodes and their labels.
  Our approach starts by filtering the fixed-position model using line diagram-specific readability criteria. For labels that cannot be placed adjacent to their node (overflow labels), we exploit the free space in the graph's interior or the infinite space surrounding the drawing and link the labels with their respective node by straight binding lines that should not cross other nodes or labels if possible. To balance quality and runtime, we derive an initial placement of the overflow labels using a cost function over a sparse grid of candidates, followed by a force-based refinement step to fine-tune the layout.
  Furthermore, we demonstrate the flexibility of this approach by applying it to the visual constraints of line diagrams in the field of Formal Concept Analysis (FCA), where certain labels have to be placed above their node and others below. This special version of the algorithm shows that a pre-filtering in the first phase and minimal adjustments for the cost function and force-based model are sufficient to handle the dual labeling requirements of concept lattices.

</details>


### [147] [How to Draw a Planar Graph: An Experimental Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23356)
*Sergey Pupyrev*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实验评估了平面图绘制算法，发现没有一种算法在所有美学标准上表现最佳，优化一个视觉属性往往会损害另一个，设计通用鲁棒的算法仍是开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管平面图绘制算法在理论上能保证几何特性，但实际应用中标准算法可能产生间距不佳、面扭曲或小角度等问题，缺乏对各类算法在实际场景中表现的系统性实验评估。

Method: 在包含10-400个顶点的大规模平面图基准数据集上，对经典平面图绘制算法、基于力导向和压力的启发式方法以及直接优化视觉属性（边长度均匀性、面面积平衡、角度分辨率）的新优化方法进行了系统比较实验。

Result: 没有一种算法在所有美学标准上全面最优；优化某一视觉属性往往导致另一属性恶化；直接优化视觉标准能提升目标分数；结合多种方法的分数引导策略可获得最佳综合结果；但不存在简单通用的默认算法。

Conclusion: 设计一个在不同图族和美学标准上均表现良好的简单鲁棒算法，仍然是平面图绘制领域一个有待解决的实际问题。

Abstract: Planar graphs are central to graph drawing, with extensive results on planar layouts and related structures. Every planar graph admits a planar straight-line drawing, and algorithms can guarantee additional geometric or combinatorial properties. However, it is unclear which algorithms work best in practice. Even for small graphs with near-perfect manual drawings, standard algorithms might produce poor spacing, distorted faces, or small angles.
  We present an experimental evaluation of planar graph drawing algorithms on a large benchmark collection of small and medium-sized planar graphs (\(10\)--\(400\) vertices). The study compares established algorithms from the graph drawing literature, practical force-directed and pressure-based heuristics, and new optimization-based methods that directly improve visual properties such as edge-length uniformity, face-area balance, and angular resolution. The results show that no evaluated algorithm is best across all aesthetic criteria, and optimizing one visual property often worsens another. Directly optimizing visual criteria improves targeted scores, and score-guided combination of several methods gives the best aggregate results, but no simple algorithm emerges as a clear universal default. Designing a simple, robust algorithm that performs well across graph families and aesthetic criteria therefore remains an open practical problem.

</details>


### [148] [On balanced circuits in uniform rank-three oriented matroids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23385)
*Ji Zeng*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 将Streltsova和Wagner关于球面上平衡四元组的下界结果从球面点配置推广到秩三有向拟阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将球面点配置中平衡四元组计数下界的结果推广到更一般的组合结构——秩三有向拟阵中，以扩展该结论的适用范围。

Method: 利用有向拟阵的组合性质，将原始结果中基于球面点配置的几何论证转化为组合框架下的证明。

Result: 在秩三有向拟阵中，平衡四元组的下界至少为 (1/4) * floor(n/2) * floor((n-1)/2) * floor((n-2)/2) * floor((n-3)/2)。

Conclusion: 成功将平衡四元组计数下界从球面点配置推广到秩三有向拟阵，证明了该组合结构同样满足相同的下界。

Abstract: A set of four points $\{p_1,p_2,p_3,p_4\}$ on the sphere is a balanced quadruple if there are four real numbers $s_1,s_2,s_3,s_4$, two positive and two negative, such that $s_1p_1+s_2p_2+s_3p_3+s_4p_4 = 0$. Streltsova and Wagner proved that $n$ points on the sphere determine at least $\frac{1}{4} \left\lfloor \frac{n}{2} \right\rfloor \left\lfloor\frac{n-1}{2}\right\rfloor \left\lfloor\frac{n-2}{2}\right\rfloor \left\lfloor\frac{n-3}{2}\right\rfloor$ many balanced quadruples provided any three points are linearly independent. We extend this result from spherical point configurations to rank-three oriented matroids.

</details>


### [149] [A Fixed-Parameter Algorithm for Extending Upward Planar Drawings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23549)
*Vera Chekan,Robert Ganian,Viktoriia Korchemna*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文针对有向无环图的向上平面绘制扩展问题，提出了一种固定参数算法，使用“顶点+边删除距离”作为参数，有效处理了图中存在大量缺失边的情况，关键创新在于对跨越水平线段的缺失边集的规范性刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向上平面绘制是自然的有向图绘制风格，但其绘制扩展问题（给定子图的绘制，完成全图绘制）已知为NP难问题。现有参数化算法通常假设缺失边数量很少，限制了实际应用。本文旨在解决这一局限，设计即使在缺失边很多时仍保持高效的算法。

Method: 提出以“顶点+边删除距离”作为参数，并引入一种对跨越水平线段的“规范性”缺失边集的新的特征刻画，以此为基础设计固定参数算法。

Result: 成功设计出固定参数可解算法，克服了以往算法对少量缺失边的依赖，能够在缺失边较多的情况下仍保持可处理性。

Conclusion: 本文证明了使用“顶点+边删除距离”作为参数可以显著提升向上平面绘制扩展问题的可处理性，为解决更一般的图绘制扩展问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: An upward planar drawing of a directed acyclic graph is a planar drawing where every edge is pointed upward from its tail to head. Upward planar drawings are among the most natural drawing styles of directed graphs and have been researched in a variety of different settings, recently including that of drawing extension. In the drawing extension setting, one asks: given a graph $G$ and a (typically connected) subgraph $H$ of $G$ with a drawing $Γ(H)$, can we complete $Γ(H)$ to a drawing of $G$?
  Drawing extension problems have been studied for numerous drawing styles; the vast majority of these are NP-hard and a typical approach aimed at circumventing their intractability is to design parameterized algorithms where the parameter measures "how much" of $G$ is still missing from the pre-drawn graph $H$. Most algorithms obtained within this framework require only a small number of edges to be missing from $H$ in order to remain efficient. In this article, we present a fixed-parameter algorithm for extending upward planar drawings which overcomes this drawback by using the $\textit{vertex+edge deletion distance}$ as the parameter, thus achieving tractability even for instances with many missing edges. A key ingredient towards our result is a novel characterization of "canonical" sets of missing edges which cross a horizontal line segment in the drawing.

</details>


### [150] [Paged Geophylogenies: A Coloring Approach to External Labeling with Tree Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23559)
*Thomas Depian,Thomas C. van Dijk,Martin Nöllenburg*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文研究了分页地理系统发育图（paged geophylogenies）的绘制问题，通过将领导者（leaders）分配到多个页面来减少交叉。对于一般情况（任意子集分配），提出了整数线性规划（ILP）来最小化页面数；对于单子树分配的特殊情况，给出了O(n⁷)时间算法，并改进了交叉无标签存在性测试的算法。实验表明，该实现在数百个位点下可在1秒内完成计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统地理系统发育图（geophylogenies）中，从叶子节点到地图位点的领导者连线容易产生大量交叉，影响可读性。已有工作主要关注最小化交叉或完全避免领导者，但未考虑通过分页（paging）来组织领导者，从而在多个页面间分散交叉。

Method: 1) 对于一般情况（任意子集分配），提出整数线性规划（ILP）公式以最小化页面数。2) 证明该一般情况等价于单侧tanglegram问题，并给出多项式时间算法。3) 提出单子树分配约束（每个页面只包含单棵子树的领导者），并设计O(n⁷)时间复杂度的算法来最小化页面数，该算法同时改进了交叉无标签存在性测试的最快已知算法。4) 通过实验评估了领导者交叉数与页面数之间的权衡。

Result: 理论研究：针对单子树约束场景，给出了O(n⁷)时间的最小化页面数算法，并改进了交叉无标签存在性测试算法。实验评估：尽管最坏情况复杂度为O(n⁷)，实际实现在数百个位点（sites）的场景下可在1秒内完成。

Conclusion: 分页地理系统发育图通过将领导者分配到不同页面，可以在减少交叉的同时保持可视化清晰度。单子树分配的约束在实践中是可计算的（O(n⁷)复杂度在实际数据中运行很快），为地理系统发育图的可视化提供了新的有效策略。

Abstract: Geophylogenies are a common type of diagram for visualizing the evolutionary history of species in a geographic context. As a drawing problem, these diagrams are commonly modeled as a rooted binary ''phylogenetic'' tree $T$ where every leaf is associated with a point feature (''site'') in a rectangular map range. The tree is drawn downward planar, its leaves are placed at equidistant positions on the upper boundary of the map, and each leaf is connected to its corresponding site by a straight-line leader. Prior work focuses on minimizing the number of leader crossings, or avoiding leaders altogether. In this paper, we explore paged geophylogenies, where the leaders can be partitioned into multiple pages where only crossings within a page are counted. For the general case, where each page can contain an arbitrary subset of the leaders, we provide an integer linear programming (ILP) formulation for minimizing the number of pages, and a polynomial-time algorithm for a special case that is equivalent to a one-sided tanglegram. We argue that, from a visualization perspective, instead each page must contain only leaders for a single subtree, and provide an $\mathcal{O}(n^7)$ time algorithm for minimizing pages in this setting - which also involves improving the fastest known algorithm for testing the existence of a crossing-free labeling. Counter to what our worst case bound suggests, our implementation can handle instances with hundreds of sites within a second, as we show in our experimental evaluation, which further investigates the trade-offs between leader crossings and the number of pages.

</details>


### [151] [On Linear-Size Guillotine-Separable Subsets of Fat Convex Objects, Disks, and Squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24147)
*Mark de Berg,Debajyoti Kar,Arindam Khan,Rudrayan Kundu*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文解决了长期悬而未决的问题：任意大小的凸胖物体族在平面和更高维度中总存在线性大小的可分离子集。通过新的框架，改进了轴对齐正方形和圆盘的可分离下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Urrutia (1996) 提出任意 n 个凸物体是否存在大小为 Ω(n) 的可分离子集。Pach 和 Tardos (2000) 对线段给出否定回答，但对胖物体给出肯定结果。然而任意大小的胖凸物体（包括圆盘）是否具有线性大小的可分离子集一直悬而未决，主要障碍在于现有技术依赖轴对齐切割，而这对圆盘等物体不足够。

Method: 提出一种新的通用框架，利用递归超平面切割序列来分离胖凸物体。对于轴对齐正方形，利用额外结构性质优化下界；对于圆盘，利用 Oler 堆积不等式证明线性可分离性。

Result: 证明任意大小的胖凸物体族在平面上存在线性大小的可分离子集；扩展到任意固定维度 ℝ^d。轴对齐正方形下界从 3.51% 提升至 13.46%；圆盘至少可分离 n/93 个。

Conclusion: 完全解决了胖凸物体可分离子集线性大小的开放问题，给出了统一框架并显著改进了特殊情形下的下界。

Abstract: Let $\mathcal{K}$ be a family of pairwise disjoint objects in the plane. We say that a subset $\mathcal{K}^*\subseteq \mathcal{K}$ is \emph{separable} if it admits a sequence of guillotine cuts that separate all objects in $\mathcal{K}^*$ from each other while not cutting any of them. Urrutia (1996) asked whether any family of $n$ convex objects has a separable subset of size $Ω(n)$. Pach and Tardos (2000) answered this question negatively for line segments, but established positive results for fat objects of similar size. More recently, it was shown that sets of arbitrarily-sized axis-aligned squares also admit a separable subset of linear size. However, the question whether any set of arbitrarily-sized fat convex objects has a separable subset of linear size has remained open, even for disks. A major obstacle is that the existing technique for arbitrarily-sized squares uses only axis-aligned cuts, while even for disks, axis-aligned cuts alone are insufficient to obtain a separable subset of linear size.
  We resolve this longstanding open problem by proving that every family of pairwise disjoint fat convex objects has a separable subset of linear size. Our result extends to higher dimensions: any family of pairwise disjoint arbitrarily-sized fat convex objects in $\mathbb{R}^d$, where $d$ is a fixed constant, has a subset of linear size that is recursively separable by a sequence of hyperplane cuts. Our framework also yields improved guarantees for important special cases. For axis-aligned squares with axis-aligned guillotine cuts, we leverage additional structural properties of squares to show that at least $13.46\%$ of the squares are separable, improving the previous best bound of $9/256 \approx 3.51\%$ due to Chalermsook, Kugelmann, Orgo, Uniyal, and Zarsav (2025). For disks, by exploiting Oler's packing inequality, we prove that at least $n/93$ disks can always be separated.

</details>


### [152] [Point Set Embeddability with List Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24182)
*Thomas Depian,Joseph Dorfer,Boris Klemz,Matthias Pfretzschner,Lena Schlipf*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文研究点集嵌入问题（Point Set Embeddability）的推广形式——列表嵌入问题（List Embedding），其中每个顶点只能放置在指定的允许点列表中。主要结果包括：凸位置点集下，即使图是匹配且双标记的，问题也是NP难的；但对于连通图，若需保持组合嵌入则有多项式时间算法，否则有关于最大度的FPT算法。对于一般位置点集，双标记路径是NP难的，并给出了关于顶点覆盖数的FPT算法。此外还研究了最大边数优化和部分绘制的扩展问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典点集嵌入问题要求每个顶点放在给定点集上，本文将其推广为每个顶点有各自的允许点列表（列表嵌入），旨在更一般化的几何限制下研究图的可嵌入性计算复杂性。

Method: 通过归约证明NP-hardness，设计多项式时间算法和FPT算法。针对凸位置点集、一般位置点集、连通图、双标记/三标记图等不同情形，分别给出复杂性分类和算法设计。

Result: (1) 凸位置下匹配的双标记图是NP难的；(2) 连通图若需保持组合嵌入则多项式时间可解，否则有关于最大度的FPT算法；(3) 一般位置下双标记路径是NP难的；(4) 关于顶点覆盖数有FPT算法；(5) 三标记下顶点覆盖数为2时为paraNP-hard；(6) 最大边数优化问题是APX难的；(7) 扩展问题给出参数化复杂性二分结果。

Conclusion: 本文系统刻画了列表点集嵌入问题的计算复杂性边界，回答了Frati等人的公开问题，并为后续研究提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Deciding whether a given graph admits a planar straight-line drawing where each vertex is placed on some point from a given finite point set is known as Point Set Embeddability and is a classical problem in graph drawing. In this paper, we study the more general embeddability question where the placement of each vertex $v$ is restricted to a list $L(v)$ of admissible points. We first study the case where the given point set is in convex position. We show that this case is NP-hard even if the given graph is a matching and bi-labeled, i.e., each vertex has at most 2 admissible points. On the positive side, we present two efficient algorithms for the case where the given graph $G$ is connected (and not necessarily bi-labeled): if $G$ is equipped with a combinatorial embedding that needs to be respected, we can solve the problem in polynomial time; otherwise we can solve it in FPT-time with regard to the maximum vertex degree. In particular, this answers an open question by Frati, Glisse, Lenhart, Liotta, Mchedlidze, and Nishat [GD'13]. We then turn our attention to the more general case where the given point set is not necessarily in convex position. Here, we show NP-hardness for bi-labeled paths; notably these graphs have a unique combinatorial embedding and maximum degree two. We also present an FPT-algorithm with respect to the vertex cover number for the special case of bi-labeled graphs. We complement this latter result by establishing paraNP-hardness in the tri-labeled setting for vertex cover number 2 and polynomial-time solvability for vertex cover number 1 and arbitrary $L$. Finally, we study optimization and extension variants, where we want to maximize the number of edges or extend a partial drawing, respectively. For the former, we show APX-hardness and for the latter, we provide a parameterized complexity dichotomy under natural extension parameters.

</details>


### [153] [Two-Layer Drawings with a Tree on Top: Vertex Splits and Fixed-Parameter Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24220)
*Alexander Firbas,Robert Ganian,Sylvain Meunier,Martin Nöllenburg*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文研究了受辅助树约束的两层二分图顶点分裂问题，提出了两个固定参数算法：一个以分裂数k为参数，另一个以树T的最大度为参数，并基于后者改进了经典无约束版本的最优算法复杂度，实验验证了算法的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统两层二分图绘制以最小化边交叉数为优化目标，但该问题是NP难的，且交叉最小化解仍可能包含大量交叉。最近，通过顶点分裂（即用多个副本替代原顶点并分配邻接关系）来消除所有交叉的方法受到关注。本文研究带辅助树约束的两层顶点分裂问题，其动机来源于人体参考图谱中解剖层次结构的可视化等应用需求。

Method: 本文采用参数化复杂度分析框架。主要方法包括：(1) 以分裂数k为参数的固定参数算法；(2) 以辅助树T的最大度Δ(T)为参数的ETH紧指数级固定参数算法。并基于后者推导出经典无约束版本的ETH紧指数级算法。

Result: (1) 提出了分裂数k的固定参数算法；(2) 提出了以树T最大度为参数的ETH紧单指数固定参数算法；(3) 基于该结果改进了经典无约束版本，将先前O^*(2^{k·log k})算法提升至ETH紧单指数时间；(4) 算法实现并在实际数据中表现良好。

Conclusion: 本文在理论上对带约束的两层顶点分裂问题给出了参数化复杂度下界与上界，提供了两个固定参数算法，其中基于最大度的算法还改进了经典问题的已知结果。实验验证了算法的实际可行性。

Abstract: Two-layer drawings of bipartite graphs place the vertices of each part on one of two parallel lines and draw the edges as straight-line links. Traditionally, the optimization goal is to find vertex permutations on one or both layers that minimize the induced number of edge crossings. This problem is NP-hard, and crossing-minimal solutions may still contain many crossings. Recently, there has been growing interest in an orthogonal optimization goal, namely removing all crossings by vertex splitting, i.e., replacing original vertices by two or more copies and distributing the adjacencies among them. In this paper, we study a natural extension of the two-layer vertex splitting problem in which the vertex order on one layer is constrained by a given auxiliary tree $T$, motivated by applications such as the visualization of anatomical hierarchies in the Human Reference Atlas. We investigate the parameterized complexity of this problem and obtain two main contributions: (1) a fixed-parameter algorithm with respect to the number $k$ of splits, and (2) an ETH-tight single-exponential fixed-parameter algorithm with respect to the maximum degree of $T$. Moreover, we build on the latter result to obtain an ETH-tight single-exponential algorithm for the classical unconstrained version of the problem, improving upon the previous $O^*(2^{k\cdot \log k})$ algorithms. Finally, we also implement our algorithm and show that it performs well in practice.

</details>


### [154] [On the Recognition of Outerplanar Graphs with Queue Number 1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24297)
*Michael A. Bekos,Thomas Depian,Stefan Felsner,Michael Kaufmann,Philipp Kindermann,Fabrizio Montecchiani,Maria Eleni Pavlidi,Alexandra Weinberger,Alexander Wolff,Johannes Zink*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文研究了图的同时具有栈数1和队列数1的刻画与识别问题。主要发现：(i) 判定一个外平面图是否具有队列数1是NP难的；(ii) 但判定一个极大外平面图是否具有队列数1可在线性时间内完成。此外，还探讨了队列数1的外路径与其最大顶点度之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图和同时具有栈数1和队列数1的图类，即同时支持单页栈布局和单页队列布局的图，这类图的结构特性尚不清晰，特别是其识别问题的计算复杂性未知。

Method: 通过计算复杂性分析（NP-hardness证明）和线性时间算法设计，以及图论结构分析（外路径与顶点度的关系）。

Result: (i) 判定外平面图队列数是否为1是NP难的；(ii) 判定极大外平面图队列数是否为1可在线性时间内完成；(iii) 揭示了队列数1的外路径与其最大度之间的约束关系。

Conclusion: 识别同时具有栈数1和队列数1的图在一般外平面图上计算困难，但在极大外平面图上存在高效算法；外路径的度约束进一步刻画了该类图的结构边界。

Abstract: A linear layout of a graph is defined as a total order of the vertices and a partition of the edges to pages. In a stack (queue) layout, no two edges on the same page may cross (nest). The stack (queue) number of a graph is the minimum number of pages required in a stack (queue) layout. This paper focuses on characterizing and recognizing graphs that have both stack number 1 and queue number 1. It is known that the graphs with stack number 1 are exactly the outerplanar graphs. We show that (i) deciding whether a given outerplanar graph has queue number 1 is NP-hard; (ii) deciding whether a given maximal outerplanar graph has queue number 1 can be done in linear time. Moreover, we investigate the interplay between outerpaths with queue number 1 and their maximum vertex degree.

</details>


### [155] [Weighted Book Thickness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24375)
*Henry Förster,Michael Hoffmann,Stephen Kobourov,Maria Eleni Pavlidi,Alexandra Weinberger,Johannes Zink*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文介绍并研究了图的加权书厚度（weighted book thickness），即最小化边出现页面的平均编号，而非传统书厚度中最小化最大页编号。研究发现：不超过9个顶点的所有平面图可用2页嵌入实现加权书厚度，但10个顶点时存在反例；存在2-树无法在2页上实现加权书厚度；路径宽度不超过2的图总能用2页书嵌入在线性时间内实现加权书厚度；判断加权书厚度是否不超过给定k是NP完全的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统书厚度关注最小化边出现的最大页面编号（即最坏情况），而实际应用中可能更关注平均页面编号（即平均情况）。作者引入加权书厚度概念，旨在研究图嵌入中边在页面上分布的平均性能，并探索其与传统书厚度的差异。

Method: 定义加权书厚度为最小化边出现页面的平均编号；通过构造具体图例（如10个顶点的平面图、2-树）证明加权书厚度与传统书厚度的分离性；利用路径宽度为2的图结构给出线性时间构造算法；通过归约证明加权书厚度的判定问题是NP完全的。

Result: 1) 不超过9个顶点的所有平面图均可用2页书嵌入实现其加权书厚度；2) 存在10个顶点的平面图，其加权书厚度的任何实现所需的页数都超过其传统书厚度；3) 存在2-树的加权书厚度无法在2页上实现；4) 路径宽度不超过2的图，加权书厚度总可在2页书嵌入中实现，且可在线性时间内找到；5) 判定加权书厚度是否≤k是NP完全的。

Conclusion: 加权书厚度作为书厚度的平均版本，展现出与传统书厚度本质不同的行为：即使是简单的平面图或2-树，也可能需要更多页面来实现最优平均性能；但对于路径宽度有限的图类，存在高效构造算法；该问题在计算复杂性上是NP难的。

Abstract: We introduce and study the weighted book thickness of graphs. A $k$-page book embedding of a graph $G=(V,E)$ is defined by a spanning cycle $C$ for $V$ (which does not need to be part of $G$) and a partition $E=\bigcup_{i=1}^{k}E_i$ such that $E\cap C\subseteq E_1$ and each graph $G_i=(V,E_i\cup C)$, for $1 \le i \le k$, is outerplane with outer cycle $C$. If $e\in E_i$, we say that $e$ appears on Page $i$. The classical book thickness of a graph $G$ is the minimum $k$ such that there exists a $k$-page book embedding of $G$, that is, the minimum (over all book embeddings of $G$) achievable maximum page an edge appears on. In contrast, the weighted book thickness is the minimum achievable average page an edge appears on. The embeddings that realize weighted book thickness can differ from those that realize (classical) book thickness. We show that, although every planar graph on at most nine vertices admits a 2-page book embedding realizing its weighted book thickness, already for ten vertices, there is a planar graph for which every realization of its weighted book thickness needs more pages than its book thickness. We prove that there even exists a 2-tree whose weighted book thickness cannot be realized on two pages. On the positive side, we show that for every graph of pathwidth at most two, the weighted book thickness can always be realized by a 2-page book embedding and such an embedding can be found in linear time. Moreover, we prove that it is NP-complete to decide if the weighted book thickness is at most $k$, for some given integer $k$.

</details>


### [156] [Denoising 3D images: robustness of persistent homology measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24579)
*Ebru Dagdelen,Aakash Karlekar,Manav Arora,Matthew Illingsworth,Jonathan Jaquette,Linda J. Cummings,Lou Kondic*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文分析了3D多孔介质合成图像在空间不相关噪声影响下的持续同调（PH）表现，比较了多种拓扑度量（瓶颈距离、Wasserstein距离、持续统计量和持续图像）对噪声及去噪过程（高斯卷积与机器学习方法）的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 噪声在计算子/超水平集持续同调时会产生大量短寿命拓扑生成元，阻碍大规模3D图像的PH计算及基于生成元数量的拓扑分析，因此需要评估不同拓扑度量在噪声和去噪条件下的可靠性。

Method: 对合成3D多孔介质图像添加空间不相关高斯噪声，分别采用高斯卷积和机器学习方法进行去噪，然后计算持续同调，并比较瓶颈距离、Wasserstein距离、持续统计量和持续图像等拓扑度量对噪声和去噪过程的敏感程度。

Result: 研究揭示了不同拓扑度量对噪声和去噪过程具有不同鲁棒性，为实际应用中选取合适的拓扑分析工具提供了依据。

Conclusion: 拓扑度量对噪声和去噪过程的敏感度存在显著差异，选择合适度量对于可靠分析噪声环境下的持续同调至关重要。

Abstract: When computing sub/super-level-set persistent homology (PH), the effect of noise may introduce millions of (short-lived) topological generators, presenting an obstacle to both the computation of PH of large 3D images, and any analysis of PH that incorporates the number of generators. As such, it is often necessary to denoise the data before computing its PH.
  We analyze the PH of synthetic 3D images of porous media in the presence of spatially uncorrelated noise, and perform a comparative analysis of various topological measures (e.g. bottleneck distance, Wasserstein distance, persistence statistics and persistence images) to assess their robustness to both noise and the denoising process (i.e. adding spatially uncorrelated Gaussian noise, and denoising by either a Gaussian convolution or a machine learning approach).

</details>


### [157] [Polynomial-time $(k+ε)$-approximation for $k$-coloured Non-crossing Euclidean TSP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24628)
*Daniel Bauer,Jan-Henrik Haunert*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文针对k色点集的欧几里得旅行商问题（k-ETSP），即寻找k条互不相交的闭曲线分别覆盖每种颜色且总欧氏长度最小，提出了一个多项式时间的(k+ε)-近似算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: k-ETSP是经典欧几里得TSP（1-ETSP）的多色推广，具有NP难度，实际应用（如多机器人路径规划、多类布线）需要高效的近似算法。

Method: 提出了一个多项式时间算法，实现了(k+ε)-近似比，即在最优解的基础上，总长度不超过最优解的(k+ε)倍。

Result: 给出了一个多项式时间的(k+ε)-近似算法，这是k-ETSP问题已知的首个常数因子近似算法（近似因子随颜色数k线性增长）。

Conclusion: 该工作为k色非交叉欧几里得TSP提供了多项式时间近似方案，为多色几何旅行商问题的近似算法研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Given a $k$-coloured point set $P\subseteq \mathbb{R}^2$, the $k$-coloured Non-crossing Euclidean Travelling Salesperson Problem (short $k$-ETSP) asks for $k$ non-crossing closed curves, where one curve spans one corresponding colour class, such that the curves are pairwise non-crossing and the sum of their Euclidean lengths is minimised. This problem is NP-hard as $1$-ETSP is the standard Euclidean Travelling Salesperson Problem. We present a polynomial-time $(k+ε)$-approximation for $k$-ETSP.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.PE'></div>

# q-bio.PE [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [Spatial spread of infection: transitions between pulled and pushed fronts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23728)
*Evgeniy Khain,Rohan Sukumar*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文研究了传染病在非稳定健康状态下的空间传播，通过理论分析和数值求解空间SIR偏微分方程，确定了感染传播脉冲为"拉型波"或"推型波"的参数相空间，发现了连续和非连续两种相变类型，并揭示了双稳态区域的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解传染病在空间传播中的前沿推进机制（拉型波 vs 推型波），探究传播速率对感染比例依赖时脉冲传播的相变行为。

Method: 通过数值求解空间SIR偏微分方程与理论分析相结合的方法，研究传播脉冲的前沿推进现象，确定拉型波和推型波的参数相空间。

Result: 发现了拉型波与推型波之间的连续相变和非连续相变（后者中波速出现跳跃）；在相变阈值附近理论与数值结果吻合良好；发现了双稳态区域，其中推型波和拉型波均可实现。

Conclusion: 传染病传播模型中的前沿推进机制存在丰富的相变行为，包括连续相变、非连续相变和双稳态现象，为理解疾病空间传播动力学提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: We consider the spatial spread of epidemic into an unstable, healthy state. When the transmission rate depends on the fraction of infected, the propagating pulse of infection can be either a pulled front or a pushed front. We determined the phase space of parameters for the pulled and pushed regions both by numerically solving the spatial SIR partial differential equations and by a theoretical analysis of the front propagation phenomenon. We found both a continuous and a discontinuous transition between the pulled and pushed front solutions; in the latter transition, the front speed undergoes a jump as a certain parameter crosses the critical threshold. The behavior of the propagating pulses near the transitions has been analyzed and a good agreement between the theory and numerics has been observed. Finally, a bistable region where both pushed and pulled propagating pulses can be realized has been discovered.

</details>


### [159] [Effect of wind on prey-predator dynamics with group defense and additional food strategy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24285)
*Anushree Hazra,Aniket Banerjee,Debaldev Jana*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文建立并分析了一个包含风力驱动环境效应、猎物群体防御和捕食者额外食物策略的捕食-被捕食模型，发现风力强度和额外食物供应可导致稳定性切换、Hopf分岔振荡以及鞍结点和Bogdanov-Takens分岔等复杂动力学行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究风力这一环境因子如何改变猎物的防御策略和捕食效率，以及额外食物供给对捕食者-猎物系统动力学的影响。

Method: 建立包含风力效应、猎物群体防御和额外食物策略的微分方程模型，通过稳定性分析和分岔理论（Hopf分岔、鞍结点分岔、Bogdanov-Takens分岔）研究系统平衡点的存在性及稳定性。

Result: 风力强度和额外食物供给可诱导稳定性切换、振荡动力学及更复杂的分岔行为（鞍结点分岔和Bogdanov-Takens分岔）。

Conclusion: 环境强迫和资源补充共同塑造了捕食者的持续生存和种群波动，为可变环境条件下涉及食物富集的生态管理策略提供了理论见解。

Abstract: Wind plays a crucial role in changing prey defense strategy and predation efficiency. We develop and analyze a prey-predator model that incorporates wind driven environmental effects, prey group defense, and an additional food strategy for the predator. Wind intensity is assumed to modulate predation efficiency, while prey aggregation reduces predation pressure at high densities, and supplementary food alters predator growth independent of prey abundance. We investigate the existence and stability of biologically feasible equilibrium points. The analysis reveals that wind strength and additional food supply can induce stability switches, oscillatory dynamics via Hopf bifurcation, and more complex behaviors including saddle node and Bogdanov Takens bifurcations. Our results demonstrate that environmental forcing and resource supplementation jointly shape predator persistence and population fluctuations, providing theoretical insights into ecological management strategies involving food enrichment under variable environmental conditions.

</details>


### [160] [The role of nestedness and saturating feedback in bipartite ecological systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24328)
*Nirbhay Patil,Ada Altieri*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 该论文提出通过Monod饱和机制解决了广义Lotka-Volterra模型中互惠关系不稳定的悖论，并揭示了网络架构（特别是嵌套性）对生态系统稳定性的真实作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准广义Lotka-Volterra模型中互惠关系会放大无序性并导致无界增长，这与自然界中竞争与合作共存的大规模生态系统稳定性相矛盾，因此需要解决这一悖论。

Method: 采用动态平均场理论和随机矩阵分析，研究Monod型饱和机制对生态系统稳定性的影响；同时分析网络架构（嵌套性）在生态系统稳定性中的作用。

Result: 发现Monod饱和能够扩大系统的稳定相区并提高物种存活率；嵌套性本身并不能提供内在的稳定性优势，而是作为高度连接度分布必要条件下的副产物出现。

Conclusion: Monod饱和机制是解决互惠关系不稳定性的有效途径，而嵌套性对生态稳定性无直接贡献，仅是高度连接度分布的结果。

Abstract: Large ecosystems balance competition and cooperation, yet standard generalized Lotka--Volterra models make mutualism destabilizing by amplifying disorder and driving unbounded growth. We show that Monod-like saturation resolves this paradox: dynamical mean-field theory and random-matrix analysis reveal a broader stable phase and enhanced survival. Network architecture provides a second control mechanism, but nestedness offers no intrinsic stability advantage. Instead, it is a byproduct of degree distributions with high connectivity necessary for stability.

</details>


### [161] [From Local Payoffs to Global Instabilities: A Spectral Cartography of Spatiotemporal Chaos in Canonical 2x2 Evolutionary Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24638)
*Ozgur Aydogmus*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于基序（motif）的框架来分析空间进化博弈中的时空混沌，通过布尔线性化推导出局部基序的不稳定阈值，并结合Derrida斜率与渐近汉明距离构建了四区域相图（有序、瞬态混沌、持续混沌、次临界混沌），揭示了次临界混沌相的存在及其机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间进化博弈的混沌分析缺乏系统性框架，难以准确刻画不同动力学相之间的边界及其微观机制。作者希望建立一种基于局部基序的解析框架，从而在收益平面上完整绘制动力学相图。

Method: 采用"模仿最优策略"规则的布尔线性化方法，推导出入侵者、合作对、条带界面和合作核心等局部基序的解析不稳定阈值。结合Derrida斜率（s）与渐近汉明距离（d∞）两种度量，构建四区域相图。利用布尔雅可比矩阵在均匀背景下建立Derrida斜率与谱半径之间的精确对应关系。

Result: 得到了四类动力学相：有序、瞬态混沌、持续混沌和次临界混沌。次临界混沌相的特点是Derrida斜率s<1但渐近汉明距离d∞>0，即无穷小扰动衰减而有限振幅扰动能够维持混沌。持续混沌区域的边界由少量基序不稳定线在不同初始密度下共同界定。

Conclusion: 基于基序的框架有效揭示了空间进化博弈中时空混沌的微观起源和相结构，次临界混沌的发现为理解有限扰动驱动混沌提供了新视角。该方法为研究其他空间分布动力系统的混沌相变提供了可推广的分析工具。

Abstract: We develop a motif-based framework for spatiotemporal chaos in spatial evolutionary games and use it to map the dynamical phase diagram in the payoff plane. Using Boolean linearization of the imitate-the-best rule, we derive analytical instability thresholds for local motifs including invaders, cooperative pairs, stripe interfaces, and cooperative cores. These thresholds are obtained from payoff balance at contested motif interfaces and recover classical invasion thresholds of spatial evolutionary games, which emerge here as boundaries of the chaotic phase. Combining the Derrida slope with the asymptotic Hamming distance, we obtain a four-region cartography: ordered, transient-chaotic, sustained-chaotic, and subcritical-chaotic dynamics. The phase diagram is organized by density-dependent motif selection: different initial cooperator densities activate different instability mechanisms, yet a small set of motif-instability lines consistently bounds the sustained-chaos region across densities. This cartography reveals a subcritical chaotic phase (Derrida slope $s<1$ but asymptotic Hamming distance $d_\infty>0$), where infinitesimal perturbations decay while finite-amplitude perturbations sustain chaos. The motif-based framework is anchored by an exact benchmark: for homogeneous backgrounds, the Boolean Jacobian yields an exact correspondence between the Derrida slope and spectral radius, linking damage spreading to deterministic instability.

</details>


<div id='stat.AP'></div>

# stat.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [162] [CoLaDAG: Compositional Latent Log-ratio DAG Analysis of the Gut Microbiome under Silver Nanoparticle Exposure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23233)
*Shuyan Chen,Ziliang Shen,Xinlei Wang*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: CoLaDAG是一种基于固定参考点与加性对数比(ALR)估计量的有向网络推断方法，用于从成分性微生物组计数数据中生成稀疏有向条件依赖假设。该方法结合多项观测模型、线性高斯结构方程模型、非凸DC-ADMM优化以及后估计阈值化与贪婪无环投影。模拟显示其马修斯相关系数最优、错误发现率最低，但在连续数据与缺失值误设下性能下降。12小鼠银纳米颗粒案例中，58节点图中60条主边的块重采样选择频率≥0.60，结果仅为探索性坐标特异性假设，而非因果或暴露效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微生物组计数数据存在成分性采样、高维度和有限生物学重复等复杂问题，现有方法难以在成分数据约束下可靠推断有向条件依赖关系。

Method: 提出CoLaDAG框架，包含：(1)固定参考点的加性对数比(ALR)变换处理成分性；(2)多项观测模型与线性高斯结构方程模型；(3)非凸DC-ADMM优化；(4)后估计阈值化与贪婪无环投影。

Result: 模拟实验中获得最大均值精确方向马修斯相关系数和最小均值错误发现率；在12小鼠银纳米颗粒案例中，58节点图60条主边块重采样频率≥0.60，结果对ALR参考选择和块重采样敏感。

Conclusion: CoLaDAG能生成探索性的坐标特异性有向假设，优先识别厌氧肠道分类群作为靶向丰度、代谢物和扰动研究的目标，但不建立交叉喂养或毒理学机制，不能直接解释为因果或暴露效应。

Abstract: Directed network analysis of microbiome counts is complicated by compositional sampling, high dimensionality, and limited biological replication. We present CoLaDAG, a fixed-reference latent additive log-ratio (ALR) estimator for generating sparse directed conditional-dependence hypotheses from compositional counts. The method combines a multinomial observation model, a working linear Gaussian structural equation model, nonconvex DC-ADMM optimization, and post-estimation thresholding with greedy acyclic projection. Under simulations aligned with this observation model, CoLaDAG obtained the largest mean exact-direction Matthews correlation and the smallest mean false discovery rate among the evaluated implementations; performance deteriorated under continuous-data and dropout misspecification. In the 12-mouse silver-nanoparticle (AgNP) case study, the 58-node fitted graph was sensitive to block resampling and ALR reference choice: 60 of 284 primary edges attained a mouse-block selection frequency of at least 0.60. The reported orientations and dose-stratified slopes are exploratory, coordinate-specific hypotheses rather than identified causal or exposure effects. The leading stable relations prioritize anaerobic gut taxa for targeted abundance, metabolite, and perturbation studies, but do not establish cross-feeding or toxicological mechanisms.

</details>


### [163] [Towards Optimal Estimators for Randomized Control Trials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23254)
*Harsh Parikh,Gabriel Levin-Konigsberg,Nilesh Tripuraneni,Dhruv Madeka,Michael I. Jordan,Dean Foster,Dominique Perrault-Joncas,Alexander Volfovsky*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于样本拆分的框架，用于在随机对照试验（RCT）家族中根据分析目标（如推断或决策）系统性地选择最优估计量，并通过亚马逊供应链优化试验和民主挑战数据集验证了不同目标下最优估计量存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准差异均值估计量虽然无偏但精度不足，尤其在处理效应异质或结果呈重尾分布时。现有众多精度增强方法中，没有任何单一估计量能在所有数据集上表现最优。若为单个RCT寻找最优估计量，可能因便利性选择而损害科学有效性，因此需要一种原则性框架来在RCT家族中根据分析目标选择最优估计量。

Method: 提出基于样本拆分的框架，通过拆分样本来估计RCT家族中评估指标（如均方误差、遗憾值）的分布，从而在保持渐近保证的前提下系统比较不同估计量。使用亚马逊供应链优化技术试验和民主挑战数据集（25个干预措施）进行实证演示。

Result: 最优估计量因分析目标而异：加权最小二乘（WLS）在推断目标下表现最佳，而差异均值估计量在决策情境下能最小化遗憾值。

Conclusion: 该框架为不同研究应用中的估计量选择提供了可操作的指导，同时保持了方法论严谨性。

Abstract: Randomized controlled trials (RCTs) are fundamental tools for causal inference across technology companies, pharmaceutical research, and federal agencies. While the standard difference-in-means estimator provides unbiased treatment effect estimates, it often lacks precision, particularly when treatment effects are heterogeneous or outcomes exhibit heavy-tailed distributions. Although numerous precision-enhancing methods exist---from covariate adjustment techniques to variance reduction strategies---recent research demonstrates that no single estimator performs optimally across all datasets. Rather than seeking the best estimator for individual RCTs, which risks compromising scientific validity through convenient selection, we propose a principled framework for identifying optimal estimators within families of RCTs based on specific analytical goals. Our approach uses sample splitting to estimate the distribution of evaluation metrics (e.g., mean squared error, regret) across RCT families, enabling systematic comparisons between estimators while maintaining asymptotic guarantees. We demonstrate this framework using a sample of Amazon's Supply Chain Optimization Technology trials and the Strengthening Democracy Challenge dataset (25 interventions). Results reveal that optimal estimators vary significantly by analytical objective: weighted least squares performs best for inference goals, while difference-in-means minimizes regret for decision-making contexts. This work provides actionable guidance for estimator selection while preserving methodological rigor across diverse research applications.

</details>


### [164] [A framework for classifying and visualising experimental designs when subjects are measured repeatedly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23818)
*Damianos Michaelides,Simon T. Bate*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文旨在澄清实验设计中“重复测量”的概念混淆，通过定义受试者被多次评估时的设计特征，并基于实验单位、重复测量方式和随机化策略对实验设计进行分类和可视化（Hasse图）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在涉及人类或动物的实验（如临床或临床前研究）中，常在受试者上进行多次测量，但研究者对何为“重复测量”存在混淆，需要澄清。

Method: 通过定义受试者多次评估时的设计特征，基于以下三个维度进行划分：受试者是否为实验单位、实验单位是否被重复测量、采用的随机化策略。利用这些特征对实验设计分类，并通过Hasse图进行可视化。

Result: 明确了重复测量的定义边界，建立了基于特征的实验设计分类体系，并提供了Hasse图的可视化表达方式。

Conclusion: 该工作有助于消除研究者对重复测量概念的混淆，为实验设计的选择和正确分析提供指导。

Abstract: When running experiments that involve humans or animals, for example in clinical or pre-clinical research, it is often the case that multiple measurements are taken on the experimental subjects. This may involve measuring subjects repeatedly over time to track any trends, administering a sequence of treatments to compare their effects within-subject, or taking multiple technical replicate samples of each subject to obtain a more reliable average response. However, it appears that there is some confusion as to what constitutes a 'repeated measure' and what does not. This paper clarifies this confusion by defining characteristics a design may possess when the subjects are assessed multiple times. These characteristics are delineated based on whether the subjects are the experimental units, if the experimental units are measured repeatedly or not, and which randomisation strategy is used. Experimental designs can then be classified using these characteristics and visualised using Hasse diagrams.

</details>


### [165] [Memory operator ensembles indicate proximity to criticality in simulated AMOC transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24310)
*Mauricio Herrera-Marín*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于记忆算子的早期预警信号诊断方法，在CESM模拟的AMOC坍塌实验中，该方法比传统物理指标提前77年发出预警，并在多种模拟场景中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统早期预警信号（如自相关和方差增加）在AMOC坍塌的CESM模拟中失效（反而减小），需要开发新的预警方法。

Method: 利用过去数据拟合演化的翻转环流结构的记忆算子，估计其最慢模态，构建算子集合作为预警诊断指标。

Result: 该方法在坍塌前77年发出预警，优于物理淡水输运指标的29年；在9个额外模拟中，对4个有限速率强迫转变提前预警，但对3个噪声诱导的快速坍塌未预警；消融实验表明信号依赖长时间尺度记忆和垂向翻转结构。

Conclusion: 该方法可作为接近临界点的度量而非通用时钟，其有效性依赖于长时间尺度记忆和垂向翻转结构信息。

Abstract: Classical early-warning signals assume that recovery slows near a tipping point, but in a well-studied CESM simulation of AMOC collapse, autocorrelation and variance decrease instead. We develop a preregistered diagnostic that uses only past data to estimate the slowest mode of a memory operator fitted to the evolving overturning structure. The operator ensemble produces an exploratory alarm 77 years before collapse, compared with 29 years for a physics-based freshwater-transport indicator. A blind test on the recovery branch does not validate the same timing rule, supporting interpretation of the diagnostic as a proximity measure rather than a universal clock. In nine additional simulations, it warns before all four finite-rate forced transitions but not before three rapid noise-induced collapses available only as scalar AMOC records. Matched ablations show that the signal depends on long-timescale memory and vertical overturning structure rather than trend, forcing, or multivariate information alone.

</details>


### [166] [Integrating Structure and Attributes for Transportation Network Partitioning via Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24506)
*Ketsia Guichard-Sustowski,Loïc Le Marrec,Véronique Thelen*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于半松弛融合Gromov-Wasserstein散度的灵活框架，用于对具有异构属性的交通网络进行分区，能够显式控制网络结构与属性信息之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常依赖预定义公式来整合交通网络的结构信息和异构操作属性（从标量指标到时间序列），无法灵活控制不同信息源的相对影响。

Method: 基于距离图表示和半松弛融合Gromov-Wasserstein散度的最优传输框架，联合考虑网络结构（通过邻接/距离矩阵）和节点属性，并通过可调超参数显式控制二者的权衡。

Result: 在城市道路网络（面向交通分区）和自行车共享系统（面向使用模式社区识别）两个不同特征的系统上进行了评估，展示了框架根据不同的结构和属性偏好调整分区结果的能力。

Conclusion: 该框架能够灵活地适应不同交通网络的分区需求，通过显式控制结构与属性的权衡，生成符合特定偏好的分区结果。

Abstract: Transportation network partitioning is essential for applications such as traffic analysis, simulation, and mobility pattern identification. However, transportation networks combine structural information with heterogeneous operational attributes, ranging from scalar indicators to temporal profiles. Existing approaches generally rely on predefined formulations to integrate these sources of information, limiting the ability to control their relative influence. This paper proposes a flexible framework for partitioning heterogeneous transportation networks represented as attributed graphs. The proposed methodology relies on a distance-based graph representation and an optimal transport formulation based on the semi-relaxed Fused Gromov-Wasserstein discrepancy, enabling joint consideration of network structure and attributes with explicit control over their trade-off. The proposed methodology is evaluated on two transportation systems with distinct characteristics: an urban road network for traffic-oriented partitioning and a bicycle-sharing system for identifying usage-based communities. Results demonstrate the ability of the framework to adapt the resulting partitions according to different structural and attribute preferences.

</details>


### [167] [Optimum Multiple Sampling Plan Based on the Process Capability Index $C_{py}$ Under Type-II Hybrid Censoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24575)
*Rajat Das,Tanmay Kayal,Yogesh Mani Tripathi,Tanmay Sen*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种阶段独立多重抽样计划（SIMSP），通过消除各抽样阶段对前次检验结果的依赖，在Type-II混合删失方案下基于过程能力指数Cpy设计，显著减少样本量并提升检验效率，同时通过最小化总成本优化约束问题，满足制造商与消费者风险要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多重抽样计划（MSP）各阶段依赖于前次检验结果，导致样本效率较低；且现有方法在非可修复产品按比例保修政策下的应用缺乏灵活性。本文旨在通过消除阶段间依赖关系来减少样本量，提高检验经济性。

Method: 1) 提出阶段独立多重抽样计划（SIMSP），各阶段独立进行无需依赖前次结果；2) 基于广义过程能力指数Cpy设计，适用于非可修复产品的按比例保修政策；3) 采用Type-II混合删失方案（Type-II HCS）以灵活控制试验时间和失效信息；4) 利用过程能力指数估计的渐近分布计算操作特征（OC）函数，并获取精确Fisher信息矩阵（FIM）；5) 构建约束优化问题，以最小化总成本为目标，满足制造商和消费者的风险要求。

Result: 数值研究结果表明，所提出的SIMSP方法在满足可容忍风险要求的前提下，提供了一种经济高效且可行的批次验收方案，显著降低了抽样成本。

Conclusion: SIMSP通过阶段独立设计有效提升了检验效率，在按比例保修政策下为产品验收提供了经济可行的解决方案，具有良好的实际应用价值。

Abstract: This paper proposes a stage independent multiple sampling plan (SIMSP) to improve inspection efficiency by reducing the number of samples required at each sampling stage. Unlike conventional multiple sampling plans (MSP), the proposed SIMSP eliminates the dependence of each sampling stage on the outcome of the preceding inspection. The proposed approach is developed for non-repairable products sold under a pro-rata warranty policy based on the generalized process capability index $C_{py}$. The SIMSP is designed under a Type-II hybrid censoring scheme (Type-II HCS), which provides greater flexibility in controlling test time and failure information in life testing experiments. The asymptotic distribution of the process capability index estimate is used to compute the operating characteristic (OC) function, and the exact Fisher information matrix (FIM) is obtained for further statistical analysis. A constraint optimization problem is formulated to determine the optimal design by minimizing the total cost subject to the manufacturer's and consumer's risk. Numerical investigations are conducted to examine the effects of model parameters, warranty policy characteristics, and cost factors on the optimal solution. The results demonstrate that the proposed approach provides an economically efficient and feasible method for lot acceptance while satisfying the tolerable risks requirements.

</details>

### [168] [Charge-dependent atmospheric muon flux at 17 GV geomagnetic cutoff measured with the mini-ICAL prototype](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23330)
*S. Pethuraj,Raj Shah,G. Majumder,J. M. John*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 印度中微子观测站（INO）的85吨级mini-ICAL原型探测器（2018-2022运行）在靠近地磁赤道（垂直截止刚度17 GV）处测量了1-5 GeV/c的μ⁻和μ⁺微分通量，结果低于低截止刚度站点数据，验证了高纬度对低/中刚度初级宇宙射线的抑制效应，并与CORSIKA模拟中不同强子相互作用模型的预测进行了对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为INO的ICAL探测器开展研发工作，通过建造缩小比例的原型探测器（mini-ICAL）来验证探测器性能、重建算法和模拟框架，并测量靠近地磁赤道处电荷依赖的宇宙线缪子谱，以研究地磁截止刚度对低能宇宙射线成分的调制作用。

Method: 建造了85吨重的mini-ICAL原型探测器（约为全尺寸ICAL的1/600），在印度Madurai（地磁纬度1.44°N，垂直截止刚度17 GV）运行4年，利用该装置测量电荷依赖的缪子微分通量，并与CORSIKA模拟中不同强子相互作用模型的预测进行比较。

Result: 获得了1-5 GeV/c动量范围内μ⁻和μ⁺的微分通量谱，观测到测量值系统地低于低地磁截止刚度站点的结果，证实了17 GV高截止刚度对低、中刚度初级宇宙射线的显著抑制。

Conclusion: mini-ICAL探测器成功实现了在赤道附近对宇宙线缪子谱的电荷分辨测量，验证了ICAL探测器的技术方案与模拟工具，高截止刚度效应得到实验确认。

Abstract: The Iron CALorimeter (ICAL) detector at the India-Based Neutrino Observatory (INO) was conceived as an underground experiment designed to measure atmospheric neutrino oscillation parameters. As part of the R\&D programme, a scaled prototype (mini-ICAL), 85\,ton, approximately 1/600$^{\mathrm{th}}$ the mass of the full detector, was constructed at the IICHEP Transit Campus, Madurai (altitude 150\,m; latitude 9.9372$^\circ$\,N; longitude 78.013$^\circ$\,E; geomagnetic latitude 1.44$^\circ$\,N; vertical cutoff rigidity 17\,GV) and operated between 2018 and 2022. The prototype enabled measurements of charge-dependent cosmic muon spectra in the vicinity of the geomagnetic equator and provided an important validation of detector performance, reconstruction algorithms, and simulation frameworks for the ICAL experiment. Differential fluxes of $μ^{-}$ and $μ^{+}$ were measured over the momentum range $\sim$\,1--5\,GeV/c. The obtained momentum spectra are systematically lower than those reported at sites with smaller geomagnetic cutoff rigidities, consistent with the suppression of low- and intermediate-rigidity primary cosmic rays at the 17\,GV cutoff. The measurements are compared with predictions from different hadronic interaction models available in CORSIKA simulations.

</details>


### [169] [Off-shell Higgs boson measurements: Yukawa couplings, self-coupling, compositeness, and width](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23352)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文基于CMS实验138 fb⁻¹的13 TeV质子-质子对撞数据，通过H→ZZ→4ℓ离壳过程对希格斯玻色子进行测量。首次直接检验复合希格斯模型，设复合尺度Λ_H下限870 GeV（95%置信水平）；首次在离壳区域约束希格斯自耦合；结合离壳与在壳测量给出迄今最严格的轻夸克汤川耦合约束；联合H→ZZ与PH→WW通道分析给出Γ_H = 5.1⁺².⁰₋₁.₈ MeV，并以超过5σ置信水平排除无非壳希格斯产生的情景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索希格斯玻色子的离壳产生过程，以测试超出标准模型的新物理，包括复合希格斯模型、有效场论框架下的胶子聚变产生、希格斯自耦合、轻夸克汤川耦合以及希格斯宽度等关键参数和模型。

Method: 利用CMS实验收集的138 fb⁻¹质子-质子对撞数据，通过H→ZZ→4ℓ离壳衰变道进行分析。采用标准模型有效场论框架测试胶子聚变产生过程；结合离壳与在壳测量进行联合分析；并联合H→ZZ和PH→WW两个通道以提升对希格斯宽度的灵敏度。

Result: 复合希格斯模型复合尺度Λ_H下限870 GeV（95%置信水平）；首次获得离壳区域希格斯自耦合约束；给出迄今最严格的轻夸克汤川耦合约束；希格斯宽度Γ_H = 5.1⁺².⁰₋₁.₈ MeV；无非壳希格斯产生情景被排除，置信水平超过5σ。

Conclusion: 本研究通过离壳希格斯玻色子产生过程，对复合希格斯模型、希格斯自耦合、轻夸克汤川耦合及希格斯宽度等关键物理量给出了迄今最强的实验约束，显著推进了对希格斯玻色子性质及其背后新物理的理解。

Abstract: Measurements of Higgs boson production in the off-shell region are presented, using the four-lepton decay channel. Data from proton-proton collisions at the CERN LHC, collected by the CMS experiment and corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$ at a center-of-mass energy of 13 TeV, are utilized. The first direct test of composite Higgs boson models is performed, with a lower limit on the compositeness scale $Λ_\mathrm{H}$ set at 870 GeV at the 95% confidence level. Tests of gluon-fusion production within the standard model effective field theory framework are performed. The first constraint on the Higgs boson self-coupling in the off-shell region is obtained. By combining on- and off-shell measurements, the analysis sets the tightest constraints to date on light-quark Yukawa couplings, while relaxing assumptions such as the bound on the Higgs boson coupling to vector bosons, thereby providing more model-independent results. Constraints on the Higgs boson width are provided while accounting for a range of beyond-the-standard-model effects, including both light and heavy particles in the gluon-fusion production loop, as well as modified couplings to vector bosons. A combined analysis of the H$\to$ZZ and PH$\to$WW channels is performed to improve sensitivity to the Higgs boson width, yielding $Γ_\mathrm{H}$ = 5.1$^{+2.0}_{-1.8}$ MeV. The scenario of no off-shell Higgs boson production is excluded at a confidence level exceeding 5 standard deviations.

</details>


### [170] [Predict before you train: Scaling Laws for particle physics foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23377)
*Jan-Lucas Uslu,Benjamin Nachman,Christopher Re*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文研究了粒子物理中大型Transformer模型的缩放定律，发现通过在小模型上拟合联合模型-数据缩放定律，可以预测训练计算量超百倍的大模型损失，误差在1%以内；并将预训练损失与下游物理性能（标记任务）联系起来，实现从计算预算到物理性能的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粒子物理中最大的机器学习模型训练成本极高，但在投入计算资源之前无法估计给定架构的扩展收益，因此需要一种能够预测模型性能的缩放定律。

Method: 对预训练于对撞机喷注的通用Transformer，在小模型（跨越三个数量级的训练计算量）上拟合联合模型-数据缩放定律，预测训练计算量超百倍的大模型损失；并将预训练损失与下游微调性能关联。

Result: 预测的大模型损失误差在1%以内；更低预训练损失带来更低的微调损失和更高的本底抑制；最终模型在准确率、AUC和夸克/胶子鉴别上与当前最先进的物理感知基础模型一致，仅在top标记的高纯度尾部有微小差距。

Conclusion: 在该模型家族和任务中，可以在训练任何大模型之前将计算预算转化为预期的物理性能，实现了计算资源与物理性能之间的可预测映射。

Abstract: The largest machine learning models in particle physics are also the most expensive to train, yet the return on scaling a given architecture cannot be estimated before that compute is spent. Scaling laws have been fit for jets, but none has yet been shown to predict the performance of models it was not fit on. We show that, for a generic transformer pretrained on collider jets, it can be forecast. Fitting a joint model-and-data scaling law on small models alone, spanning three orders of magnitude of training compute, we predict the loss of models trained afterward with more than one hundred times more compute to within one percent. We then connect the forecast to downstream physics performance: across two standard tagging benchmarks, lower pretraining loss yields systematically lower fine-tuning loss and higher background rejection after fine-tuning. Within this model family and these tasks, a compute budget can therefore be translated into expected physics performance before any large model is trained. The final frontier model is consistent with the published numbers for current state-of-the-art physics-aware foundation models trained on the same corpus, on accuracy, AUC, and quark/gluon rejection, with a residual edge for the physics-aware model only in the high-purity tail of top tagging. We release five pretrained models spanning multiple sizes, together with the complete training recipe and code.

</details>

Zhou,Y. Z. Zhou,A. N. Zhu,J. Zhu,K. Zhu,K. J. Zhu,K. S. Zhu,L. X. Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: BESIII利用20.3 fb⁻¹的e⁺e⁻对撞数据，以最高精度测量了D⁰→π⁻ℓ⁺ν和D⁺→π⁰ℓ⁺ν（ℓ=e,μ）的分支比、微分衰变率及前后不对称性，通过联合拟合得到f⁺^{D→π}(0)|V_cd|，并从中提取了|V_cd|和形状因子，同时检验了轻子味普适性并搜索了标量流贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测量D介子半轻衰变以提取CKM矩阵元|V_cd|和形状因子，检验轻子味普适性，并搜索超出标准模型的新物理信号（标量流贡献）。

Method: 基于BESIII探测器收集的20.3 fb⁻¹ e⁺e⁻对撞数据（质心能量3.773 GeV），利用标记方法测量D⁰→π⁻ℓ⁺ν和D⁺→π⁰ℓ⁺ν（ℓ=e,μ）的分支比、微分衰变率和前后不对称性，并对四个衰变道进行联合拟合提取f⁺^{D→π}(0)|V_cd|。

Result: 测得分支比分别为(2.950±0.017±0.017)×10⁻³、(2.817±0.037±0.019)×10⁻³、(3.622±0.034±0.018)×10⁻³和(3.507±0.043±0.026)×10⁻³。联合拟合得f⁺(0)|V_cd|=0.1425±0.0005±0.0003。结合标准模型全局拟合和格点QCD计算，得|V_cd|=0.2262±0.0008±0.0005±0.0018和f⁺(0)=0.6339±0.0024±0.0014。轻子味普适性检验未发现明显偏离，标量流耦合参数Re(C_S^μ)=0.022±0.023±0.003。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了目前最优精度的D→π半轻衰变测量结果，未发现轻子味普适性破坏或标量流新物理信号，进一步验证了标准模型。

Abstract: The branching fractions of $D^0\to π^-e^+ν_e$, $D^0\to π^-μ^+ν_μ$, $D^+\to π^0e^+ν_e$, and $D^+\to π^0μ^+ν_μ$ are measured to be $(2.950\pm0.017_{\rm stat.}\pm 0.017_{\rm syst.})\times10^{-3}$, $(2.817\pm0.037_{\rm stat.}\pm 0.019_{\rm syst.})\times10^{-3}$, $(3.622\pm0.034_{\rm stat.}\pm 0.018_{\rm syst.})\times10^{-3}$, and $(3.507\pm0.043_{\rm stat.}\pm 0.026_{\rm syst.})\times10^{-3}$ using $e^+e^-$ collision data with an integrated luminosity of 20.3 fb$^{-1}$ collected at the center-of-mass energy of 3.773 GeV with the BESIII detector. The partial decay rates of these four decays are measured with the best precision to date and their forward-backward asymmetries are determined for the first time. By performing a simultaneous fit to these results, the product of the hadronic transition form factor $f^{D\toπ}_+(0)$ and the modulus of the $c\to d$ Cabibbo-Kobayashi-Maskawa matrix element $|V_{cd}|$ is given by $f^{D\toπ}_+(0)|V_{cd}|=0.1425\pm0.0005_{\rm stat.}\pm0.0003_{\rm syst.}$. Taking the $|V_{cd}|$ provided by the standard model global fit and the $f^{D\toπ}_+(0)$ calculated from the lattice quantum chromodynamics as input, we obtain $f^{D\toπ}_+(0)=0.6339\pm0.0024_{\rm stat.}\pm0.0014_{\rm syst.}$ and $|V_{cd}|=0.2262\pm0.0008_{\rm stat.}\pm0.0005_{\rm syst.}\pm0.0018_{\rm LQCD.}$, respectively. The reported results have the best precision to date. We also search for the scalar current contribution in the $c\to d \ell^+ν_{\ell}$ transition and determine Re$(C_S^μ)=$ $0.022 \pm 0.023_{\rm stat.}\pm 0.003_{\rm syst.}$ and $|{\rm Im}(C_S^μ)|=0.000 \pm $ $0.038_{\rm stat.} \pm 0.012_{\rm syst.}$. In addition, the lepton flavor universality is tested with the ratios of the decay rates between semimuonic and semielectronic decays in full and several $\ell^+ν_\ell$ four-momentum transfer ranges.

</details>


### [172] [Precision Measurement of Decay Dynamics in $D^{0(+)}\to π^{-(0)}\ell^+ν_\ell$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23949)
