Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 利用BESIII探测器在3.773 GeV能量下采集的20.3 fb⁻¹数据，精确测量了D⁰→π⁻e⁺νₑ、D⁰→π⁻μ⁺ν_μ、D⁺→π⁰e⁺νₑ和D⁺→π⁰μ⁺ν_μ的分支比，未发现轻子味普适性破坏，并通过联合拟合以前所未有的精度测得了强子跃迁形状因子与CKM矩阵元|V_cd|的乘积，以及标量流贡献的实部和虚部。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测量粲介子半轻衰变，检验轻子味普适性，并精确确定强子形状因子与CKM矩阵元|V_cd|。

Method: 基于BESIII探测器采集的20.3 fb⁻¹ e⁺e⁻对撞数据（质心能量3.773 GeV），测量D⁰→π⁻ℓ⁺ν_ℓ和D⁺→π⁰ℓ⁺ν_ℓ（ℓ=e,μ）的分支比；通过不同四动量转移区间的衰变宽度比检验轻子味普适性；联合拟合精确测量的分宽度和前向-后向不对称性，提取f⁺(0)|V_cd|、f⁺(0)及|V_cd|，并首次测量标量流贡献C_S^μ的实部和虚部。

Result: 轻子味普适性在当前数据中未发现破坏；f⁺^{D→π}(0)|V_cd| = 0.1425 ± 0.0005(stat.) ± 0.0003(syst.)，精度比此前最好测量提高2-3倍；|V_cd| = 0.2262 ± 0.0008(stat.) ± 0.0005(syst.) ± 0.0018(LQCD)；首次测得Re(C_S^μ) = 0.022 ± 0.023(stat.) ± 0.003(syst.)，|Im(C_S^μ)| = 0.000 ± 0.038(stat.) ± 0.012(syst.)。

Conclusion: 该工作以2-3倍于此前最好测量的精度确定了f⁺(0)|V_cd|、f⁺(0)和|V_cd|，并首次测量了标量流贡献，为检验标准模型和寻找新物理提供了重要约束。

Abstract: The branching fractions of $D^0\to π^-e^+ν_e$, $D^0\to π^-μ^+ν_μ$, $D^+\to π^0e^+ν_e$, and $D^+\to π^0μ^+ν_μ$ are precisely measured, using 20.3 fb$^{-1}$ of $e^+e^-$ collision data collected at the center-of-mass energy of 3.773 GeV with the BESIII detector. The ratios of the decay widths between muon and positron channels are examined in full, across several four-momentum transfer ranges of $\ell^+ν_{\ell}$. No lepton flavor universality violation is found in the current data. From a simultaneous fit to the precisely measured partial decay rates and the first measured forward-backward asymmetries of these four decays, the product of the hadronic transition form factor, $f^{D\toπ}_+(0)$, and the modulus of the $c\to d$ quark mixing element, $|V_{cd}|$, is measured with unprecedented precision to be $f^{D\toπ}_+(0)|V_{cd}|=0.1425\pm0.0005_{\rm stat.}\pm0.0003_{\rm syst.}$. Taking the value of $|V_{cd}|$ from the standard model global fit and $f^{D\toπ}_+(0)$ derived by the lattice quantum chromodynamics calculation as input, we obtain $f^{D\toπ}_+(0)=0.1425\pm0.0005_{\rm stat.}\pm0.0003_{\rm syst.}$ and $|V_{cd}|=0.2262\pm0.0008_{\rm stat.}\pm0.0005_{\rm syst.}\pm0.0018_{\rm LQCD.}$, respectively. The precision of each result is a factor of 2-3 better than the previous best measurements. Additionally, the real and imaginary parts of the scalar current contribution in the $c\to d \ell^+ν_{\ell}$ transition are measured for the first time to be Re $(C_S^μ)=$ $0.022 \pm 0.023_{\rm stat.}\pm 0.003_{\rm syst.}$ and $|\mathrm{Im} (C_S^μ)|=0.000 \pm 0.038_{\rm stat.}\pm 0.012_{\rm syst.}$.

</details>


### [173] [The mathematical theory of photomultiplier tube calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24250)
*L. N. Kalousis,E. A. Dris,A. Vynias*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文系统介绍了光电倍增管（PMT）校准的数学理论，从基本原理到数值方法，涵盖单光电子响应建模、增益校准技术及软电荷成分影响，并提供了理论推导、蒙特卡洛验证和实际PMT数据分析，旨在为学生和年轻研究者提供入门参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为PMT校准提供一套清晰、教学化的数学理论框架，帮助初学者理解PMT校准中的核心概念和步骤，填补现有文献中缺乏系统性、易理解性介绍的空白。

Method: 通过理论推导建立PMT工作流程（光电转换、聚焦、倍增等）的数学模型；采用伽马函数模型描述单光电子（SPE）响应并计算电荷放大函数S_R(x)；利用玩具蒙特卡洛数据测试增益校准方法的精度；对Hamamatsu R1408 PMT的真实数据进行分析。

Result: 展示了基于伽马函数SPE响应模型的S_R(x)曲线及其特征；评估了不同增益校准技术的精度；发现软电荷成分的存在会显著影响增益测定结果；真实PMT数据分析验证了理论方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为PMT校准提供了系统性的数学理论参考，强调软电荷成分对增益校准的影响，并对大型探测器原位校准提出一般性建议，可作为PMT校准领域的入门教材。

Abstract: In this technical note we describe the main features of the mathematical theory of photomultiplier tube (PMT) calibration. Attention was paid to explain the various arguments and concepts in a simple and pedagogical manner that everybody understands. The basic operational principles of a PMT are discussed from a theoretical standpoint. The essential steps of its function (photoconversion, focusing, multiplication, etc.) are laid down together with the mathematical schemes necessary to model the charge output of a PMT when illuminated by a faint poissonian light source. In case of important omissions we direct the reader to some of the standard references. The most common numerical methods, used to calculate the charge amplification function $S_R(x)$, are also presented. As an example, we plot $S_R(x)$ utilizing a gamma function model for the single photoelectron (SPE) response and showcase its main characteristics. The basic techniques for gain calibration are also introduced, and we probed their precision using toy Monte Carlo data. Additionally, data from a Hamamatsu R1408 PMT were analyzed. Finally, we show how the presence of soft charge component can affect the results of gain determination. We conclude this report with some general comments regarding \emph{in situ} calibration of large-scale detectors. We hope that this document can serve as a reference for students and young researchers that want to learn more about the theory of PMT calibration.

</details>


### [174] [Static compilation of Julia packages for integration with existing HEP codebases: a case study with JetReconstruction.jl](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24321)
*Mateusz Jakub Fila,Graeme A Stewart*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文研究了将Julia代码静态编译后集成到C++/Python主导的高能物理(HEP)计算生态系统中的可行性，以JetReconstruction.jl包为例，比较了两种静态编译后端（PackageCompiler.jl与Julia v1.12原生静态编译）的性能，并与原生Julia代码和C++ FastJet进行了对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Julia在HEP计算领域具有潜力，但现有HEP代码库以C++和Python为主。为了让Julia代码能够被现有HEP代码库调用（而非仅从Julia调用C++），需要实现从Julia到C++/Python的反向互操作性。随着Julia静态编译能力的成熟，这一目标变得可行，因此本文旨在探索并验证这一方向。

Method: 以JetReconstruction.jl包为案例，使用两种静态编译后端（PackageCompiler.jl和Julia v1.12新增的原生静态编译功能）将其编译为静态库，然后与C++代码链接调用。对比这两种后端的编译和运行性能，并与原生Julia执行和C++ FastJet库进行基准测试。

Result: 提供了静态编译JetReconstruction.jl后的性能数据，展示了两种编译后端的对比结果，以及与原生Julia代码和C++ FastJet的性能比较。

Conclusion: Julia静态编译能力使得Julia代码可以被C++/Python HEP代码库调用，为Julia在HEP生态中的渐进式采用提供了可行路径，其中Julia v1.12的原生静态编译特性是重要的技术进展。

Abstract: The Julia programming language is considered a strong contender as a future language for high-energy physics (HEP) computing. However, transitioning to the Julia ecosystem will be a long process and interoperability between Julia and C++ is required. So far several successful attempts have been made to wrap HEP C++ packages for use in Julia. It is also important to explore the reverse direction, allowing Julia code to be called from existing HEP codebases, written primarily in C++ and Python, which would significantly improve potential adoption of Julia code. With recent developments in Julia enabling it to produce statically compiled code, this approach is becoming increasingly feasible, and investigating this potential for the benefit of HEP community is the focus of this work.
  This work presents a case study of statically compiling the JetReconstruction.jl package - a highly performant, native Julia implementation of sequential jet reconstruction algorithms. Two different backends for Julia code compilation are compared: the existing PackageCompiler.jl and the new static compilation feature of Julia language, which is one of the major improvements in the Julia v1.12 release. The performance of the statically compiled JetReconstruction.jl is compared with both native Julia code and C++ FastJet.

</details>


### [175] [Dark Matter Searches with Sodium Iodide Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24448)
*Maria Martinez,Reina H. Maruyama,Maria Luisa Sarsa*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文综述了掺铊碘化钠探测器在暗物质研究中的作用，重点介绍了DAMA/NaI和DAMA/LIBRA实验报告的年度调制信号（迄今未被其他实验复现），以及为解决这一异常而开展的新型NaI实验的最新进展与前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质身份识别是当代物理学最具挑战性的问题之一，而掺铊碘化钠探测器在其中发挥了关键作用。本文旨在系统梳理NaI探测器在暗物质研究中的科学背景与现状。

Method: 以综述形式展开：先描述NaI探测器适合暗物质探测的特性，再回顾DAMA/NaI和DAMA/LIBRA实验的历史与结果（年度调制信号），随后介绍为验证该信号而启动的新型NaI实验的最新成果。

Result: DAMA/NaI和DAMA/LIBRA实验观测到与暗物质相互作用一致的年度调制信号，但该信号至今未被其他实验复现；近期其他NaI实验已取得初步结果。

Conclusion: 总结了当前和未来基于NaI的暗物质搜寻实验的现状与前景，强调该领域仍处于解决DAMA异常的关键阶段。

Abstract: The search for the identity of dark matter remains one of the most intriguing challenges in physics today, driving innovative advances in experiment and theory. Thallium-doped sodium iodide (NaI) detectors have played a pivotal role in this context. This review outlines the scientific landscape surrounding NaI detectors, emphasizing their role in the broader context of dark matter research. We begin with a brief description of characteristics that make these detectors well-suited for dark matter detection. We then provide a historical overview and explore the results from the DAMA/NaI and DAMA/LIBRA experiments, which have reported annual modulation signals consistent with dark matter interactions--a signal that to date no other experiment has replicated. We discuss recent results from NaI-based experiments launched, in part, to resolve this tantalizing anomaly. We conclude with a summary of the status and prospects for current and future NaI-based dark matter searches.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.HE'></div>

# astro-ph.HE [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [Constraining cosmological parameters from very-high-energy $γ$-ray attenuation in blazar spectra: Bayesian inference and systematic uncertainties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23095)
*Patryk Liniewicz,Emmanuel Moulin*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用活动星系核发射的甚高能γ射线与河外背景光（EBL）的成对湮灭作用，发展了一套贝叶斯推断框架，通过241条来自H.E.S.S.、MAGIC和VERITAS的AGN能谱数据，测量了EBL归一化与哈勃常数约化值之比r=1.723^{+0.096}_{-0.095}，并由此约束哈勃常数H0，结果表明测量精度主要受EBL归一化不确定性限制而非统计误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用γ射线在河外背景光（EBL）上的成对湮灭作为宇宙膨胀的探针，发展一种不依赖传统距离阶梯的、独立测量哈勃常数H0的方法，以解决当前宇宙学中H0测量的张力和系统误差问题。

Method: 构建贝叶斯推断分析框架，采用观测驱动的红移依赖EBL模型，将源内秉能谱锚定到Fermi-LAT 3FHL星表，同时边缘化能谱形状偏差和数据集归一化差异；方法在模拟观测上验证后，应用于STeVECat编录的来自H.E.S.S.、MAGIC和VERITAS的241条AGN能谱（共50个源）。

Result: 测量得EBL归一化与约化哈勃常数之比r=1.723^{+0.096}_{-0.095}；固定EBL尺度时H0=58.3^{+3.4}_{-3.0} km/s/Mpc；考虑18% EBL尺度不确定性时H0=60.5^{+10.8}_{-10.0} km/s/Mpc；注入测试显示无显著偏差，除大红外背景误模外，内秉谱和EBL形状扰动影响次优；ΩM约束较弱。

Conclusion: γ射线不透明度测量主要受EBL归一化不确定性限制而非统计误差；若能将其不确定性降低至百分之几水平，该方法将成为与距离阶梯无关、具有竞争力的H0测量探针。

Abstract: Very-high-energy $γ$ rays from active galactic nuclei are attenuated through pair production on the extragalactic background light (EBL), providing a probe of cosmic expansion. We develop a Bayesian inference analysis framework that uses an observationally driven, redshift-dependent EBL model and anchors intrinsic source spectra to the Fermi-LAT 3FHL catalogue while marginalising spectral-shape departures and dataset normalisations. The method is validated on realistic mock observations and applied to 241 archival H.E.S.S., MAGIC and VERITAS spectra of 50 AGN compiled in STeVECat. Because the leading opacity depends on the ratio of the EBL normalisation to the reduced Hubble constant, the gamma-ray data primarily constrain this ratio rather than $H_0$ alone. We measure $r=1.723^{+0.096}_{-0.095}$. For a fixed EBL scale, this gives $H_0=58.3^{+3.4}_{-3.0},\mathrm{km,s^{-1},Mpc^{-1}}$; allowing an 18\% EBL-scale uncertainty gives $H_0=60.5^{+10.8}_{-10.0},\mathrm{km,s^{-1},Mpc^{-1}}$. Matched injections recover the input opacity without appreciable bias, and the tested intrinsic-spectrum and EBL-shape perturbations are subdominant except for large infrared-background mismodelling. The predominantly low-redshift sample leaves $Ω\mathrm{M}$ weakly constrained. The measurement is therefore limited by the EBL normalisation rather than statistics; reducing its uncertainty to a few per cent would make gamma-ray opacity a competitive, distance-ladder-independent probe of $H_0$.

</details>


### [177] [Diverse Morphologies of GRB X-Ray Plateaus within a Common Magnetar Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23114)
*Xiao-Fei Dong,Yong-Feng Huang,Nurimangul Nurmamat,Chen Deng,Ze-Cheng Zou,Fan Xu,Abdusattar Kurban,Chen Du,Chen-Ran Hu,Jin-Jun Geng*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文对185个长伽马射线暴（GRB）的X射线平台相进行了首次层级化群体推断，在条件泊松点过程框架下拟合磁星参数。结果表明，观测到的平台群体可由物理上合理的磁星群体很好地重现，不同平台形态（上升、平坦、衰减）的子类之间没有显著的统计分离，但所有子类都表现出较大的固有光度弥散（~0.5-1.0 dex）而持续时间弥散较小。这为GRB X射线平台的磁星解释提供了群体层面的检验，表明平台形态的多样性不需要不同的磁星群体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伽马射线暴X射线平台相的起源尚未解决，特别是不同时间形态（上升、平坦、衰减）的平台是否源于同一物理机制尚不清楚。虽然磁星能量注入是主流解释，但以往研究仅在单个暴发层面推断磁星参数，未在群体层面进行检验。

Method: 采用层级化群体推断方法，在条件泊松点过程框架下，对185个具有X射线平台的长GRB均匀样本进行磁星参数拟合，首次在群体层面系统分析磁星参数分布。

Result: 物理上合理的磁星群体可很好地重现观测的平台群体；不同平台形态子类之间在推断参数分布上未显示强统计分离；所有子类均表现出较大的固有光度弥散（~0.5-1.0 dex），而固有持续时间弥散显著较小。

Conclusion: 该结果为GRB X射线平台的磁星解释提供了群体层面的检验证据，表明观测到的平台形态多样性并不需要假设不同的磁星群体，支持统一的磁星能量注入机制。

Abstract: The origin of the X-ray plateau phase in gamma-ray bursts (GRBs) remains an open problem. In particular, it is unclear whether GRBs with different temporal morphologies (i.e., with a rising, flat, or decaying plateau) arise from a common underlying mechanism. Although magnetar energy injection is a leading explanation, previous studies have primarily inferred magnetar properties on a burst-by-burst basis and have not tested the model at the population level. Here we perform the first hierarchical population inference of magnetar parameters for a uniform sample of 185 long GRBs with X-ray plateaus within a conditional Poisson point-process framework. It is found that the observed plateau population is well reproduced by physically plausible magnetar populations. The inferred parameter distributions show no strong statistical separation among subclasses with different plateau morphologies. Nevertheless, all subclasses show a substantial intrinsic luminosity scatter, $σ_{L,\rm int}\sim0.5$--1.0 dex, whereas the intrinsic duration scatter remains considerably smaller. The results provide a population-level test of the magnetar interpretation of GRB X-ray plateaus, showing that the observed diversity of plateau morphologies does not require distinct magnetar populations.

</details>


### [178] [Dust Without Na I D Trace: The Case of Highly Attenuated Type Ib SN 2024vjc](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23690)
*Javier Silva-Farfán,Keiichi Maeda,Andrea Pastorello,Hanindyo Kuncarayakti,Takashi Nagao,Giorgio Valerin,Andrea Reguitti,Dino Pierluigi Fugazza,Giuliano Pignata,Jesper Sollerman,Chris Ashall,Tomás E. Müller-Bravo,Joseph P. Anderson,Claudia P. Gutiérrez,Priscila J. Pessi,Erkki Kankare,Niko Pyykkinen,Mariusz Gromadzki,Ting-Wan Chen,Thomas M. Reynolds,Irene Salmaso,Avishay Gal-Yam*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: SN 2024vjc是一个弱Na I D吸收但实际消光严重的Ib型超新星，挑战了"弱Na I D吸收=低消光"的常规假设，强调需采用多种独立消光诊断方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 河外暂现源的前向消光估计对恢复其内在性质和探测寄主星系局域环境至关重要，而Na I D吸收等值宽度是广泛使用的诊断工具。本研究旨在检验这一诊断方法的普适性。

Method: 对SN 2024vjc进行了从爆发后约130天的早期光学和近红外时序观测；使用多种超新星消光诊断方法（内在颜色模板、颜色曲线演化、光谱去红）以及巴尔默减缩进行消光估计；结合晚期光谱中的窄发射线探测分析Na I D吸收弱的原因。

Result: SN 2024vjc的Na I D吸收很弱，若据此推断寄主消光仅E(B-V)~0.18 mag，则SN为异常暗弱红色Ib型；但多种SN诊断方法和巴尔默减缩均显示实际消光为E(B-V)~0.45-0.8 mag。弱Na I D吸收可能源于爆炸位点年轻H II区域的强辐射场对中性钠的光电离，晚期光谱中探测到的窄Hα、[N II]和Hβ发射线及蓝连续谱超越支持了这一解释。

Conclusion: SN 2024vjc是"弱或缺失Na I D吸收意味着可忽略的寄主星系消光"这一常见假设的明确反例，强调了采用多种独立消光诊断方法的重要性。

Abstract: Understanding dust attenuation toward extragalactic transients is critical for recovering their intrinsic properties and probing the local environments of distant galaxies. A popular diagnostic is the Na I D absorption equivalent width widely applied to extragalactic transients. In this paper, we present early-time optical and near-infrared observations of the Type Ib supernova (SN) SN 2024vjc, followed for $\sim$130 days post-explosion. SN~2024vjc exhibits only weak Na~I~D absorption, indicating only a modest host attenuation with $E (B-V)_{\rm host} \sim 0.18$ mag; if this argument were applied, SN~2024vjc would be a peculiar, faint, and red SN Ib while showing the light-curve shape and spectral evolution broadly consistent with those of canonical SNe Ib. We show that this is not the case; SN-based diagnostics (intrinsic color templates, color-curve evolution, and spectral dereddening) together with the Balmer decrement indicate substantial attenuation, $E(B-V) \sim 0.45$ $-$ $0.8$\,mag. The weak Na I D absorption may result from photoionization of neutral sodium by the intense radiation field of a young H$_{2}$ region at the explosion site; this scenario is directly supported by the detection of narrow H$_α$, [N$_{2}$], and H$_β$ emission lines and a blue continuum excess in late-time spectroscopy. SN~2024vjc represents a clear counterexample to the commonly assumption that weak or absent Na I D absorption implies negligible host-galaxy attenuation, and highlights the importance of employing multiple, independent attenuation diagnostics.

</details>


### [179] [Shock breakout from mildly relativistic ejecta in a dense wind: the case of EP260321a/SN~2026gzf](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24045)
*Akihiro Suzuki,Keiichi Maeda,Toshikazu Shigeyama*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究对爱因斯坦探针卫星发现的X射线暂现源EP260321a进行了激波突破（SBO）建模。通过半解析薄壳近似模型，发现其X射线辐射可用高速星周介质包裹层（动能约3.5×10^49 erg）与稠密星风（质量损失率约1.2×10^{-3} M⊙/yr）间的相互作用解释。暴发持续时间要求稠密CSM延伸至约350 R⊙，对应质量约10^{-5} M⊙。结果表明SBO观测可灵敏探测剥离包层超新星前身星在核坍缩前的质量损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用爱因斯坦探针卫星发现的新X射线暂现源EP260321a，检验激波突破模型在解释超新星早期辐射方面的能力，并探讨其对前身星质量损失过程的探测潜力。

Method: 采用基于先前工作的半解析模型，使用考虑相对论效应的薄壳近似，模拟超新星抛射物中具有 mildly 相对论性外层的物质与稠密类星风周介质之间的相互作用过程。

Result: 成功用激波突破辐射解释了EP260321a的观测特性，得到高速包裹层动能约3.5×10^49 erg，前身星质量损失率约1.2×10^{-3} M⊙/yr，CSM延伸尺度约350 R⊙，质量约10^{-5} M⊙。

Conclusion: 激波突破观测可作为灵敏探针，用于探测剥离包层超新星前身星在核坍缩前短时间内发生的质量损失事件。

Abstract: We present shock breakout (SBO) modeling of the recently discovered X-ray transient EP260321a detected by the \textit{Einstein Probe} mission. Our semi-analytic model, based on our previous work, follows the interaction between a supernova ejecta with a mildly relativistic outer envelope and a dense, wind-like circumstellar medium (CSM) by using a thin-shell approximation that incorporates relativistic effects. We find that the observed properties of the X-ray emission are well explained by the breakout emission powered by a high-velocity envelope with a kinetic energy of $\sim3.5\times10^{49}\,\mathrm{erg}$ (excluding the supernova ejecta) and a dense wind characterized by a mass-loss rate of $\dot{M}\simeq1.2\times 10^{-3}(v_\mathrm{w}/10^3\,\mathrm{km\,s}^{-1})\,M_\odot\,\mathrm{yr}^{-1}$, where $v_\mathrm{w}$ is the wind velocity. The observed burst duration requires the dense CSM to extend up to $\sim350\,R_\odot$, corresponding to a CSM mass of $\sim 10^{-5}\,M_\odot$. These results demonstrate that SBO observations provide a sensitive probe of mass loss from stripped-envelope supernova progenitors shortly before core collapse.

</details>


### [180] [Probing an Intermediate-Mass Black Hole Companion of Sagittarius A* with Pulsar Timing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24201)
*Zexin Hu,Lijing Shao*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用脉冲星定时观测来探测银河系中心可能存在的中等质量黑洞（IMBH），发现脉冲星-超大质量黑洞系统对第三体扰动极为敏感，即使存在复杂环境干扰，定时观测仍能对IMBH的存在提供强约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银河系中心人马座A*周围的恒星分布存在难以解释的观测特征，这可能由隐藏在中超大质量黑洞附近的第三体——中等质量黑洞造成；未来射电望远镜（如SKA）有望发现绕Sgr A*运行的脉冲星，从而提供通过脉冲星定时约束隐藏IMBH的可能性。

Method: 基于射电定时观测，研究脉冲星-超大质量黑洞系统中第三体（IMBH）的扰动可探测性，分析复杂银河系中心天体物理环境对观测的影响。

Result: 脉冲星-SMBH系统对第三体扰动极为敏感，即使在强扰动环境下，定时观测仍能对IMBH的存在施加严格约束，补充现有观测限。

Conclusion: 脉冲星定时观测有望成为探测银河系中心隐藏IMBH的有效手段，且与现有观测约束互补。

Abstract: An intermediate-mass black hole (IMBH) hidden in our Galactic Center (GC) may explain the puzzling observations of the stellar distribution around Sagittarius A* (Sgr A*), the supermassive black hole (SMBH) in the GC. Future observations with the next-generation radio telescopes, such as the SKA, are promising to discover pulsars orbiting around Sgr A*, and thus provide the possibility of constraining the hidden IMBH with pulsar timing. We study the detectability of a third-body, the IMBH, in the pulsar-SMBH system based on radio timing observation. We find that the pulsar-SMBH system is very sensitive to such a third-body perturbation and can be used to put stringent constraints on the existence of the IMBH. Even under strong perturbations caused by the complex GC astrophysical environments, timing observation will still complement the existing observational constraints.

</details>


### [181] [Constraints on the magnetic field in the hadronic scenario for the origin of the radio halo in the A1795 galaxy cluster](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24397)
*P. Marchegiani,V. Vacca,F. Loi,F. Govoni,M. Murgia*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文在强子起源假说下，结合费米-LAT伽马射线上限，约束了冷核星系团A1795中非热质子属性与磁场强度，发现中心磁场需大于5μG才能避免伽马射线超限，且非热质子径向分布需略缓于热气体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: A1795星团中的射电晕尺度达Mpc量级，但该冷核弛豫星团中预期没有湍流注入，因此射电晕可能源于强子过程。本文旨在利用伽马射线观测上限来检验这一假说。

Method: 假设磁场径向分布与热气体分布一致，设定多个中心磁场强度值，反演出重现射电晕通量和面亮度分布所需的非热质子分布，然后计算对应的伽马射线辐射并与费米-LAT上限比较。

Result: 中心磁场B₀<5μG时，伽马射线辐射接近或超过费米-LAT上限；B₀>5μG时才能满足约束，此时非热质子径向分布需略缓于热气体分布。

Conclusion: 强子起源要求A1795中心磁场大于5μG；若磁场更小，则需要其他机制来产生或加速非热电子。

Abstract: The radio halo in the cool-core galaxy cluster A1795 has recently been proposed to be of hadronic origin, because it extends on a scale of the order of Mpc, where no turbulence is expected to be injected in a relaxed cluster. In this paper we aim to constrain the properties of the non-thermal protons and the cluster magnetic field strength under the hypothesis of a hadronic origin of the radio halo at the light of the available gamma ray upper limit in this cluster. We assume a radial profile and several values of the central intensity of the magnetic field, and derive the corresponding properties of the non-thermal protons necessary to reproduce the flux density and the surface brightness profile of the radio halo. For the obtained results, we calculate the corresponding gamma ray emission and compare it with the Fermi-LAT upper limit. We obtain that, for a magnetic field energy radial profile following the thermal one, magnetic field central intensities $B_0<5\,μ$G imply a gamma ray emission similar to or above the Fermi-LAT upper limit. Therefore larger values of the magnetic field are necessary in the hadronic scenario. For these values the radial profile of non-thermal protons needs to be slightly decreasing with radius, although more slowly than the thermal one. For smaller values of the magnetic field different mechanisms for producing or accelerating the non-thermal electrons are necessary.

</details>


### [182] [Transport Properties of the MRI in Differentially Rotating Neutron Stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24457)
*Harshraj Bandyopadhyay,Peter Hammond,David Radice*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过高分辨率三维GRMHD模拟研究了差动旋转中子星中的磁旋转不稳定性（MRI），发现湍流输运系数（α_vis、ℓ_mix、α_DYN）在饱和时与λ_MRI和v_A存在相关性，但数值与基于量纲分析的预测存在约一个数量级的偏差，且具有高度时空间歇性；混合长度与密度基本无关，质疑了常用的大涡模拟假设；结论认为充分分辨的GRMHD模拟对定量预测不可或缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解差动旋转中子星中MRI诱导湍流的有效输运系数（剪切粘性α_vis、混合长度ℓ_mix、平均场发电机α_DYN）及其与平均流参数的关系，检验量纲分析预测的准确性，评估现有大涡模拟中常用假设的有效性。

Method: 采用高分辨率三维广义相对论磁流体动力学（GRMHD）模拟，对差动旋转中子星的MRI进行全分辨模拟，涵盖多种质量模型（高质量致密化模型和低质量稳定模型），测量湍流的输运系数并分析其与密度、MRI特征波长、垂直Alfvén速度的相关性。

Result: 饱和时ℓ_mix和α_DYN分别与λ_MRI和v_A相关，但数值比量纲分析预测值偏差约一个数量级；输运系数在时空上高度间歇；混合长度与密度基本无关；高质量模型因角动量输运失去旋转支撑而坍缩。

Conclusion: MRI诱导湍流的效应可通过量纲分析选定的输运系数在定性上复现，但定量预测必须依赖充分分辨的GRMHD模拟；现有大涡模拟中ℓ_mix(ρ)的假设需要重新审视。

Abstract: We perform three-dimensional, high-resolution, general-relativistic magnetohydrodynamics (GRMHD) simulations of the magnetorotational instability (MRI) in differentially rotating neutron stars. We consider both high-mass models which collapse either promptly, or due to the outward transport of angular momentum removing rotational support, and lower-mass models, which remain stable even after solid-body rotation has been achieved. We measure effective transport coefficients of the resulting turbulent flow, shear-viscosity $α_{\rm vis}$, mixing-length $\ell_{\rm mix}$, and mean-field dynamo $α_{\rm DYN}$-parameter, and their correlations with mean-flow parameters, such as rest-mass density $ρ$, characteristic wavelength of the MRI $λ_{\rm MRI}$, and vertical Alfvén velocity $v_A$. At saturation, $\ell_{\rm mix}$ and $α_{\rm DYN}$ correlate with $λ_{\rm MRI}$ and $v_A$ respectively, as expected from dimensional analysis. However, both quantities show deviations of roughly an order of magnitude from the values predicted on the basis of these correlations. They are also highly intermittent in space and time. The mixing length is found to be largely independent of density, calling into question the $\ell_{\rm mix}(ρ)$ ansatz used in many general-relativistic large-eddy simulations. Our results show that, while the effects of MRI-induced turbulence might be qualitatively reproduced by simulations that employ transport coefficients chosen using dimensional-analysis considerations, fully-resolved GRMHD simulations are needed to make quantitative predictions.

</details>


### [183] [Evolution of Calvera and Descendants of Calvera-like Central Compact Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24458)
*Ali Arda Gencali,Unal Ertan,Fatmanur Ertugrul,Seyda Demirok,Ndiogou Niang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用回落盘模型研究孤立中子星Calvera（被归类为中心致密天体CCO）的长期演化与后代，发现其观测的自转周期、周期导数及X射线光度可在~10^4年年龄、~4×10^10 G磁场强度下同时被复现，并预测其将在吸积终止后转变为普通射电脉冲星（RP），且大多数类似初始条件的CCO最终也会成为RP，分布在脉冲星死亡谷上边界附近，与观测中周期-周期导数图的RP星团位置一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Calvera（新近被归类为CCO的孤立中子星）的长期演化与其在回落盘模型下的后续演化路径，探索CCO与普通射电脉冲星之间的演化联系。

Method: 采用回落盘模型（fallback disc model）对Calvera及其类似初始条件的CCO进行数值模拟，拟合其自转周期、周期导数及X射线光度等观测数据，并追踪其长期演化至吸积盘失活后的射电脉冲星阶段。

Result: Calvera的观测参数可在回落盘模型下被同时复现：磁场强度~4×10^10 G，年龄~10^4年（与超新星遗迹年龄一致）；缺乏普通射电脉冲辐射的原因在于持续的吸积过程；吸积终止后它将变为普通射电脉冲星并在弱偶极矩作用下自旋减慢超过10^8年；大多数类似初始条件的CCO也将演化为射电脉冲星，位于或接近脉冲星死亡谷上边界；在P-Ṗ图中存在一个与模型预测位置一致的RP星团。

Conclusion: Calvera及其类似CCO可在回落盘模型下统一解释为处于吸积阶段的中子星，其最终演化归宿为普通射电脉冲星，这为CCO与射电脉冲星之间的演化联系提供了理论支持。

Abstract: Calvera (1RXS J141256.0+792204) is an isolated neutron star recently classified as a central compact object (CCO) after its association with the supernova remnant (SNR) candidate G118.4+37.0. In this work, we investigate the long-term evolution and descendants of Calvera and the CCOs with similar initial conditions in the fallback disc model. We show that the observed spin period, period derivative, and X-ray luminosity of Calvera can be reproduced simultaneously with a magnetic field strength of $\simeq 4 \times 10^{10}$ G at the pole of the neutron star at an age of $\sim 10^4$ yr which is consistent with the estimated SNR age of the source. In the model, the lack of ordinary radio pulsations is due to ongoing mass accretion on to the star. From our simulations, we estimate that the source will become an ordinary radio pulsar (RP) after the termination of the accretion. The source will spin down under the weak dipole torque alone as an RP for a very long time ($\gtrsim 10^8$ yr) after the inactivation of the disc. From our simulations, we find that most of the CCOs with initial conditions similar to those of Calvera also become RPs which remain above or close to the upper border of the pulsar death valley after the inactivation of their discs. In the period-period derivative diagram, there is indeed a cluster of RPs in the region where the descendants of Calvera-like CCOs are estimated to be located in our model.

</details>


### [184] [R-process nucleosynthesis from magnetar giant flares in neutron star--white dwarf mergers: A unified picture for peculiar long gamma-ray bursts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24531)
*Shu-Qing Zhong,Long Li,Le Zou,Ji-Gui Cheng,Yan-Zhi Meng,Jia-Hong Gu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出中子星-白矮星并合（含并前磁星和大质量白矮星）的统一模型，解释奇特长伽马射线暴（如GRB 211211A和230307A）的多个观测特征（多成分瞬时辐射、X射线平台、千新星）。白矮星潮汐瓦解形成等熵吸积盘，早期超吸积放大中子星环形磁场触发重复磁星巨耀发；主暴来自巨耀发"森林"，后续磁推进相产生延展辐射并解释主暴-延展辐射之间的低谷。巨耀发的正负电子-伽马火球冲击中子星壳层，通过α富集冻结机制合成r过程重元素，解决传统模拟中r过程产量不足的问题。火球集体抛射物质量足以驱动千新星，并合后磁星自旋减慢辐射同时解释X射线平台。该模型自洽统一了奇特长伽马射线暴的瞬时辐射、余辉、千新星和r过程核合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对GRB 211211A和230307A等奇特长伽马射线暴的多成分瞬时辐射、X射线平台和千新星等复杂观测特征难以用传统模型（如中子星-中子星并合或大质量恒星坍缩）解释的问题，本文旨在提出一个统一的理论框架来解释这些现象。

Method: 提出中子星-白矮星并合模型：白矮星潮汐瓦解形成等熵吸积盘，早期超吸积放大中子星环形磁场触发重复磁星巨耀发；主暴由巨耀发初始尖峰构成，随后磁推进相产生延展辐射；巨耀发的正负电子-伽马火球冲击中子星壳层，通过α富集冻结合成r过程重元素；火球集体抛射和并合后磁星自旋减慢辐射分别驱动千新星和X射线平台。

Result: 该模型自洽解释了主暴-延展辐射之间的观测低谷；产生了足够的抛射物质量（M_ej≳10⁻⁵-10⁻³ M_⊙）以驱动观测到的千新星；解决了传统中子星-白矮星并合流体力学模拟中r过程重元素产量不足的问题。

Conclusion: 所提出的潮汐瓦解中子星-白矮星并合模型为奇特长伽马射线暴的瞬时辐射、余辉、千新星和r过程核合成提供了统一且自洽的理论框架。

Abstract: Peculiar long gamma-ray bursts (GRBs), exemplified by GRBs 211211A and 230307A, exhibit a long-duration multi-component prompt emission, an X-ray plateau in their afterglow, and a kilonova signature. Their origin remains highly debated. In this work, we present a unified picture for these events based on neutron star--white dwarf (NS--WD) mergers involving a pre-merger magnetar and a massive WD. In this picture, tidal disruption of the WD forms a constant-entropy accretion disk. Hyperaccretion from this disk onto the NS during the early accretion phase amplifies its toroidal magnetic field to strengths sufficient to trigger repeated magnetar giant flares (GFs). The main burst (MB) of the prompt emission consists of a ``forest'' of initial spikes from these GFs, while the subsequent magnetic propeller phase generates the extended emission (EE) and naturally explains the observed MB--EE trough. Crucially, the $e^{\pm}$-$γ$ fireball associated with each GF initial spike shocks the NS crust, leading to crustal ejection that synthesizes r-process heavy elements via the $α$-rich freeze-out mechanism, thereby resolving the r-process deficit in conventional NS--WD hydrodynamic simulations. The ensemble of such fireballs over the MB duration collectively yields $M_{\rm ej}\gtrsim 10^{-5}-10^{-3}\,M_\odot$ of ejecta, sufficient to power the observed kilonova signature when further boosted by the spin-down of the post-merger magnetar. Meanwhile, the spin-down radiation also powers the X-ray plateau. This tidally disrupted NS--WD merger picture provides a self-consistent framework that unifies the prompt emission, afterglow, kilonova, and r-process nucleosynthesis observed in peculiar long GRBs.

</details>

### [185] [New class of exactly flat topological bands - compact localised states protected by local graph topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22831)
*Tamaghna Hazra*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在任意图的面（faces）上构建精确平带哈密顿量的通用方法，证明了这些平带具有由面-图连接性局部拓扑保护的大规模简并性，并讨论了其在量子网络、量子机器学习及拓扑量子计算中的潜在应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强关联量子物质的核心在于非对易量子算符之间的张力。具有宏观简并性的哈密顿量对这一领域具有普遍意义，因为这意味着对任何非对易微扰都具有无限敏感性。在莫尔异质结构中，在动力学哈密顿量中工程化这种广泛简并性，为从产生的平带中涌现出奇异强关联物相创造了肥沃的土壤。

Method: 引入了一种在任意图的面（faces）上构建无限族精确平带哈密顿量的方案，并在四个布拉维格子的面上演示了该算法。利用离散图版本的Atiyah-Singer指标定理，证明了这些面-图哈密顿量的大规模简并性由面-图连接性的局部拓扑保护。

Result: 产生的宏观零空间产生紧致的局域化态，由于跃迁路径中的阻挫，这些态随时间保持局域化。

Conclusion: 探讨了这种非色散量子模式在不同场景中的广泛影响，包括量子网络中的阻滞动力学、量子机器学习算法以及无马约拉纳费米子的拓扑量子计算。

Abstract: Strongly correlated quantum matter is fundamentally defined by the tension between non-commuting quantum operators. Hamiltonians exhibiting macroscopic degeneracies are of general interest in this field because they imply an infinite susceptibility to any non-commuting perturbation. In moiré heterostructures, engineering such extensive degeneracies in the kinetic Hamiltonian creates a fertile garden for exotic strongly correlated phases of matter to emerge from the resulting flat bands. Here, we introduce a prescription to construct an infinite family of exact flat band Hamiltonians supported on the faces of arbitrary graphs. We demonstrate this algorithm on the faces of four Bravais lattices. Using a discrete graph generalization of the Atiyah-Singer index theorem, we prove that the extensive degeneracies of such face-graph Hamiltonians are protected by the local topology of the face-graph connectivity. The resulting macroscopic null spaces yield compact localised states that remain localised over time due to frustration in hopping pathways. We discuss the broad implications of such non-dispersing quantum modes in diverse settings, from arrested dynamics in quantum networks and quantum machine learning algorithms to Majorana-free topological quantum computation.

</details>


### [186] [Single crystal growth, structural and magnetic properties of CeZn$_{2-x}$Ga$_{2+x}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22942)
*Danila Sokratov,H. Cein Mandujano,Ram Kumar,Jared Z. Dans,Phineas Sobel,Nicholas A. Crombie,Alicia Manjón-Sanz,Danielle R. Yahne,Philip Piccoli,Peter Y. Zavalij,Efrain E. Rodriguez,Johnpierre Paglione*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文报道了CeZn$_{2-x}$Ga$_{2+x}$单晶的结构与磁性，发现其在~4K存在磁转变、变磁性态和强磁各向异性，并通过反向蒙特卡洛分析揭示了Ce位局域晶体学无序驱动了铁磁与反铁磁竞争，导致自旋玻璃行为，表明局域无序可用于调控BaAl$_4$型金属间化合物的阻挫磁态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索BaAl$_4$母体结构中R-Zn-Ga三元化合物的磁性成员，特别是寻找Ga取代对CeZn$_2$Ga$_2$磁性的调控作用。

Method: 单晶生长、磁性质测量、粉末中子衍射及反向蒙特卡洛分析。

Result: 观察到~4K磁转变、低温变磁性态和强磁各向异性；发现Ce位存在显著的局域晶体学无序，且批次间存在差异，表明对局域结构缺陷高度敏感。

Conclusion: 局域晶体学无序驱动了铁磁与反铁磁相互作用的竞争，导致自旋玻璃行为和复杂各向异性磁性；通过调控局域晶体学无序可工程设计BaAl$_4$型结构中的阻挫磁态。

Abstract: The tetragonal BaAl$_4$ ($I4/mmm$) parent structure underpins a diverse family of materials exhibiting novel phenomena, including nematic superconductivity, topological semimetallicity, and heavy fermion behavior. The recent growth of ternary R-Zn-Ga compounds, such as the previously reported CeZn$_2$Ga$_2$, has explored some of the members exhibiting rare-earth magnetism within this family. In this paper, we report on the structural and magnetic properties of single crystals of CeZn$_{2-x}$Ga$_{2+x}$, a Ga-rich analogue of CeZn$_2$Ga$_2$. Our CeZn$_{2-x}$Ga$_{2+x}$ samples exhibit magnetic properties distinct from the paramagnetic behavior previously reported for CeZn$_2$Ga$_2$. We observe a magnetic transition around 4 K, pronounced metamagnetic states at low temperatures, and strong magnetic anisotropy. Though there are batch-to-batch variations that suggest a strong sensitivity to local structural imperfections, we consistently see the presence of magnetic transitions and metamagnetic states in our crystals. To investigate the local structural sensitivity hypothesis, we performed Reverse Monte Carlo analysis of collected powder neutron diffraction data, revealing the presence of significant local crystallographic disorder of the magnetic Ce site. Our findings demonstrate that the positional disorder drives competing ferromagnetic and antiferromagnetic correlations that lead to the observed spin glass behavior and complex anisotropic magnetism. This study illustrates that tuning the local crystallographic disorder enables engineering frustrated magnetic states in BaAl$_4$-type and similar intermetallic structures.

</details>


### [187] [Field-tuned incommensurate fan phase in an Ising-like triangular antiferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23173)
*D. Flavián,R. Okuma,P. Manuel,Q. Huang,H. Zhou,R. Coldea*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用单晶中子衍射技术，在Ising型三角晶格反铁磁体Na₂BaCo(PO₄)₂中，于磁化饱和以下磁场范围内发现了一种场调制的非公度相。该相沿Ising轴施加磁场时出现，其面内非公度传播矢量随磁场变化沿六方布里渊区边界移动。通过半经典框架将其识别为共面扇形相，并研究了偶极-偶极相互作用和键相关交换各向异性对其稳定机制的作用。提出的包含层间耦合的最小交换模型重现了复杂的磁场相图及扇形相中非公度传播矢量的场依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统XXZ模型无法解释该体系中观察到的场致非公度相，需要更精细的模型来理解Ising型三角晶格反铁磁体在强磁场下的行为。

Method: 单晶中子衍射实验观测结合半经典理论框架分析，考虑偶极-偶极相互作用、键相关交换各向异性及层间耦合构建最小交换模型。

Result: 在Na₂BaCo(PO₄)₂中发现了磁化饱和前的场调非公度相，其传播矢量沿六方布里渊区边界移动；将该相识别为共面扇形相。

Conclusion: 偶极-偶极相互作用和键相关交换各向异性是稳定该非公度扇形相的关键机制，包含层间耦合的最小交换模型能完整重现复杂磁场相图。

Abstract: Using single-crystal neutron diffraction we report the observation of a field-tuned incommensurate phase in a field range just below magnetization saturation in the Ising-like triangular-lattice antiferromagnet Na$_2$BaCo(PO$_4$)$_2$. This phase occurs for magnetic fields applied along the Ising axis and exhibits an in-plane incommensurate propagation vector that moves along the hexagonal Brillouin zone boundary upon varying field, challenging the simple $XXZ$ antiferromagnetic description of the system. Within a semiclassical framework, we successfully identify this pre-saturation phase as a coplanar fan phase and study the role of dipole-dipole interactions and bond-dependent exchange anisotropy in its stabilization mechanism. We additionally present a minimal exchange model including interlayer couplings, which reproduces the complex magnetic phase diagram in field including the observed field-dependence of the incommensurate propagation vector in the fan phase.

</details>


### [188] [Hyperdeterminant wavefunctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23392)
*Guan-Lin Lin,Di Xiao,Ying Ran*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文系统介绍了超行列式波函数作为强关联量子态变分波函数理论框架，推广了费米子部分子构造，提供了可靠的微观物理图像，并允许直接访问分数化自由度及微观有效场理论。在分数量子反常霍尔绝缘体和量子自旋液体中展示了应用，并指出超行列式态属于更一般的融合高斯态变分波函数类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为强关联量子态建立一种系统化的变分波函数理论框架，以克服现有费米子部分子构造的局限性，提供更可靠的微观物理描述。

Method: 提出超行列式波函数框架，推广费米子部分子构造，结合数值模拟算法，通过变分法研究强关联量子态。

Result: 成功应用于分数量子反常霍尔绝缘体和量子自旋液体，揭示了分数化自由度及微观有效场理论。

Conclusion: 超行列式态属于更一般的融合高斯态变分波函数类，为强关联量子态研究提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We systematically introduce hyperdeterminant wavefunctions as a variational-wavefunction-based theoretical framework for strongly correlated quantum states of matter, together with practical numerical simulation algorithms. This framework generalizes previously known fermionic parton constructions, yields reliable microscopics with intuitive physical pictures, and allows direct access to the fractionalized degrees of freedom together with associated microscopic effective field theories. We demonstrate the applications of this framework to fractional Chern insulators and quantum spin liquids. We comment that the hyperdeterminant states belong to a more general class of variational wavefunctions: the fused Gaussian states.

</details>


### [189] [Interplay of Spin Waves, Crystal-Field Excitations, and Phonons in Multiferroic Ba3HoRu2O9 revealed by Inelastic Neutron Scattering, Crystal-Field Analysis, and Machine-Learned Phonon Calculations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23490)
*Ekta Kushwaha,Sayan Ghosh,Gourab Roy,Mohit Kumar,Manh Duc Le,Jhuma Shannigrahi,Som Datta Kaushik,Devashibhai T. Adroja,Tathamay Basu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文结合非弹性中子散射、线性自旋波计算、晶体场分析、拉曼光谱和机器学习力场声子计算，系统解析了六方6H钙钛矿Ba3HoRu2O9中自旋-偶极耦合和高能激发的微观起源，确定了6.2 meV以下色散磁激发为Ru-Ho耦合磁网络的集体自旋波激发，而39 meV附近的宽峰则源于Ho3+晶体场激发、晶格振动和Ru2O9分子磁激发的重叠贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解相关4d-4f多铁性氧化物中自旋-偶极耦合和高能激发的微观起源具有挑战性，因为磁、晶体场和晶格激发在能量上经常重叠。Ba3HoRu2O9因其Ru2O9分子单元和局域Ho3+矩共存，为研究这种相互作用提供了理想平台。

Method: 结合非弹性中子散射（INS）、线性自旋波计算、晶体场分析（Stevens形式）、拉曼光谱和机器学习力场（MLFF）声子计算等多种互补手段。

Result: 1) 6.2 meV以下的色散磁激发被线性自旋波理论准确再现，确定为Ru-Ho耦合磁网络的集体自旋波激发；2) 在20、39、70和90 meV附近观测到宽激发，在远高于磁有序温度时仍存在；3) 晶体场计算将最强Ho3+跃迁置于实验观测能量窗口内；4) 拉曼光谱和MLFF声子计算识别出能量相当的光学声子；5) 39 meV附近的宽INS峰与Ho3+晶体场激发、晶格振动和Ru2O9分子磁激发的重叠贡献一致。

Conclusion: 这些发现建立了理解该多铁性4d-4f化合物中自旋、晶体场和晶格自由度之间相互作用的微观框架。

Abstract: Understanding the microscopic origin of spin-dipole coupling and high-energy excitations in correlated 4d-4f multiferroic oxides is challenging because magnetic, crystal-field, and lattice excitations frequently overlap in energy. The hexagonal 6H perovskite Ba3HoRu2O9 provides an ideal platform to investigate this interplay owing to the coexistence of Ru2O9 molecular units and localized Ho3+ moments. To identify the contributions from these different excitations, we combine inelastic neutron scattering (INS) with linear spin-wave calculations, crystal-field analysis, Raman spectroscopy, and machine-learned force field (MLFF) phonon calculations. A dispersive magnetic excitation below 6.2 meV is accurately reproduced by linear spin-wave theory, establishing its origin as a collective spin-wave excitation of the coupled Ru-Ho magnetic network. At higher energies, broad excitations centered near 20, 39, 70, and 90 meV is observed that are present far above magnetic ordering temperature. Crystal-field calculations based on the Stevens formalism place the strongest Ho3+ transitions within the experimentally observed energy window, while Raman spectroscopy and MLFF phonon calculations identify optical phonons with comparable energies. Together, these complementary results show that the broad INS feature near 39 meV is consistent with overlapping contributions from Ho3+ crystal-field excitations, lattice vibrations, and previously reported Ru2O9 molecular magnetic excitations. These findings establish a microscopic framework for understanding the interplay between spin, crystal-field, and lattice degrees of freedom in this multiferroic 4d-4f compound.

</details>


### [190] [Observation of an emergent energy scale close to dimensional reduction in a quasi-two-dimensional quantum magnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23547)
*Anneke Reinold,Laur Peedu,Kirill Amelin,Urmas Nagel,Toomas Rõõm,Sven Luther,Hannes Kühne,Dirk Wulferding,Kingshuk Mukhuti,Maarten W. de Dreu,Peter C. M. Christianen,Dennis Kudlacik,Dmitri Yakovlev,Zhiying Zhao,Taro Nakajima,Yoshimitsu Kohama,Thomas Lorenz,Franco Lisandrini,Corinna Kollath,Marcin Raczkowski,Fakher F. Assaad,Zhe Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文报道在准二维量子磁体Cu₂(OH)₃Br中，通过临界横场伊辛链的适当扰动，实验观测到E₈李代数对应的特征有质量谱，为E₈对称性和束缚态激发提供了证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证可积场论对凝聚态系统中涌现的多体量子临界现象的描述能力，特别是E₈对称性在真实材料中的实验实现。

Method: 对横场伊辛链在远离临界点处施加适当扰动，利用准二维量子磁体Cu₂(OH)₃Br中铁磁链子晶格进行实验观测。

Result: 实验观测到临界横场诱导的维度缩减附近的特征有质量谱，该谱的比率和关联与E₈李代数对应的可积场论精确吻合。

Conclusion: 可积场论能够精确描述凝聚态系统中涌现的量子临界现象，E₈对称性在真实材料中得以实验实现。

Abstract: By appropriately perturbing a critical transverse-field Ising chain away from its critical point, the system can develop a finite correlation length with a characteristic purely massive spectrum, whose ratios and correlations are precisely described by an integrable field theory and an infinite set of integrals of motion corresponding to the $E_8$ Lie algebra. In this work, we report on experimental observation of a characteristic massive spectrum close to transverse field-induced dimensional reduction in a quasi-two-dimensional quantum magnet Cu$_2$(OH)$_3$Br, providing evidence for an emergent $E_8$ symmetry and the corresponding excitations of bound states in the sublattice of its ferromagnetic chains. These results demonstrate the power of integrable field theory in describing emergent many-body quantum critical phenomena in condensed matter systems.

</details>


### [191] [Pair-Density Wave from Doping an Altermagnetic Mott Insulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23654)
*Shuai A. Chen,Qianqian Chen,Guangyu Yu,Zheng Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过大尺度密度矩阵重正化群方法，研究了掺杂交错磁性莫特绝缘体的棋盘格t-J模型，发现从均匀d波超导态到与条纹序共存的配对密度波（PDW）态的转变，并观察到PDW波矢与条纹波矢之间的非常规锁定关系Q_PDW≈2Q_Stripe，揭示了交错磁性是掺杂莫特绝缘体中有限动量超导的强耦合微观途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究掺杂交错磁性莫特绝缘体中是否存在有限动量超导（PDW）态，以及交错磁性如何作为产生有限动量超导的微观机制。

Method: 采用大尺度密度矩阵重正化群（DMRG）方法，在六腿圆柱上的棋盘格t-J模型中研究掺杂与交错磁性各向异性的基态相图。

Result: 发现从均匀d波超导态（伴有电荷调制）到与条纹序共存的PDW态的转变；报道了非常规的波矢锁定关系Q_PDW≈2Q_Stripe；揭示了短程局域d波配对与长程有限动量PDW关联的两尺度结构。

Conclusion: 交错磁性为掺杂莫特绝缘体中有限动量超导提供了一条强耦合的微观实现途径。

Abstract: Pair-density-wave (PDW) superconductivity is a state in which the superconducting order parameter modulates at a finite wavevector. Using large-scale density matrix renormalization group, we study the doped altermagnetic Mott insulator in the checkerboard $t$-$J$ model, where altermagnetic exchange anisotropy is encoded microscopically through anisotropic ferromagnetic next-nearest-neighbor exchange. By mapping the ground-state phase diagram as a function of doping and altermagnetic anisotropy, mainly on six-leg cylinders, we identify a transition from a uniform $d$-wave superconducting regime with charge modulation to a PDW regime coexisting with stripe order. In the PDW regime, we report an unconventional wave-vector locking $\mathbf Q_{\mathrm{PDW}}\approx 2\mathbf Q_{\mathrm{Stripe}}$ along the cylinder direction, in contrast to the conventional relation. Pair correlations reveal a two-scale structure, consisting of short-distance local $d$-wave pairing and long-distance finite-momentum PDW correlations. A symmetry-based Ginzburg--Landau analysis is presented for the observed locking. Our results identify altermagnetism as a strong-coupling, microscopically grounded route to finite-momentum superconductivity in doped Mott insulators.

</details>


### [192] [Engineering two-body interaction for the Moore-Read State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23695)
*Yi Yang,Xin Wan,Zi-Xiang Hu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于可微分的逆向哈密顿量设计框架，通过梯度优化的精确对角化方法优化Haldane赝势，成功实现了超过99%重叠率的Pfaffian分数量子霍尔相的稳定，并发现了一种近乎普适的指数衰减赝势轮廓。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定非阿贝尔分数量子霍尔相是强关联拓扑物质和量子模拟中的核心挑战，传统库仑相互作用难以高效实现该拓扑相。

Method: 采用基于JAX的可微分精确对角化框架，将Haldane赝势作为变分参数，通过梯度上升最大化多体基态与Moore-Read态的重叠，优化得到赝势分布。

Result: 对于N_e≤12的系统，Pfaffian重叠超过99%；中性激发谱和轨道纠缠谱进一步证实了优化相互作用稳定了Pfaffian拓扑相。

Conclusion: 三体Pfaffian母哈密顿量的关键特征可被有效编码在恰当设计的二体相互作用中；发现了近乎普适的指数衰减赝势轮廓，为在量子模拟中工程化非阿贝尔拓扑序建立了通用框架。

Abstract: Engineering interactions that stabilize non-Abelian fractional quantum Hall phases is a central challenge in strongly correlated topological matter and quantum simulation. We introduce a differentiable framework for inverse Hamiltonian design, in which Haldane pseudopotentials are optimized by gradient-based exact diagonalization to stabilize target fractional quantum Hall phases. In spherical geometry, the Haldane pseudopotentials are treated as variational parameters and optimized in a JAX-based exact-diagonalization framework. By directly maximizing the overlap between the many-body ground state and the Moore-Read state, we obtain a robust pseudopotential profile that has Pfaffian overlaps exceeding $99\%$ for systems up to $N_e=12$, substantially improving over conventional Coulomb interactions. Analyses of the neutral excitation spectrum and orbital entanglement spectrum further confirm that the optimized interaction stabilizes the Pfaffian topological phase. Our results demonstrate that essential features of the three-body Pfaffian parent Hamiltonian can be effectively encoded in a suitably designed two-body interaction. Furthermore, they identify a nearly universal exponentially decaying pseudopotential profile that stabilizes the Pfaffian phase and establishes a general framework toward engineering non-Abelian topological order in quantum simulation.

</details>


### [193] [Symmetry Criterion for Van Hove Criticality at Non-Time-Reversal-Invariant Momenta](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23985)
*Min-Quan Kuang,Hua-Yu Li*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于表示论和非时间反演不变动量点（non-TRIMs）的完整分类框架，系统判定能带线性色散项是否由对称性强制为零，从而预测三维量子材料中范霍夫奇点的存在与临界性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非时间反演不变动量点（non-TRIMs），时间反演对称性不对能带线性项施加约束，因此需要从群表示论出发，系统分类对称性是否强制能带线性项为零，从而为工程化范霍夫奇点提供参数无关的普适准则。

Method: 利用小群表示论，将线性项是否为零归约为矢量表示是否包含平凡表示：非简并带取决于Γ_vec是否含Γ_1及其重数；简并带则通过Wigner-Eckart定理和Clebsch-Gordan系数在子带层面分类。以空间群225为例验证，并推广至所有单群极限下含non-TRIMs的空间群。

Result: 对空间群225，W点对所有非简并带是临界的；简并E带一般非临界；K和U点临界性依赖参数。相图揭示了对称性强制∇E=0（第一层次）与带参数决定高阶特征（第二层次）的双层层次结构。

Conclusion: 建立了一个由对称性决定、与参数无关的框架，用于工程化三维量子材料中的范霍夫奇点，并推广至所有单群极限下含non-TRIMs的空间群。

Abstract: At non-time-reversal-invariant momenta (non-TRIMs), time-reversal symmetry does not constrain the linear term of the band dispersion. Whether $\nabla E$ vanishes is therefore determined entirely by the representation theory of the little group. For nondegenerate bands, $\nabla E$ is forced to zero if and only if the vector representation $Γ_{\mathrm{vec}}$ of the little group does not contain the trivial representation $Γ_1$. When $Γ_{\mathrm{vec}}$ does contain $Γ_1$, $\nabla E$ is not forced to vanish for any nondegenerate band; the classification instead depends on the multiplicity of $Γ_1$ in $Γ_{\mathrm{vec}}$. For degenerate bands, the Wigner-Eckart theorem and Clebsch--Gordan coefficients determine whether linear couplings vanish, with classification performed at the subband level. Applied to space group 225, the criterion explains why the $W$ point is critical for all nondegenerate bands, the degenerate $E$ bands are generically noncritical, and the $K$ and $U$ points host parameter-dependent criticality. Supporting phase diagrams reveal a two-tier hierarchy: symmetry enforces $\nabla E=0$, while band parameters determine higher-order character. We extend this classification to all space groups hosting non-TRIMs in the single-group limit, providing a symmetry-dictated, parameter-independent framework for engineering Van Hove singularities in three-dimensional quantum materials.

</details>


### [194] [Magnetic-texture winding controls fermion-parity switches in an interacting $p$-wave magnet wire](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24038)
*Hyun-Yong Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文证明磁螺旋纹理的缠绕数变化改变电子边界条件，在半通量周期处产生奇偶效应，结合DMRG模拟支持Ising转变与无能隙电荷模式共存，为拓扑配对提供闭合几何中的数守恒探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究共面磁螺旋在环形几何中如何通过旋转自旋框架改变电子边界条件，以及这种边界条件对多体谱和拓扑配对的影响，特别是数守恒拓扑相中的奇偶约束反转。

Method: 理论证明（能级逐级分析旋量边界相位）、对称性比较（消除螺距修正）、密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）数值模拟、电荷刚度独立计算。

Result: 奇偶和偶纹理缠绕数诱导的边界相位相差半个单电子通量周期；数守恒拓扑相中，相邻相位缠绕分支的费米子奇偶性分配反转；DMRG验证了该反转，支持Ising转变与无能隙电荷模式共存，并建立有限尺寸奇偶分裂与电荷刚度的关联。

Conclusion: 磁纹理缠绕通过全局边界条件（而非局域能带色散）改变多体谱，为闭合几何中的数守恒拓扑配对提供新的探测手段。

Abstract: Coplanar magnetic spirals map locally onto uniform spin--orbit-coupled wires, but on a ring the rotating spin frame can change the electronic boundary condition. We prove, level by level in every fixed-particle-number sector, that even and odd texture windings impose boundary phases separated by half the single-electron flux period. Changing the winding by one also changes the physical pitch in inverse proportion to the circumference; a symmetric comparison removes this leading pitch correction. In the number-conserving topological phase, a global parity constraint then reverses the fermion-parity assignment of neighboring phase-winding branches. Density-matrix renormalization-group simulations show this reversal, support an Ising transition coexisting with a gapless charge mode, and relate the finite-size parity splitting to an independently calculated charge stiffness. Magnetic-texture winding thus changes the many-body spectrum through a global boundary condition that is not determined by the local band dispersion, providing a closed-geometry, number-conserving probe of topological pairing.

</details>


### [195] [Accessing Few-Layer CrI$_3$ Magnetoelasticity Through Bulk Single Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24222)
*J. Arneth,M. Möller,D. A. S. Kaib,P. P. Stavropoulos,A. Razpopov,M. Jonak,S. Spachmann,M. Abdel-Hafiez,S. Biswas,K. Riedl,R. Valentí,R. Klingeler*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过高分辨率磁致伸缩实验和第一性原理计算，研究了单轴晶格应变对范德瓦尔斯半导体CrI₃磁性的影响，发现磁致伸缩对表面效应异常敏感，并量化了表面反铁磁相中应变效应的强度（比体相铁磁相大~30倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CrI₃单分子层中铁磁长程有序的发现为基于二维量子磁体的自旋电子学应用开辟了新途径，但在器件制造过程中各向异性应变不可避免地会改变其本征电子性质；同时应变也可作为调控参数，因此需要系统研究单轴应变对CrI₃磁性的影响。

Method: 对块体CrI₃样品进行高分辨率磁致伸缩实验，并结合基于第一性原理的磁弹性计算，分别研究面内和面外应变对体相铁磁相（T_C=61 K）和表面反铁磁相（T^*≈50 K）磁性质的影响。

Result: 磁致伸缩对表面效应异常敏感；量化了表面反铁磁相中表面层间耦合J⊥^{SAFM}和表面自旋翻转场B^*的单轴应变依赖性，其应变效应比体相铁磁相大~30倍。

Conclusion: CrI₃具有较大的磁致伸缩响应，使得可通过块体单晶实验研究少层CrI₃的磁弹性耦合，无需剥离处理。

Abstract: The persistence of ferromagnetic long-range order in monolayers of the van der Waals semiconductor CrI$_3$ opens new routes for spintronic applications based on two-dimensional quantum magnets. In the fabrication of such devices, the constituent materials inevitably experience anisotropic strain, which modifies their intrinsic electronic properties. At the same time, strain can serve as a powerful tuning parameter, driving the material to desired regimes. While several theoretical studies have investigated the effect of biaxial in-plane strain on CrI$_3$ numerically, experiments are widely limited to the application of hydrostatic pressure. Here, we perform high-resolution magnetostriction experiments on bulk CrI$_3$ samples, and \textit{ab-initio}-based magnetoelastic calculations, to elucidate the role of uniaxial lattice strain on the magnetic properties. Our data show that magnetostriction in CrI$_3$ is unexpectedly sensitive to surface effects, which enables us to investigate the influence of in-plane and out-of-plane strain separately, in both the bulk ferromagnetic (BFM) phase emerging at $T_{\rm C}=61\,\mathrm{K}$ and the surface antiferromagnetic (SAFM) phase below $T^* \simeq 50\,\mathrm{K}$. In particular, we quantify the uniaxial strain dependence of the surface interlayer coupling $J^{\rm SAFM}_{\perp}$ and the surface spin-flip field $B^*$, which drastically exceed the strain effects in the BFM phase by a factor of $\sim 30$. The large magnetostrictive response allows us to study the magnetoelastic coupling in few-layer CrI$_3$ through experiments on bulk single crystals, without requiring exfoliation.

</details>


### [196] [Distinct finite-temperature phase diagrams of non-invertible Kennedy--Tasaki duals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24231)
*Weiguang Cao,Haruki Watanabe*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 非可逆变换联系的两个哈密顿量不一定共享相同的有限温度相图。一维下两者均无热序，仅共享零温转变；三维下两者从s=0就开始不等价：簇模型解析顺磁自由能，而其对偶Z₂规范理论在Tc≈1.31处存在解禁闭相变。量子蒙特卡洛显示这种不匹配占据了一个有限窗口，两侧相图在窗口关闭后重新一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究由非可逆变换（Kennedy-Tasaki对偶，部分等距映射）联系的两个哈密顿量是否必然具有相同的有限温度相图，验证这种变换在热力学层面的等价性边界。

Method: 理论分析结合量子蒙特卡洛数值模拟，比较簇模型插值H(s)及其Kennedy-Tasaki对偶Ĥ(s)在一维和三维下的有限温度相行为。

Result: 一维下两侧均无热序，仅共享s=1/2的零温转变；三维下s=0处已不等价：簇模型为解析顺磁相，对偶Z₂规范理论存在解禁闭相变。不匹配出现在插值的有限窗口中，表现为簇模型侧的自对偶冻结楔形区与规范理论侧的解禁闭穹顶区，窗口关闭后两侧相图重新一致直至共享端点s=1。

Conclusion: 非可逆变换（部分等距映射）不能保证有限温度相图的等价性，其有效性依赖于空间维度和哈密顿量参数区间，存在有限窗口内的相图不匹配现象。

Abstract: Do two Hamiltonians related by a non-invertible transformation necessarily share the same finite-temperature phase diagram? For a cluster-model interpolation $H(s)$ and its Kennedy--Tasaki dual $\tilde H(s)$, the map is a partial isometry and equates only their all-plus-sector partition functions. In one dimension, thermal order is forbidden on both sides, leaving only the common zero-temperature transition at $s=\tfrac12$. In three dimensions, however, the inequivalence is exact already at $s=0$: the cluster model has an analytic paramagnetic free energy, whereas its dual $\mathbb Z_2$ gauge theory has a deconfinement transition at $T_c\approx1.31$. Quantum Monte Carlo shows that the mismatch occupies a finite window of the interpolation, marked by a self-dual frozen wedge on the cluster side and a deconfined dome on the gauge side; once both close the two phase diagrams agree again, coinciding over the entire remaining interval up to the shared trivial endpoint $s=1$.

</details>


### [197] [Magnetic anisotropy in the near-stoichiometric van der Waals ferromagnet Fe$_3$GeTe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24543)
*Riju Pal,Joyal John Abraham,Satyabrata Bera,Mintu Mondal,Atindra Nath Pal,Bernd Buchner,Vladislav Kataev,Alexey Alfonsov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文利用电子自旋共振(ESR)技术，对近化学计量比的准二维范德瓦尔斯铁磁体Fe₃GeTe₂进行了系统研究，揭示了其易轴铁磁性、强单轴磁晶各向异性(约-5×10⁶ erg/cm³)、大磁子能隙(约0.363 meV)以及高温下仍存在的短程自旋关联，并发现Fe含量微小变化会显著影响低能磁子能隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准二维范德瓦尔斯铁磁体如Fe₃GeTe₂具有较高居里温度和稳健金属性，是研究低维巡游磁性的理想平台，但对其磁各向异性和自旋动力学缺乏定量理解，亟需系统的实验研究。

Method: 采用宽频段、宽温区、强磁场下的电子自旋共振(ESR)测量，包括频率依赖和温度依赖的高场ESR实验，对单晶Fe₃.₀₃±₀.₀₃GeTe₂进行表征。

Result: (1) 确立Fe₃GeTe₂为易轴铁磁体；(2) 发现大的内场随温度升高逐渐减小，且在T_C以上仍存在，证实短程自旋关联；(3) 自旋波模式分析得到3K下强单轴磁晶各向异性常数K_int ≈ -5×10⁶ erg/cm³；(4) 磁子能隙Δ(3K) ≈ 87.8 ± 13.7 GHz (0.363 ± 0.057 meV)；(5) Fe含量微小变化导致低能磁子能隙显著改变。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了Fe₃GeTe₂作为研究金属性二维磁体中可调各向异性和磁子激发的模型范德瓦尔斯铁磁体，并强调化学组分对自旋动力学的极端敏感性。

Abstract: Quasi-two-dimensional (2D) van der Waals (vdW) ferromagnets such as the series Fe$_{3-x}$GeTe$_2$, with a relatively high Curie temperature and robust metallicity, offer an ideal platform for investigating itinerant magnetism in reduced dimensions. Here, we present a comprehensive electron spin resonance (ESR) investigation of single-crystalline almost-stoichiometric Fe$_{3.03 \pm 0.03}$GeTe$_{2}$ across wide ranges of frequencies, temperatures, and magnetic fields to gain quantitative insights into its magnetic anisotropy and spin dynamics. Frequency-dependent ESR measurements establish Fe$_{3}$GeTe$_{2}$ as an easy-axis ferromagnet. Temperature-dependent high-field ESR reveals a large internal field that gradually decreases at higher temperatures. Remarkably, this internal field persists even above $T_\mathrm{C}$, evidencing short-range spin correlations in Fe$_{3}$GeTe$_{2}$. Analysis of spin-wave modes yields a strong uniaxial magnetocrystalline anisotropy $K_{\text{int}} \approx - 5 \times 10^6$ erg cm$^{-3}$ at 3 K and a large magnon gap $Δ(3 \mathrm{K})$ $\approx$ 87.8 $\pm$ 13.7 GHz ($\approx$ 0.363 $\pm$ 0.057 meV). Our study highlights that small variations in Fe content in Fe$_{3}$GeTe$_{2}$ lead to substantial changes in the magnon gap at low temperatures, indicating the extreme sensitivity of spin dynamics to the chemical composition. These results establish Fe$_{3}$GeTe$_{2}$ as a model vdW ferromagnet for exploring tunable anisotropies and magnon excitations in metallic 2D magnets.

</details>


### [198] [Anyon Condensation In Symmetry-Enriched Topological Phases: $G$-Grading of Multifusion Categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24740)
*Nianrui Fu,Siyuan Wang,Yu Zhao,Yidun Wan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了对称保护拓扑（SET）相中保持对称性的任意子凝聚机制，通过扩展的HGW弦网模型，证明保持G对称性的凝聚可由输入多融合范畴的相容分级刻画，并利用有限群扩展E=G×N构造具体实现，通过三个量子双例子（Z₂×Z₂、S₃、Z₄）展示该构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任意子凝聚是关联拓扑序的标准机制，但在对称保护拓扑（SET）相中，凝聚必须与全局对称性兼容，这使得问题更加复杂。本文旨在建立SET相中保持对称性的任意子凝聚的理论框架。

Method: 基于扩展的HGW弦网模型，利用多融合范畴输入数据。通过相容分级刻画G保持的凝聚，该分级构造了子SET相的输入。考虑有限群扩展E（G被N扩展）情形，E分级的融合范畴通过商对称性G诱导G-SET输入，且该输入自然携带相容的N分级以实现SET相内的进一步凝聚。用三个量子双例子（平凡扩展Z₂×Z₂、非阿贝尔半直积S₃、非平凡中心扩展Z₄）展示构造。

Result: 对称性分数量子化任意子并不阻碍保持对称性的凝聚，只要将凝聚体视为物理相干态。通过三个量子双例子验证了构造的有效性。

Conclusion: 建立了SET相中保持对称性的任意子凝聚的理论框架，证明了可用输入多融合范畴的相容分级刻画，并通过有限群扩展实现具体构造。为理解SET相中的任意子凝聚提供了系统方法。

Abstract: Although anyon condensation is a standard mechanism for relating topological orders, anyon condensation in symmetry-enriched topological (SET) phases is more intricate because the condensate must also be compatible with the global symmetry. We study symmetry-preserving anyon condensation in SET phases described by the enlarged Hu-Geer-Wu (HGW) string-net model with multifusion-category input data. We show that a $G$-preserving condensation is characterized by a compatible grading of the input multifusion category, and that this grading constructs the multifusion-category input of the child SET phase. To make this construction concrete, we consider the case where the relevant data come from a finite group extension $E$ of $G$ by $N$: an $E$-graded fusion category induces a $G$-SET input by passing to the quotient symmetry $G$, and the resulting input naturally carries a compatible $N$-grading that implements the further condensation inside the SET phase while preserving $G$. We illustrate the construction using three quantum-double examples: the trivial extension $\mathbb{Z}_2\times\mathbb{Z}_2$, the non-Abelian semidirect product $S_3$, and the nontrivial central extension $\mathbb{Z}_4$. The $\mathbb{Z}_4$ example further shows that symmetry fractionalized anyons do not obstruct symmetry-preserving condensation once the condensate is treated as a physical coherent state.

</details>


### [199] [Self-energy pole optimization of superconductivity in the bilayer Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24549)
*Taka-Shi Fujiwara,Shiro Sakai,Ryotaro Arita*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用动力学团簇近似研究双层Hubbard模型的实频自能结构，发现层间跃迁可调控自能极点，在Mott绝缘体-能带绝缘体边界附近产生带选择性赝能隙并增强s±波超导，其中自能极点的协同发展是配对增强的关键机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解双层Hubbard模型中自能的实频结构，揭示Mott绝缘体-能带绝缘体交叉区域的自能极点重排机制及其对超导配对的增强作用。

Method: 采用动力学团簇近似(DCA)计算双层Hubbard模型的实频自能，分析不同层间跃迁强度和掺杂浓度下的自能极点结构。

Result: 发现半满时自能极点在成键带和反键带之间交叉；掺杂后产生带选择性赝能隙并增强s±波超导；超导序参量在MI-BI边界附近最大，此处低能反常自能极点在两带中同时形成并协同增强配对。

Conclusion: 自能极点可解释为涌现的费米子激发，其与单层Hubbard模型的增强配对机制一致；通过层间跃迁调控自能极点使双层体系成为优化强关联超导的非常规平台。

Abstract: We study the real-frequency structure of the self-energy in the bilayer Hubbard model, using the dynamical cluster approximation. At half filling, the Mott insulator-band insulator (MI-BI) crossover involves a rearrangement of self-energy poles between the bonding and antibonding bands; these poles cross as the interlayer hopping $t_{\perp}$ is varied. Upon doping, this pole structure produces a band-selective pseudogap and enhances $s^{\pm}$-wave superconductivity. The order parameter is maximized near the MI-BI boundary, where low-energy anomalous self-energy poles develop simultaneously in both bands and cooperatively enhance the pairing. We further show that these self-energy poles can be interpreted as emergent fermionic excitations, offering an enhanced-pairing mechanism in common with the single-layer Hubbard model. The controllability of these poles through $t_{\perp}$ makes the bilayer system an unconventional platform for optimizing strongly correlated superconductivity.

</details>


### [200] [Sign-optimized Quantum Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24679)
*Julius S. Herz,Robin Schäfer,Matías G. Gonzalez,David J. Luitz*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出通过旋转希尔伯特空间局部基矢来最小化旋转后哈密顿量非对角矩阵元的相位，从而缓解量子蒙特卡洛方法中的符号问题。该方法在受阻海森堡反铁磁体的一维和二维枫叶晶格上进行了基准测试，在大部分参数空间内找到了更高效的基矢并改善了符号，达到了与最先进的数值链接团簇展开相当的温度范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号问题（sign problem）破坏了量子蒙特卡洛方法的多项式缩放，亟需有效缓解方法以扩展QMC的适用范围。

Method: 通过旋转希尔伯特空间的局部基矢，最小化旋转后哈密顿量非对角矩阵元的相位，使得这些最小值与平均符号的最优值重合。

Result: 在受阻海森堡反铁磁体的一维和二维枫叶晶格上，该方法在大部分参数空间内找到了更高效的基矢，改善了符号，达到了与数值链接团簇展开（NLCE）相当的温度下限。

Conclusion: 局部基矢旋转策略能有效缓解QMC的符号问题，扩展了QMC在强关联体系中的可及温度范围。

Abstract: The sign problem breaks the polynomial scaling of Quantum Monte Carlo methods. We alleviate it by rotation of the local basis of the Hilbert space, such that the phase of off-diagonal matrix elements of the rotated Hamiltonian is minimized. These minima coincide with optima of the average sign. We benchmark our method for frustrated Heisenberg antiferromagnets in one dimension and on the two-dimensional maple-leaf lattice. Our approach reveals more efficient bases with improved sign in a large part of the parameter space for all models considered, enabling us to reach lower temperatures, on par with state-of-the-art numerical linked cluster expansions which we directly compare to.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [201] [Optimal Investment with Switching Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22536)
*Yu-Jui Huang,Liviu Ignat,Traian A. Pirvu,Reihaneh Vafadar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了重大人生事件（如失业、疾病等）如何通过提升个体的风险厌恶水平，影响其最优投资决策。通过求解一个含事件前后风险厌恶变化的HJB方程，作者发现该方程无法通过Fenchel-Legendre变换线性化，并利用不动点、紧性和验证论证等方法，证明了该全非线性抛物方程存在正凸经典解，进而得到了最优投资策略的形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实证研究表明，重大人生事件会显著且持续地提升个体的风险厌恶水平。然而，这种事件触发的风险偏好变化如何影响最优投资决策，在理论上尚缺乏系统分析。本文旨在填补这一空白，构建一个可解析处理的理论框架。

Method: 在有限时间区间内建模，假设重大人生事件独立于金融市场发生，投资者预见其风险厌恶可能变化。推导HJB方程，发现其包含事件后的最优值函数。由于Fenchel-Legendre变换无法线性化该方程（产生含全非线性项的抛物方程），采用不动点、紧性和验证论证相结合的技巧，证明全非线性抛物方程正凸经典解的存在性。

Result: 建立了全非线性抛物方程正凸经典解的存在性与适当增长性；证明该解的凸共轭满足原HJB方程，即为事件前最优值函数。最优投资策略由事件前策略（由HJB方程解表示）和事件后策略（传统Merton比例）拼接而成。

Conclusion: 本文为事件驱动型风险厌恶变化下的最优投资问题提供了完整的理论求解方案，揭示了风险偏好突变对投资行为的非线性影响机制，为后续实证和扩展研究奠定了数学基础。

Abstract: Major life events can significantly increase individuals' risk aversion over a sustained period of time, as empirical studies reveal. How such an event-triggered shift of risk preferences impacts optimal investment is the focus of this paper. On a finite time horizon where a major life event may occur independently of the financial market, an investor aims to maximize her expected utility from terminal wealth while foreseeing a potential change in her risk aversion. We find that the associated Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman (HJB) equation involves the post-event optimal value function (under elevated but fixed risk aversion after the event's occurrence), and the Fenchel--Legendre transform fails to linearize this HJB equation: it yields a parabolic equation with a fully nonlinear term, induced precisely by the post-event optimal value function. Through a combination of fixed-point, compactness, and verification arguments, we establish the existence of a positive convex classical solution with suitable growth to the fully-nonlinear parabolic equation. The convex conjugate of this solution is shown to satisfy the HJB equation and coincides with the pre-event optimal value function. The optimal trading strategy is obtained by concatenating the optimal pre-event and post-event strategies -- the former is expressed in terms of the solution to the HJB equation and the latter is traditional Merton's ratio.

</details>


### [202] [A uniqueness result for finite-state mean field games with non-separable Hamiltonian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22537)
*Alekos Cecchin,Luca Di Persio,Nicola Fraccarolo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究有限状态空间上、转移率依赖于种群分布的连续时间平均场博弈，针对不可分离Hamiltonian情形，在成本强单调性与动力学交互项定量约束下，证明了任意时间区间上的新唯一性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Lasry-Lions单调性论证无法直接应用于转移率依赖于种群分布（即不可分离Hamiltonian）的平均场博弈，因此需要发展新的唯一性理论。

Method: 通过组合成本函数的强单调性假设与动力学中交互项的定量界来建立唯一性结果。

Result: 在任意时间区间上证明了该不可分离Hamiltonian平均场博弈系统的唯一性。

Conclusion: 在强单调性和交互项定量控制条件下，即使Hamiltonian不可分离，平均场博弈的解仍然是唯一的，拓展了经典理论的适用范围。

Abstract: We study a class of continuous-time mean field games on a finite state space with transition rates depending on the population distribution, leading to a non-separable Hamiltonian. In this setting, classical Lasry--Lions monotonicity arguments do not apply directly. We establish a new uniqueness result on arbitrary time horizons under a combination of strong monotonicity assumptions on the costs and quantitative bounds on the interaction term in the dynamics.

</details>


### [203] [Low-Rank KKT Updates and a Parallel Flipping Mechanism for Model-Based Derivative-Free Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22538)
*Donghan Wu,Pengcheng Xie*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对最小Frobenius范数更新模型，利用其KKT矩阵的固定内积结构，提出了一种坐标轴翻转操作及其诱导的Rank-2扰动，推导出$\mathcal{O}(n^2)$的KKT逆更新公式，避免了每次迭代的昂贵重分解。集成到并行信赖域算法中，在530个基准问题上显著降低了模型维护开销，并在紧函数评估预算下取得了更高的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的导数自由优化依赖二次插值，但每次迭代维护这些模型通常需要$\mathcal{O}(m^3)$的线性系统求解，计算开销过大，亟需降低模型维护的复杂度。

Method: 发现最小Frobenius范数更新模型的KKT矩阵具有固定内积结构；单点替换和提出的坐标轴翻转操作对该矩阵仅产生精确的Rank-2扰动；基于此结构推导$\mathcal{O}(n^2)$的KKT逆更新公式；将更新公式集成到并行信赖域算法中，工作节点独立翻转插值轴、刷新本地模型并同步最优配置。

Result: 在530个基准问题上测试，该方法相比标准求解器降低了模型维护开销，并在紧函数评估预算下取得了更高的成功率。

Conclusion: 利用KKT矩阵的固定内积结构和Rank-2扰动特性，可以实现高效的KKT逆更新，显著降低模型维护成本，提升导数自由优化在预算受限场景下的性能。

Abstract: Model-based derivative-free optimization relies on quadratic interpolation, but maintaining these models typically requires $\mathcal{O}(m^3)$ linear system solves. We show that for the least Frobenius norm updating model, the associated KKT matrix possesses a fixed inner-product structure. Both single-point replacements and a proposed coordinate-axis flipping operation induce exact Rank-2 perturbations to this matrix. Using this structure, we derive an $\mathcal{O}(n^2)$ update formula for the KKT inverse, eliminating costly refactorizations at each iteration. We integrate the update into a parallel trust-region algorithm where workers independently flip interpolation axes, refresh local models, and synchronize the best configuration. Tests on 530 benchmark problems show the method reduces model-maintenance overhead and achieves higher success rates under tight function-evaluation budgets compared to standard solvers.

</details>


### [204] [Comparing Optimization Models for Radiotherapy Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22539)
*C. C. Rambaldi Migliore,D. Stanicel,N. Musliu,G. Iacca,M. Roveri*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对放射治疗调度问题（RTSP），提出了两种新的贪婪启发式算法（RTSP First Fit 和 RTSP Best Fit），并将其作为模拟退火（SA）的构造启发式，在显著降低计算时间和内存消耗的同时，实现了接近精确方法的调度质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于整数线性规划（ILP）的每日批处理调度方法虽然在RTSP中效果显著，但需要大量计算时间和内存资源，限制了其在实际临床环境中的应用。

Method: 提出了两种贪婪启发式算法（RTSP First Fit 和 RTSP Best Fit），并将其作为模拟退火（SA）的构造启发式。在公开数据集上，与两种最先进的精确求解器求解的ILP公式进行对比评估。

Result: 新提出的启发式算法在保持接近精确方法求解质量的同时，大幅减少了运行时间和内存使用；与SA结合后，进一步提升了解的质量，同时维持了较低的计算资源消耗。

Conclusion: 所提出的贪婪启发式及其与模拟退火的组合方法，为解决RTSP的计算效率瓶颈提供了有效且实用的替代方案。

Abstract: The Radiotherapy Scheduling Problem (RTSP) involves determining an optimal schedule for patients undergoing radiation treatments, a task that has a massive impact on clinical outcomes given the central role of radiotherapy in cancer care. The daily batch approach--which consists of scheduling all the newly arrived patients together at the end of each day--modelled with Integer Linear Programming, is currently one of the most effective methods for the RTSP. However, this kind of formulation requires substantial computational resources in terms of time and memory. Here, we address these limitations by developing two novel greedy heuristics (named RTSP First Fit and RTSP Best Fit) and use them as constructive heuristics for a Simulated Annealing (SA) approach to optimize the scheduling. The proposed methods--the heuristics alone and their combination with SA--are evaluated on a publicly available dataset against an integer linear program formulation solved with two different state-of-the-art exact solvers. Evaluation metrics include six scheduling objectives capturing patient waiting times, preference satisfaction, and changes in linear accelerator assignment (aggregated in four different weight configurations), solving time, and memory consumption. The results show that the novel heuristics achieve solutions close to those of exact methods, while dramatically reducing runtime and memory usage; furthermore, when combined with SA, they further improve the solution quality while maintaining low runtime and memory usage.

</details>


### [205] [Multi-Path Routing in Decentralized Exchange Networks: Convex Allocation and an Improving-Path Certificate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22540)
*Ilia Zhavoronkov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一个基于图论和凸优化的去中心化交易所多路径路由框架，包含路径生成和流量分配两层实现，并通过KKT条件推导出改进路径证书。在以太坊主网上与四个生产级DEX聚合器对比，中位差距低于5个基点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化交易所中的多路径路由问题缺乏系统化的理论框架，现有方案往往依赖启发式方法，无法保证解的质量或提供最优性保证。

Method: 1) 将市场建模为有向代币多重图，弧上承载AMM交换函数；2) 路由分解为两层：基于gas感知的边缘k-最短路径枚举生成候选路径，以及在单纯形上求解带每池价格冲击上限的凹最大化问题分配流量；3) 利用KKT条件导出的乘子作为阈值，通过单次最短路径查询判断是否存在可改进的路径（改进路径证书）。

Result: 在以太坊主网上六个交易规模的重复WETH-USDT报价观测中，与四个生产级DEX聚合器相比，中位差距低于5个基点，且超过57%的epoch中排名前三。

Conclusion: 该框架在理论上提供了最优性证书，在实践中达到了与生产级聚合器竞争的性能，证明了图论与凸优化结合的去中心化路由方法的有效性。

Abstract: We present a graph-theoretic and convex optimization framework for multi-path routing in decentralized exchange networks, together with its implementation and empirical evaluation on Ethereum mainnet. The framework models the market as a directed token multigraph whose arcs carry AMM exchange functions. Routing is decomposed into two implemented layers: candidate path generation via gas-aware marginal k-shortest-path enumeration, where edge scores embed expected execution cost directly into graph traversal with an explicit pool-simple constraint tracked during path construction, and continuous flow allocation over the selected candidates solved as a concave maximization over a simplex with a per-pool price-impact cap. Under standard concavity and monotonicity assumptions, the KKT conditions imply marginal-output equalization across active paths. The central technical contribution is an improving-path certificate: after solving the allocation on k=20 candidate paths, the KKT multiplier is used as a threshold to determine via a single shortest-path query whether any omitted pool-simple path could improve the current solution; in our implementation the certificate confirms sufficiency in the majority of epochs. Execution is protected by an on-chain slippage tolerance enforced at the smart-contract level. We evaluate the implemented engine against four production DEX aggregators on repeated WETH-USDT quote observations across six trade sizes on Ethereum mainnet: median shortfall is below 5 bps across all sizes and top-3 quote rank exceeds 57% of epochs.

</details>


### [206] [SDE Guided Monte Carlo Reinforcement Learning: A Stochastic Maximum Principle Approach for Robust Decision Making in Noisy Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22541)
*Juncai Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了SDE-MC-AC框架，将随机最大值原理(SMP)的最优性条件启发式地映射到表格型强化学习算法设计中，包括势场梯度→基于势的奖励塑形、扩散系数→softmax温度、值函数梯度幅值→平均绝对TD误差，并推导了SMP驱动的自适应温度调度策略。在固定迷宫测试床的三种噪声条件下实验验证了五个可证伪假设，结果表明该方法在非平稳噪声下具有鲁棒性、收敛加速和跨噪声泛化能力，展现出"宏观确定、微观随机"的导航模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究随机最大值原理(SMP)的定性最优性条件能否作为设计鲁棒表格型强化学习算法的指导性启发。

Method: 提出SDE-MC-AC框架，建立SMP与强化学习组件之间的对应映射关系：势场梯度→基于势的奖励塑形，扩散系数→softmax温度，值函数梯度幅值→平均绝对TD误差。推导了SMP驱动的自适应温度调度策略，结合势塑形奖励、熵正则化和自适应温度控制，构建蒙特卡洛Actor-Critic智能体。

Result: 五个实验验证了：(i)倒U型最优随机性曲线，(ii)自适应调度在非平稳噪声下的鲁棒失败预防，(iii)势场引导的收敛加速，(iv)跨噪声泛化能力，(v)通过系统消融实验证明SDE组件的不可或缺性。轨迹可视化展示了"宏观确定、微观随机"的导航模式。

Conclusion: 即使将SMP启发式地转置到离散MDP中也能获得显著的实证收益，为随机最优控制与鲁棒强化学习之间架起了桥梁。

Abstract: This work investigates whether the qualitative optimality conditions of the SMP can serve as guiding heuristics for designing robust tabular RL algorithms. We propose the \textbf{SDE-MC-AC} framework, which establishes a set of SMP-inspired correspondences: the potential field gradient is mapped to potential-based reward shaping, the diffusion coefficient to a softmax temperature, and the value function gradient magnitude to the episode-averaged absolute temporal-difference (TD) error. Crucially, we derive an SMP-motivated adaptive temperature schedule that scales exploration stochastically in response to local value uncertainty. The resulting Monte Carlo Actor-Critic agent integrates potential-shaped rewards, entropy regularization, and adaptive temperature control within episodic updates. We conduct a set of five principled experiments in a minimal, fixed maze testbed under three canonical noise regimes (perceptual, dynamic goal, action) to test five falsifiable hypotheses derived from the SMP. Our results provide evidence for (i) an inverted-U optimal stochasticity curve, (ii) the robust failure prevention of the adaptive schedule under non-stationary noise, (iii) convergence acceleration by potential field guidance, (iv) cross-noise generalization, and (v) the indispensability of the combined SDE components through systematic ablation. Trajectory visualizations reveal a characteristic ``macroscopically deterministic, microscopically stochastic'' navigation pattern consistent with the SDE model. The study demonstrates that even a heuristic transposition of the SMP into a discrete MDP can yield significant empirical gains, thereby opening a bridge between rigorous stochastic optimal control and practical robust reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [207] [LiFT-MPC: Language-in-the-Loop Feedback Tuning of Cost Previews for MPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23832)
*Xinyi Yi,Ioannis Lestas*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出LiFT-MPC框架，通过在MPC循环中集成语言反馈调优（LiFT）校正机制，利用上下文信息（如新闻）改进时变目标（如能源价格）的预测，并给出性能保证，在储能管理案例中验证了经济性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有时变目标的模型预测控制（MPC）中，预测信号（如能源价格）常依赖于历史轨迹之外的上下文事件，难以准确预测，影响控制性能。

Method: 提出LiFT-MPC框架，将LiFT（Language-in-the-Loop Feedback Tuning）校正方案集成到MPC循环中，通过控制性能损失函数在线更新预测机制，并建立了闭环系统的性能保证。

Result: 在基于真实电价和新闻上下文的储能管理数值实验中，该方法显著提升了经济性能。

Conclusion: LiFT-MPC能有效利用语言上下文信息改进MPC中的预测精度，提升闭环经济性能，并具有理论保证。

Abstract: In model predictive control (MPC) with time-varying objectives, predicted signals need to be often incorporated in the cost function, such as prices in energy system operation. These are, however, often difficult to predict from the historical trajectory of these signals alone, as they may depend on other contextual events. We propose LiFT-MPC, an MPC framework that integrates a LiFT (Language-in-the-Loop Feedback Tuning) correction scheme to refine such predictions within the MPC loop. The prediction mechanism is updated online via a control-performance loss function, and we establish a performance guarantee for the resulting closed loop system. Numerical experiments using a realistic example of energy-storage management with real prices and news context to improve predictions, demonstrate an improved economic performance

</details>


### [208] [A Hybrid Matheuristic Framework for the Chinese Postman Problem with Load-Dependent Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22542)
*Thieu Khang Nguyen,Thu Huong Dang,Truong-Son Hy*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合元启发式搜索与数学规划的混合优化框架，用于求解带负载依赖成本的中国邮递员问题，并通过蚁群算法提升大规模实例的可扩展性。实验表明该方法在解质量和计算效率上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实物流与运输系统中，旅行成本依赖于车辆负载和能源消耗，传统中国邮递员问题无法建模此类负载依赖成本，因此需要设计专门的方法来高效求解CPP-LC问题。

Method: 提出混合优化框架，将局部搜索过程与简化的混合整数线性规划模型相结合，平衡探索与强化；同时开发蚁群优化算法以提升大规模实例上的可扩展性。

Result: 在基准数据集上的大量实验表明，所提框架能够持续获得高质量解，在解质量上优于现有方法，同时保持有竞争力的计算效率。

Conclusion: 混合优化策略对于实际应用中复杂的负载依赖路径规划问题具有显著有效性。

Abstract: The Chinese Postman Problem with load-dependent costs (CPP-LC) arises in real-world logistics and transportation systems where travel costs depend on vehicle load and energy consumption. In this work, we propose a hybrid optimization framework that integrates metaheuristic search with mathematical programming to efficiently solve CPP-LC. The proposed method combines local search procedures with reduced mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) models to balance exploration and intensification. In addition, we develop an Ant Colony Optimization (ACO) algorithm to enhance scalability on large instances. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently achieves high-quality solutions and outperforms existing approaches in solution quality, while maintaining competitive computational efficiency. These results highlight the effectiveness of hybrid optimization strategies for complex, load-dependent routing problems in practical applications. Our implementation is publicly available at https://github.com/HySonLab/MatCPP

</details>


### [209] [Route Based Map Matching via a Structured Codebook and Token Sequence Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22543)
*Takara Sakai*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于路线代码本和模糊字符串匹配的高效轻量级地图匹配方法，利用命名线路和命名枢纽的符号结构，在GPS噪声下仍能恢复精确路线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于隐马尔可夫模型（HMM）的方法计算量大，难以满足大规模GPS轨迹数据实时处理的需求。本文探索利用城市快速路网中命名线路和命名枢纽的符号结构，实现计算高效的地图匹配。

Method: 将候选路线表示为线路名与枢纽名构成的符号序列，形成路线代码本；使用网格量化器将GPS探针坐标转换为令牌序列；利用DAFSA×Levenshtein自动机对查询进行模糊匹配解码，实现低成本的近似字符串匹配。

Result: 在东京首都高速道路网的变形副本上评估，该方法在中等GPS噪声下能精确恢复路线，在强噪声下仍能识别线路和枢纽序列。解码成本比暴力扫描低数个数量级。

Conclusion: 提出了一种利用符号路线结构的高效地图匹配方法，通过路线代码本和模糊字符串匹配实现低计算成本下的精确路线恢复，适用于城市快速路网。

Abstract: This study proposes an efficient and computationally light route based map matching method for GPS track data on urban expressway networks. The key idea is to exploit a symbolic structure of named lines and named junctions that link level map matching leaves unused. We represent each candidate route as a sequence of line and junction names, take the set of such sequences as a route codebook, and formulate map matching as scored alignment of a probe trajectory against members of the codebook. Probes become token sequences via a mesh quantizer, a precomputed grid mapping each coordinate to a line or junction token, and the decoder returns a member of the codebook by construction. The codebook is indexed by a DAFSA $\times$ Levenshtein automaton, a fuzzy lookup technique from approximate string matching and speech recognition; the per query decoding cost is orders of magnitude lower than a brute force scan. We evaluate the method on a deformed replica of the Tokyo Metropolitan Expressway topology. The method recovers the exact route at moderate GPS noise and continues to identify the line and junction sequence under heavy noise; a sensitivity analysis maps the mesh resolution operating range. Real probe evaluation, channel model calibration, and a head to head HMM comparison are left to a forthcoming version.

</details>


### [210] [Learning to Optimize at Scale: A Benders Decomposition-TransfORmers Framework for Stochastic Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22550)
*Seung Jin Choi,Kimiya Jozani,Josh Cooper,Esra Buyuktahtakin Toy*

Main category: math.OC
