  For each of the $3$ profiles, we give an explicit full-capacity matrix with $9$ regular Pareto eigenvalues. We also prove that each profile occurs on a nonempty Euclidean-open set where every Pareto eigenvalue has a unique producing support.

</details>


### [249] [New Globalized Newton-Type Methods for Nonconvex Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23433)
*Vo Thanh Phat,Tuyen Tran*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通用的线搜索牛顿框架用于无约束优化，避免重复Hessian正则化，在PLK条件下建立全局收敛性，并展示了局部超线性/二次收敛速度，首次将牛顿法应用于强拟凸优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的全局收敛牛顿型方法通常要求目标函数凸或强凸，而非凸优化方法往往需要在每次迭代中进行Hessian正则化。本文旨在提出一种无需重复Hessian正则化的通用线搜索牛顿框架，并拓展到非凸优化（特别是强拟凸优化）场景。

Method: 提出一个通用的线搜索牛顿框架，仅在牛顿方向适当时才使用，否则切换为梯度方向。该框架统一了多种混合梯度-牛顿方法，并自然衍生出一种新的外梯度牛顿方法。理论分析基于PLK条件建立全局收敛性，并在适当正则性假设下证明局部超线性与二次收敛。

Result: 在PLK条件下建立了全局收敛性（允许孤立和非孤立聚点）；证明了局部超线性和二次收敛速度；首次为强拟凸优化提供了牛顿型算法及其完整的收敛性分析；数值实验验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的框架有效解决了非凸优化中牛顿法全局收敛的难题，特别是首次将牛顿型方法成功应用于强拟凸优化，并提供了完整的理论保证。

Abstract: Newton's method is one of the most effective second-order algorithms for smooth optimization because of its fast local convergence. However, existing globally convergent Newton-type methods typically require convexity or strong convexity of the objective function, while approaches for nonconvex optimization often rely on Hessian regularization at every iteration. In this paper, we propose a general line-search Newton framework for unconstrained optimization that avoids repeated Hessian regularization by exploiting the Newton direction only when it is well-defined and suitable. The proposed framework encompasses several existing hybrid gradient--Newton methods as special cases and naturally yields a new extragradient Newton method. We establish global convergence under mild assumptions, including the Polyak--Lojasiewicz--Kurdyka (PLK) condition, allowing both isolated and nonisolated accumulation points. We further prove local superlinear and quadratic convergence under appropriate regularity assumptions. Finally, we apply the proposed framework to strongly quasiconvex optimization and provide, to the best of our knowledge, the first Newton-type algorithm together with a comprehensive convergence analysis for this important class of nonconvex optimization problems. Numerical experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [250] [On the Bellman equation in recursive stochastic dynamic programming with the CES aggregator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23475)
*Anna Jaśkiewicz,Andrzej S. Nowak*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有CES聚合器和熵风险度量确定性等价的递归效用的离散时间无限时域马尔可夫决策过程，证明了贝尔曼方程解的存在唯一性以及最优平稳策略的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为具有递归效用（CES聚合器与熵风险度量）的无限时域MDP建立严格的数学理论基础，填补该领域在解的存在唯一性和最优策略存在性方面的理论空白。

Method: 通过数学分析证明贝尔曼方程解的存在唯一性，利用不动点定理和测度选择定理证明Koopmans方程的唯一解，进而证明可测最大值选择器构成最优平稳策略。并将方法推广到其他确定性等价情形。

Result: 在原始数据的温和条件下，证明了贝尔曼方程解的存在唯一性；任意平稳策略对应的Koopmans方程有唯一解；贝尔曼方程的可测最大值选择器即为最优平稳策略；该方法还可推广至包括风险中性在内的其他确定性等价情形。

Conclusion: 为CES聚合器与熵风险度量结合的递归效用MDP提供了完整的理论基础，证明了最优平稳策略的存在性，且方法具有一定的普适性。

Abstract: In this paper we investigate discrete-time infinite horizon Markov decision processes (dynamic programming models) with recursive utilities defined by the classical \emph{CES} aggregator and the certainty equivalent operator being the negative of the entropic risk measure. Under mild conditions on the primitive data we prove existence and uniqueness of a solution to the Bellman equation. Moreover, we show that for any given stationary policy, the Koopmans equation has a unique solution. These two facts imply that a measurable selector of maxima in the Bellman equation is an optimal stationary policy. The methods of our proofs are applied to study solutions to the Bellman equation for other certainty equivalents, including risk-neutral case.

</details>


### [251] [Convexity and SOS-Convexity of Sum of Separable and Biquadratic Quartic Polynomials and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23476)
*Shaon Naskar,Sujeet Kumar Singh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对四次及以上多元多项式的非负性和凸性判定是强NP难问题，提出了一类名为SPBQ（可分离与双二次型之和）的结构化四次多项式子类。研究发现，当关联的双二次型规模为n×2时，每个凸的SPBQ多项式必然具有SOS-凸性；但通过一个3×3双二次型的显式反例证明存在凸而非SOS-凸的SPBQ多项式。最后展示了SPBQ在凸多项式回归和流体动力学中的计算优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高次多元多项式的凸性判定是强NP难问题，现有的SOS-凸性松弛虽然可计算但存在充分性差距，需要明确其适用边界。

Method: 定义SPBQ多项式子类（可分离形式与双二次型之和），通过代数分析建立凸性与SOS-凸性之间的等价条件，构造反例证明充分性差距的存在，并在优化问题中对比计算效率。

Result: 对于n×2双二次型的SPBQ多项式，凸性等价于SOS-凸性；存在3×3双二次型的凸SPBQ多项式不是SOS-凸的。

Conclusion: SPBQ子类在保持结构可处理性的同时揭示了凸性与SOS-凸性之间的精确关系，并在实际应用中展现出计算优势。

Abstract: Determining whether multivariate polynomials of degree four or higher are nonnegative and convex is a strongly NP-hard problem. To mitigate these computational difficulties, sum-of- squares (SOS) convexity has been proposed as a tractable algebraic relaxation that yields a checkable sufficient condition for convexity and can be expressed as a semidefinite program (SDP). In this work, we introduce a structured subclass of quartic polynomials, called the Sum of Separable and Biquadratic (SPBQ) forms, and conduct a systematic analysis of the connection between convexity and SOS-convexity within this class. Specifically, we show that every convex SPBQ polynomial is necessarily SOS-convex when the associated biquadratic form has size n x 2. We then construct an explicit SPBQ example with a 3 x 3 biquadratic form that is convex but fails to be SOS-convex. Finally, we examine both unconstrained and constrained optimization problems involving SPBQ polynomials, demonstrate notable computational benefits compared to general SOS-based methods, and illustrate their use in convex polynomial regression and fluid dynamics.

</details>


### [252] [Existence of stable Lur'e systems for which the O'Shea-Zames-Falb stability test fails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23599)
*Andrey Kharitenko*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过构造反例，证明了O'Shea-Zames-Falb(OZF)稳定性检验对于具有斜率受限非线性的Lur'e系统的鲁棒稳定性并非必要条件，从而否定了J. Carrasco的一个猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来，OZF稳定性检验被认为可能是Lur'e系统鲁棒稳定性的必要条件，这一猜想由J. Carrasco提出。验证该猜想的正确性对于理解非线性系统稳定性理论具有重要理论意义。

Method: 构造一个具体的Lur'e互联系统，并设计一个全块乘子（full-block multiplier）来证明该系统的稳定性，同时证明该系统不存在OZF乘子。

Result: 成功构造了一个反例：一个稳定的Lur'e系统，其稳定性可由全块乘子证明，但无法通过OZF乘子检验。这证明了OZF检验并非必要条件。

Conclusion: O'Shea-Zames-Falb稳定性检验对于具有斜率受限非线性的Lur'e系统不是必要的，推翻了J. Carrasco的相关猜想。

Abstract: In this note we show that the well-known stability test proposed by O'Shea, Zames and Falb is not necessary for robust stability of Lur'e systems with slope-restricted nonlinearities and thereby disprove a conjecture by J. Carrasco. Specifically we construct a full-block multiplier which certifies stability of a constructed Lur'e interconnection that does not admit an OZF multiplier.

</details>


### [253] [Exact Worst-case Convergence Rates of Distributed Gradient Tracking Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23601)
*Qiuchen Tian,Li Chai,Jinming Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文分析了两种分布式梯度追踪算法DIGing和AugDGM的精确最坏情况收敛速率，证明它们在平均状态动力学上等价但在梯度追踪子系统上不同，并发现图连通性存在拐点σ=1/3，超过该点时AugDGM在目标函数条件数足够差时可达到集中式梯度下降的最优收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式优化算法分析大多给出保守的充分收敛条件和保守的收敛速率，缺乏精确的最坏情况收敛速率分析，无法准确理解算法性能极限。

Method: 通过特征值分解将MIMO系统稳定性分析简化为一系列参数变化SISO系统的稳定性分析，推导出精确的最坏情况收敛速率显式公式。

Result: (1) DIGing和AugDGM在平均状态动力学上相同，但梯度追踪子系统不同，后者完全决定收敛性；(2) 相同假设下DIGing的最优最坏情况收敛速率大于AugDGM；(3) 发现图连通性拐点σ=1/3，优于该点时AugDGM在目标函数条件数足够差时可达到集中式梯度下降的最优收敛率，否则无法达到。

Conclusion: 提供了两种典型梯度追踪算法的精确最坏情况收敛速率，揭示了图连通性对分布式优化算法性能的临界影响，为算法选择提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Different from most existing literature in the analysis of distributed optimization algorithms that reports sufficient convergence conditions leading to a conservative convergence rate, this work provides the exact worst-case convergence rates for two typical gradient tracking algorithms, DIGing and AugDGM. By eigen-decomposition, we show that two algorithms share the same average-state dynamics, while they differ from each other in the gradient tracking subsystems, which entirely govern algorithm convergence. Exploiting the diagonal structure of this decomposition, we reduce the stability analysis of MIMO systems to that of a set of parameter-varying SISO systems, from which explicit formulas for the exact worst-case convergence rate can be derived. These formulas clearly show that the optimal worst-case convergence rate of DIGing is larger than that of AugDGM under the same assumptions on objective functions and communication networks. Furthermore, we find that there is an inflection point in the graph connectivity, which is $σ=1/3$. For graphs with connectivity better than this inflection point, the optimal convergence rate of centralized gradient descent can be achieved by AugDGM provided the condition number of objective functions is worse enough. On the other hand, for graphs with connectivity worse than this inflection point, the centralized optimal rate can never be achieved. Numerical experiments validate the theoretical results.

</details>


### [254] [When Rates Are Geometric: Rate-Certificate Transfer for Contact Splittings in Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23642)
*George A Kevrekidis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出接触哈密顿系统作为连接连续时间ODE与离散优化算法的理论框架，证明了在三个可验证假设下，接触分裂算法能将连续时间收敛证书精确传递到离散算法，并通过数值实验验证了其在病态条件和深度学习任务中的竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散优化算法的收敛性分析常借助连续时间极限ODE，但ODE的收敛保证无法自动推广到离散算法。现有理论缺乏一个能精确传递收敛证书的统一框架。

Method: 建立接触哈密顿系统$H$在$J^1(\mathbb{R}^n)$上的内在衰减恒等式$\dot H = -H\,\partial_s H$，构造增广能量$\mathcal{E}$作为连续时间收敛证书。提出阶-$r$接触分裂算法，通过反向误差分析将连续证书在有限时间内传递给离散算法。将算法分解为动能$K$、势能$V$和耗散项$D$的设计模板。

Result: 主定理证明在三个可验证假设下，离散衰减包络由修正共形因子主导（误差$O(h^r)$加上反向误差影子缺陷）。二次重球问题给出完全可解的例子，其投影耗散跳蛙谱与共形辛优化理论一致。对强凸目标函数，通过辅助影子推论传递显式Bregman型李雅普诺夫证书。

Conclusion: 接触哈密顿系统为离散优化算法提供了精确的收敛证书传递机制，设计模板$H=K+V+D$可以系统构造闭式子流（包含接触特有的阻尼族），数值实验验证了共形因子跟踪阶数和在病态基准及深度学习上的竞争力。

Abstract: Discrete optimization algorithms are often analyzed through continuous-time limiting ODEs, but a convergence certificate for the ODE is not automatically one for the discrete algorithm. We develop contact Hamiltonian systems as a setting where the transfer can be made precise. A contact Hamiltonian $H$ on $J^1(\mathbb{R}^n)$ obeys the intrinsic decay identity $\dot H = -H\,\partial_s H$, so an augmented energy $\mathcal{E}$ built from $H$, together with the conformal rate $\partial_s H$, is a continuous-time rate certificate whenever $\mathcal{E}$ controls the objective gap. Our main theorem states, under three named and independently checkable hypotheses, that an order-$r$ contact splitting with step $h$ transfers this certificate over the finite horizon set by backward error analysis. The discrete decay envelope is governed by the modified conformal factor up to $O(h^r)$ perturbations plus a backward-error shadowing defect, and the mechanism is inherited exactly because the modified Hamiltonian is itself a contact Hamiltonian. Quadratic heavy ball is a fully solvable example: its projected dissipative-leapfrog spectrum agrees with established conformal-symplectic optimization theory, while the augmented contact Hamiltonian yields a sharp objective-to-certificate comparison that verifies the transfer hypotheses. For strongly convex objectives with state-dependent damping, an explicit Bregman-type Lyapunov certificate instead transfers by an auxiliary-shadowing corollary. The decomposition $H=K+V+D$ into kinetic, objective-encoding potential, and dissipation terms serves as a design template, with a catalogue of closed-form sub-flows including contact-specific damping families. Numerical experiments confirm the predicted conformal-factor tracking orders and show competitive performance on ill-conditioned benchmarks and deep-learning tasks.

</details>


### [255] [A Homogeneous Tensor Framework for High-Order Trust-Region and Spherical Polynomial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24046)
*Wenqi Zhu,Haibin Chen,Guanglu Zhou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于齐次张量表示的半径控制边界方法来解决高阶方法中非凸泰勒子问题的困难。针对三次情形(p=3)，引入二次移位并证明与三块多线性形式的等价性，进而提出近端交替最小化(PAM)方法。将该方法嵌入自适应齐次张量方法(Ada-HTM)中，在显式光滑性等条件下达到了ARp型评估复杂度O(ε^{-(p+1)/p})。数值实验表明PAM方法在结构化三次实例上匹配SOS证书，尤其适用于低秩张量，且Ada-HTM在坏条件问题上优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶方法可以改善最坏情况评估复杂度，但p≥3时的泰勒子问题是非凸多项式优化问题，通常难以求解。现有方法缺乏对高阶子问题的有效处理手段，需要开发能够处理任意阶泰勒多项式、特别是三次情形的实用算法。

Method: 1) 通过将步长增加一个常数坐标，将任意p阶泰勒多项式精确表示为p阶齐次张量形式；2) 针对三次情形，引入二次移位并证明在显式移位边界下与三块多线性形式在全局最优性上等价；3) 提出近端交替最小化(PAM)方法，具有闭式块更新；4) 将边界步骤机制嵌入自适应齐次张量方法(Ada-HTM)。

Result: 在显式光滑性、保护性下降、弱曲率非退化性和局部细化条件下，Ada-HTM达到自适应正则化类型(ARp)的评估复杂度O(ε^{-(p+1)/p})。数值上，PAM在结构化三次实例上匹配二阶矩-平方和(SOS)证书，对低秩张量扩展性好，Ada-HTM在坏条件和病态缩放问题上优于信赖域和三次正则化方法。

Conclusion: 该工作为高阶非凸优化提供了一种理论基础扎实且数值有效的解决方案，特别是通过齐次张量表示和交替最小化策略，使得高阶方法在实际问题中变得可行且具有竞争力。未来可推广到更高阶(p>3)的情形。

Abstract: High-order methods can improve worst-case evaluation complexity, but for orders $p\geq3$ their Taylor subproblems are nonconvex polynomial optimization problems and are generally difficult to solve. We develop a radius-controlled boundary approach based on homogeneous tensor representations. By augmenting the step with a constant coordinate, any $p$th-order Taylor polynomial can be represented exactly as an order-$p$ homogeneous tensor form; at a prescribed radius, the boundary model is a spherical polynomial optimization problem. The representation applies to arbitrary $p$, while the algorithmic development focuses on the cubic case $p=3$. For an inhomogeneous cubic on the sphere, we introduce a quadratic shift and prove, under an explicit shift bound, equivalence with a three-block multilinear formulation at global optimality. This motivates a proximal alternating minimization (PAM) method with closed-form block updates; its objective values decrease and every accumulation point is stationary. We embed the boundary-step mechanism in an Adaptive Homogeneous Tensor Method (Ada--HTM). Under explicit smoothness, safeguarded-decrease, weak-curvature nondegeneracy, and local-refinement conditions, Ada--HTM attains the adaptive-regularization-type (AR$p$-type) evaluation complexity $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-(p+1)/p})$ for first-order stationarity. Numerically, PAM matches order-$2$ moment--sum-of-squares (SOS) certificates on the structured cubic instances for which certification is tractable, scales particularly well for low-rank tensors, and makes Ada--HTM competitive with trust-region and cubic-regularization methods, with its largest gains on ill-conditioned and badly-scaled problems.

</details>


### [256] [Projected-Gradient Analysis for Open-Domain Convex Optimization under Boundary Blow-Up:Application to Controllability Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23674)
*Kazuhiro Sato*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对定义域内光滑凸但边界处存在爆发现象的紧凸集优化问题，提出了一种域感知的Armijo投影梯度法，在边界吹爆条件下证明了算法收敛性，并应用于可控性评分与输入分配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统凸优化方法要求目标函数在整个可行集上光滑凸，但许多实际问题中目标函数仅在开域内光滑凸，边界处趋于无穷（边界吹爆现象），需要建立适应此类场景的优化理论与算法。

Method: 提出域感知的Armijo投影梯度法，通过安全邻域分析确保目标函数评估的良定义性、有限回退和充分下降，证明存在与迭代无关的正步长下界；在可行位移方向上的正曲率条件下进一步证明唯一性与线性收敛。

Result: 建立了显式的次线性目标函数和驻点保证，以及完整迭代序列的收敛性；在正曲率条件下获得唯一性和线性收敛率。应用于可控性评分时，精确刻画了可行性的充分必要条件（正分配候选输入的可控性），并给出了强凸性界。

Conclusion: 所提框架统一处理了定义域有界开凸集上光滑凸目标函数的优化问题，在可控性评分与输入分配问题中具有应用价值，能揭示候选排除对可行性和最优分配的依赖关系。

Abstract: We study convex optimization over a compact convex set when the objective is smooth and convex only on an open domain. Under a boundary-blow-up condition, every feasible initialization yields a compact invariant sublevel set separated from the complement of the objective domain, and an optimal solution exists. For a domain-aware Armijo projected-gradient method, a safe-neighborhood analysis establishes well-defined objective evaluations, finite backtracking, sufficient decrease, and a run-specific but iteration-independent positive lower bound on the accepted step sizes. These properties yield explicit sublinear objective and stationarity guarantees, together with convergence of the full iterate sequence. Positive curvature restricted to feasible displacement directions further guarantees uniqueness and linear convergence. We apply the framework to controllability scoring with prescribed input directions and compact convex allocation constraints. Feasibility is characterized exactly by controllability of the input directions eligible for positive allocation, while restricted injectivity of the Gramian map guarantees uniqueness of the optimal allocation and provides explicit strong-convexity bounds. A directed-network example illustrates how candidate exclusion can preserve or destroy feasibility and alter the optimal allocation in a criterion- and horizon-dependent manner.

</details>


### [257] [Closed-loop solvability of infinite-horizon stochastic linear-quadratic problem for Markov regime-switching jump-diffusion system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24004)
*Kai Ding,Fan Wu,Jie Xiong,Xinyue Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了马尔可夫机制切换跳扩散系统在无限时域下的一类随机线性二次控制问题，其中状态过程的跳跃完全由马尔可夫链的状态转换诱导，区别于经典的泊松跳扩散模型。通过耦合代数Riccati方程的镇定解，给出了最优控制的反馈表示，并将其应用于终身财富跟踪问题以推导最优投资策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究中，马尔可夫机制切换系统的跳跃通常由独立于机制切换的泊松过程驱动，而本文希望研究一种更贴合实际的场景：状态过程的跳跃完全由马尔可夫链本身的状态转换引起（即机制切换瞬间带来的瞬时收益或损失），并探讨此类系统的随机线性二次控制问题在无限时域下的可解性。

Method: 建立马尔可夫机制切换跳扩散系统的SLQ控制模型，其中跳跃由马尔可夫链状态转换同步诱导；利用耦合代数Riccati方程（CAREs）的镇定解来刻画最优控制的反馈表示；从闭环可解性角度进行系统分析。

Result: 给出了该SLQ控制问题闭环可解性的充分条件，并通过耦合代数Riccati方程的镇定解获得了最优控制的状态反馈形式。进一步将理论结果应用于终身财富跟踪问题，得到了相应的最优投资策略。

Conclusion: 本文提出的模型及其控制框架为机制切换诱导跳跃的系统提供了有效的控制方法，并验证了其在金融投资领域（如终身财富跟踪）的应用价值。

Abstract: This paper investigates a class of stochastic linear-quadratic (SLQ) control problems over an infinite horizon for Markov regime-switching jump-diffusion systems. Unlike classical diffusion models modulated by a Markov chain, we assume that the state process undergoes abrupt jumps that are synchronous with the regime switches of the Markov chain. In contrast to conventional Poisson jump-diffusion models, the jumps in the state process are entirely induced by the state transitions of the Markov chain, which can be interpreted as losses or gains of state process incurred during regime changes. Under this formulation, we thoroughly discuss the closed-loop solvability of the SLQ control problem and provide a feedback representation of the optimal control via the stabilizing solution of a system of coupled algebraic Riccati equations (CAREs). Finally, we further apply our results to a lifetime wealth tracking problem and derive the corresponding optimal investment strategy.

</details>


### [258] [Smooth Learning with Hard Constraints via Legendre-Regularized Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24007)
*Zikun Lin,Rui Chen,Yijie Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出Legendre正则化策略，将决策参数化为原始可行域上正则化优化问题的解，该策略天然满足可行性约束、可微分且具有通用逼近能力，在上下文报童和资源分配问题上优于基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文优化方法在策略类设计上存在不足：要么难以同时满足表达力丰富、硬约束可行性和梯度训练平滑性三个要求，往往只侧重其中一部分。

Method: 提出Legendre正则化策略，将决策参数化为原始可行域上正则化优化问题的解，利用Legendre型正则化项使优化器映射为单值、可微、Lipschitz连续且可任意光滑，并证明该策略类可在紧致上下文集上通用逼近任意连续可行策略。

Result: 在上下文报童问题和资源分配问题上的实验表明，该方法相比基准方法提升了预测性决策性能（prescriptive performance）。

Conclusion: Legendre正则化策略统一了显式正则化优化器和隐式扰动平滑优化器，为上下文优化提供了一种兼具可行性、可微性与表达力的策略类设计框架。

Abstract: We revisit contextual optimization from the perspective of policy class design. A desirable policy class should be expressive enough to learn rich context-decision relationships, should enforce hard feasibility constraints rather than soft penalty terms, and should remain smooth enough for gradient-based training on downstream decision losses. Existing approaches usually emphasize only part of these requirements. We propose Legendre-regularized policies, which parameterize decisions as solutions of regularized optimization problems over the original feasible region. This construction yields policies that are feasible by construction and differentiable with respect to learned latent parameters. We prove that the associated optimizer map is single-valued, maps onto the relative interior of the feasible set, admits an explicit Jacobian, is Lipschitz continuous, and can be made arbitrarily smooth. We also establish a universal approximation result showing that the proposed class can approximate any continuous feasible policy on compact context sets. The framework unifies explicitly regularized optimizers and implicit perturbation-based smooth optimizers. Experiments on contextual newsvendor and resource allocation problems show that our approach improves prescriptive performance relative to the benchmark methods.

</details>


### [259] [Stochastic Control of Addiction with State-Dependent Jump Relapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24099)
*Dounia Aissi,Ioana Ciotir,Dan Goreac,Juan Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个连续时间理性成瘾模型，其中成瘾资本遵循具有状态依赖跳跃（捕获复发和恢复）的分段确定性马尔可夫过程。在带上限的有界控制情形下，获得了唯一有界粘性解，并证明最优策略为bang-bang类型；在无上限情形下，推导了显式线性反馈控制和闭式价值函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将理性成瘾模型扩展到连续时间框架，并引入具有状态依赖跳跃的随机过程来更真实地刻画成瘾行为中的复发与恢复现象。

Method: 采用分段确定性马尔可夫过程建模成瘾资本动态，通过带有非局部跳跃项的HJB方程求解最优控制问题，分别分析有界控制和无界控制两种情形。

Result: 有界控制情形下得到唯一有界粘性解，最优策略为bang-bang切换；无界控制情形下获得显式线性反馈控制和闭式价值函数。

Conclusion: 该模型能够有效刻画成瘾行为中的复发与恢复机制，并在不同控制约束下给出可解析处理的最优策略形式。

Abstract: We study a continuous-time rational addiction model where addiction capital follows a piecewise deterministic Markov process with state-dependent jumps capturing relapse and recovery. The instantaneous utility combines consumption and addiction capital via a power specification, leading to Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (HJB) equations with nonlocal jump terms. In the capped, bounded-control case we obtain a unique bounded viscosity solution and prove that optimal policies are bang-bang, switching between minimal and maximal consumption, while in the uncapped case we derive explicit linear feedback controls and closed-form value functions in several parameter regimes.

</details>


### [260] [Optimal Control with Expectation Constraint in a Smooth Boundary Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24114)
*Bruno Bouchard,Lucas Gnecco Heredia,Ludovic Moreau,Kim-Anh Pham*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究带期望约束的效用最大化问题，提出两种近似方法（均匀椭圆情形下的截断论证和退化情形下的加噪近似），证明收敛性，并利用神经网络进行数值求解和误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对带期望约束的随机控制问题进行完整的理论分析，为该类问题的数值求解奠定基础。

Method: 1) 均匀椭圆情形：通过内源状态边界的光滑性导出Dirichlet条件，提出鞅表示中的截断论证，构造PDE辅助系统逼近；2) 退化情形：添加小噪声项恢复均匀椭圆性；3) 数值求解：采用神经网络方法并结合神经网络误差估计。

Result: 两种近似方法均证明收敛到原最优控制问题；在玩具示例上通过神经网络实现了数值求解和误差估计。

Conclusion: 首次对带期望约束的控制问题进行了完整的理论分析，为数值方案的应用打开了大门。

Abstract: As in Bouchard et al. (2010) and Bouchard and Nutz (2014), we study a utility maximization problem with expectation constraint. We first consider a uniformly elliptic case in which the endogenous state boundary associated with the constraint in expectation is proved to be smooth. This allows one to derive a proper Dirichlet condition for the value function of the optimal control problem on this boundary. We then propose a new truncation argument in the martingale representation of the expectation constraint. This leads to an approximating sequence of auxiliary systems of PDEs for which comparison holds. Convergence to the initial optimal control problem is proved. In the degenerate case, we propose another approximation which consists in adding a small noise term to recover uniformly ellipticity. Convergence is also proved. To the best of our knowledge, it is the first time that a full analysis is performed for such control problems, so as to open the doors to the use of numerical schemes. Numerical resolution in a toy example is performed using neural networks. It is complemented by an estimation of the numerical error, also performed by using a neural network approach.

</details>


### [261] [On the hardness of deterministic second-order optimization of functions with Lipschitz gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24120)
*Jiewen Guan,Anthony Man-Cho So*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了确定性零尊重算法无法在有限次二阶预言机调用内计算Goldstein近似二阶稳定点，而一般确定性算法则无法在不超过n-3次调用内完成（n为输入维度），从而表明确定性二阶弱凸优化是难解的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索确定性算法在二阶弱凸优化中的计算极限，特别是关于Goldstein近似二阶稳定点的可计算性。

Method: 通过构造反例和复杂度下界分析，分别针对零尊重算法和一般确定性算法，证明其在有限次（或最多n-3次）二阶预言机调用内无法计算Goldstein近似二阶稳定点。

Result: 确定性零尊重算法无法在有限次调用内完成任务；一般确定性算法需要至少n-2次调用才能保证找到近似二阶稳定点。

Conclusion: 确定性二阶弱凸优化在标准预言机模型下是本质困难的，现有方法无法高效求解。

Abstract: We show that no deterministic zero-respecting algorithm (resp., (general) deterministic algorithm) can compute Goldstein approximate second-order stationary points of functions with Lipschitz continuous gradients within a finite number of (resp., no more than $n-3$ with $n$ being the input dimension) second-order oracle calls. This, among other consequences, shows that deterministic second-order weakly convex optimization is intractable.

</details>


### [262] [On computing Goldstein approximate second-order stationary points of structured nonsmooth nonconvex programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24122)
*Jiewen Guan,Anthony Man-Cho So*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种随机一阶算法，用于计算L-光滑函数的Goldstein近似二阶稳定点，利用随机平滑技术，实现了$\widetilde{O}({ n^2}/{\varepsilon^9}+{ n^3}/{\varepsilon^7})$的oracle复杂度，并推广到弱凸函数及双层优化应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统一阶方法难以保证二阶稳定点的收敛性，而二阶方法计算开销大。本文旨在设计一种仅依赖一阶信息但能逼近二阶稳定点的高效随机算法，降低oracle复杂度。

Method: 利用随机平滑（randomized smoothing）技术，将原始非光滑或高阶信息转化为可通过一阶oracle近似的形式，结合Goldstein近似稳定点的定义，设计随机一阶算法迭代求解。

Result: 算法实现oracle复杂度$\widetilde{O}({ n^2}/{\varepsilon^9}+{ n^3}/{\varepsilon^7})$，并成功推广至弱凸函数及双层优化场景。

Conclusion: 该工作表明随机平滑工具能有效桥接一阶与二阶优化目标，为高维非凸优化提供新的理论效率保证，并在弱凸和双层优化中展示适用性。

Abstract: In this paper, we exhibit a randomized first-order algorithm to compute Goldstein approximate second-order stationary points of $L$-smooth functions, using tools from randomized smoothing. The algorithm has oracle complexity $\widetilde{O}({ n^2}/{\varepsilon^9}+{ n^3}/{\varepsilon^7})$, where $n=1,2,\ldots$ is the input dimension and $\varepsilon>0$ is the (common) tolerance. We also present extensions to weakly convex functions and applications to bilevel optimization.

</details>


### [263] [Optimal Rank Selection for Nonnegative Matrix Factorization via $\ell_{2,0}$-Norm Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24193)
*Yulin He,Ran Gu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出使用列ℓ₂,₀范数正则化同时作用于两个因子矩阵来解决非负矩阵分解中的秩选择问题。通过暖启动的λ路径从过估计的分解维度出发逐步消除冗余成分，并开发了惯性近端交替线性化最小化方法及其尺度平衡变体、基于P-稳定性的近端积极集方法。理论分析证明了模型恢复潜在非负秩的条件以及算法的收敛性，实验表明该方法在秩估计精度和计算效率上优于奇异值硬阈值和交叉验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非负矩阵分解中确定最优分解秩是一个基础但极具挑战性的问题，传统方法依赖启发式阈值或计算昂贵的交叉验证，缺乏系统且高效的理论框架。

Method: 在非负矩阵分解中引入列ℓ₂,₀范数正则化同时约束两个因子矩阵以实现秩缩减；采用暖启动λ路径从过估计维度出发渐进消除冗余成分；开发惯性近端交替线性化最小化（iPALM）及其尺度平衡变体、基于P-稳定性的近端积极集方法；引入尺度平衡策略控制因子矩阵列对间的数值不平衡。

Result: 建立了模型恢复潜在非负秩的理论条件；基于Kurdyka–Łojasiewicz框架证明了算法生成的整个序列收敛到临界点；在合成数据和基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法在秩估计精度和计算效率上优于奇异值硬阈值和交叉验证。

Conclusion: 所提出的正则化框架能够从数据驱动的方式估计非负矩阵分解的最优秩，在理论保证和实际性能上均优于传统方法。

Abstract: Determining the optimal factorization rank is a fundamental yet notoriously challenging problem in Nonnegative Matrix Factorization, conventionally relying on heuristic thresholds or computationally expensive cross-validation. We introduce column $\ell_{2,0}$-norm regularization on both factor matrices to promote rank reduction. Starting from an overestimated factorization dimension, a warm-started $λ$-path is employed to progressively eliminate redundant components and produce a data-driven rank estimate. For the resulting nonconvex and discontinuous optimization problem, we develop an inertial Proximal Alternating Linearized Minimization method, its scale-balanced variant, and a Proximal Active-Set Method based on P-stationarity. The scale-balancing strategy controls numerical imbalance between paired factor columns while preserving the reconstructed matrix. We establish conditions under which the proposed model recovers the underlying nonnegative rank and provide a Kurdyka--Łojasiewicz-based convergence analysis showing that, under the stated algorithmic conditions, the entire sequences generated by the proposed methods converge to critical points. Numerical experiments on synthetic and benchmark datasets, together with comparisons against Singular Value Hard Thresholding and cross-validation, show that the proposed framework provides favorable rank-estimation accuracy and computational efficiency in the tested settings.

</details>


### [264] [A Nonlinear CTH-Based Adaptive Cruise Controller With Safety and String Stability Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24228)
*Dionysios Theodosis,Amirhossein Samii,Nikolaos Bekiaris-Liberis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对异构车辆队列，提出了一种基于恒定车头时距（CTH）策略的非线性自适应巡航控制器，该控制器通过速度相关非线性增益和有界加速度约束，在保证防碰撞和队列弦稳定性的同时，实现了间距误差的收敛，并直接推导了L_p弦稳定的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有车辆队列控制方法在保证安全性（防碰撞）和队列弦稳定性方面存在不足，尤其是针对异构车辆和非线性动态特性的分析往往依赖线性化或频域方法，缺乏直接的非线性理论保证。

Method: 设计基于CTH策略的非线性自适应巡航控制器，引入速度相关非线性增益限制车速，并采用饱和型非线性约束加速度；通过定义合适的间距误差变量，建立显式非线性误差动态方程，直接分析闭环系统。

Result: 证明了碰撞自由区域的正不变性以及间距误差的收敛性；直接从闭环动态推导了非线性弦稳定性，给出了L_p弦稳定性（1≤p≤∞）的充分条件。

Conclusion: 所提出的非线性控制器能够在不依赖线性化或频域分析的前提下，同时保证异构车辆队列的防碰撞安全性和弦稳定性，为车辆队列控制提供了严格的理论基础。

Abstract: This paper studies the problem of longitudinal control, with safety and string stability guarantees, in vehicle platoons. A nonlinear adaptive cruise controller based on the Constant Time Headway (CTH) policy is designed for platoons of heterogeneous vehicles, that incorporates a speed-dependent nonlinear gain guaranteeing bounded vehicle speeds and a saturation-type nonlinearity to guarantee bounded (or non-aggressive) accelerations, while preserving collision-avoidance properties of time-headway-based spacing policies. By introducing a suitable spacing-error variable, explicit nonlinear error dynamics are obtained, allowing a direct analysis of the closed-loop system. It is shown that the collision-free region is positively invariant and that the proposed controller guarantees convergence of the spacing errors. Furthermore, nonlinear string-stability properties are established directly from the closed-loop dynamics without resorting to linearization or frequency-domain arguments. In particular, sufficient conditions are derived for $L_p$ string stability, $1\le p\le\infty$, with respect to disturbances, external inputs acting on the leading vehicle, and perturbations of the initial conditions.

</details>


### [265] [Optimal Convex Optimization with Inexact Second-Order Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24520)
*Lesi Chen,Chengchang Liu,Luo Luo,John C. S. Lui,Jingzhao Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为加速非精确牛顿外梯度法（AINE）的二阶优化方法，使用δ-非精确Hessian矩阵求解凸优化问题。在Hessian满足L₂-Lipschitz连续时，ISO复杂度为O((δ/ε)^{1/2} + (L₂/ε)^{2/7})；在三阶导数满足L₃-Lipschitz连续时，复杂度提升至O((δ/ε)^{1/2} + (L₃/ε)^{1/5})。每轮迭代运行时间与矩阵乘法同阶（对数因子内），并建立了匹配的oracle复杂度下界，证明方法最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二阶优化方法在处理非精确Hessian信息时存在收敛速度瓶颈，且缺乏对高阶光滑性（如三阶导数Lipschitz连续）条件下复杂度增益的理论分析与匹配下界。

Method: 提出Accelerated Inexact Newton Extragradient (AINE)方法，结合加速外梯度框架与δ-非精确牛顿步，利用δ-非精确Hessian进行迭代更新，并分别针对二阶和三阶光滑性条件设计复杂度分析。

Result: 在Hessian L₂-Lipschitz连续下ISO复杂度为O((δ/ε)^{1/2} + (L₂/ε)^{2/7})；在三阶导数L₃-Lipschitz连续下ISO复杂度为O((δ/ε)^{1/2} + (L₃/ε)^{1/5})。每轮迭代时间复杂度接近矩阵乘法（对数因子内），并给出了匹配的oracle复杂度下界。

Conclusion: AINE方法在非精确Hessian场景下实现了最优的oracle复杂度，且具有高效的迭代实现，为凸优化中的二阶方法提供了理论和实践上的新基准。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a novel second-order method called Accelerated Inexact Newton Extragradient (AINE) for convex optimization using $δ$-inexact Hessians. We show that AINE can find an $ε$-solution in the inexact second-order oracle (ISO) complexity of $\mathcal{O}( (δ/ε)^{1/2} + (L_2/ε)^{2/7} )$ when the Hessian is $L_2$-Lipschitz continuous, and a better complexity of $\mathcal{O}( (δ/ε)^{1/2} + (L_3/ε)^{1/5} )$ when the third-order derivative is $L_3$-Lipschitz continuous. Notably, each iteration of our method can be conducted in the same running time as matrix multiplication up to logarithmic factors. In addition, we also establish matching oracle complexity lower bounds for both setups, demonstrating the optimality of our methods.

</details>


### [266] [Using Subproblem Objective Gaps in Inexact Augmented Lagrangian and ADMM Algorithms, with Applications to Stochastic Mixed Integer Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24535)
*Jonathan Eckstein*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过“部分强凸性”引理，展示了如何将子问题目标值次优性界限用于不精确增广拉格朗日方法和ADMM算法。ADMM结果对长期存在的子问题近似求解准则进行了重要改进。这些成果为随机混合整数规划问题的最优值计算提供了两种新的拉格朗日界限方法，收敛分析比先前方法更简洁。每种情况下子问题均由经典Frank-Wolfe算法的变体求解，但与先前同类方法相比，Frank-Wolfe变体的选择自由度更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为随机混合整数规划问题提供更高效、更灵活的最优值拉格朗日界限计算方法，同时简化收敛分析。

Method: 利用“部分强凸性”引理，将子问题目标值次优性界限融入不精确增广拉格朗日方法和ADMM算法；对ADMM中子问题近似求解准则进行精细改进；子问题采用Frank-Wolfe算法变体求解。

Result: 提出了两种新的拉格朗日界限计算方法，收敛分析更简洁；Frank-Wolfe变体选择自由度显著提升。

Conclusion: 本文的理论贡献在于扩展了不精确优化方法在随机混合整数规划中的应用，并为Frank-Wolfe算法在该类问题中的使用提供了更大的灵活性。

Abstract: Through a "partial strong convexity" lemma, this paper shows how bounds on subproblem objective value suboptimality can be used in inexact augmented Lagrangian methods and ADMM algorithms. The ADMM result uses a small but important refinement on a long-standing criterion for approximately solving subproblems. The results enable two new approaches to computing Lagrangian bounds on the optimal values of stochastic mixed-integer programming problems, with simpler convergence analysis than the prior state of the art. In each case, the subproblems are solved by variants of the classical Frank-Wolfe algorithm. However, as compared to prior methods of the same type, there is much more freedom in the choice of Frank-Wolfe variant.

</details>


### [267] [Communication Security via Temporal Dependency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24578)
*Mohsen Abedi,Ahmed Badawy,Amr Mohamed*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用时间依赖作为安全资源的新型通信安全范式，通过状态链式随机线性网络编码（RLNC）使得窃听者即使能解码数据包也无法正确解读，实现无需共享密钥或信道优势的安全通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统通信安全依赖共享密钥或信道优势，但在无基础设施、应急和高度动态等实际无线场景中，这两种资源均无法保证，亟需新的安全通信原则。

Method: 提出状态链式RLNC框架：将每个传输块所需的同步状态嵌入前一个块中，使原始包与伪包在计算上不可区分；通过发射功率和有意干扰优化传输策略，实现窃听者同步失败且失效沿未来传输传播。

Result: 在无信道优势、无秘密假设、完全协议已知且窃听者任意强的对抗模型下，数值结果实现亚秒级窃听者失同步。

Conclusion: 时间依赖可作为独立的安全资源，为无法保证传统安全资源的无线场景提供了可行的安全通信新范式。

Abstract: Communication security has traditionally been built upon one of two external resources: shared secret keys or a communication advantage over the eavesdropper. However, many practical wireless scenarios, including infrastructure-less, emergency, and highly dynamic networks, cannot guarantee either resource, motivating the need for a new communication security principle. This paper introduces a new communication security paradigm that exploits temporal dependency as a security resource. Unlike conventional secrecy techniques that prevent an eavesdropper from recovering transmitted bits, the proposed paradigm allows packet decoding but prevents correct interpretation by making original and dummy packets computationally indistinguishable. Rather than protecting individual transmissions, successive transmissions are intentionally coupled so that future communication depends on correctly interpreting previous ones. As one realization, we develop a state-chained random linear network coding (RLNC) framework in which the synchronization state required to interpret each transmission block is embedded in the previous block. Therefore, synchronization failures propagate across future transmissions, resulting in persistent eavesdropper asynchronization. We analytically characterize the probability and persistence of eavesdropper asynchronization, together with the computational complexity of resynchronization, and develop transmission strategies based on transmit power and intentional-interference optimization. Numerical results demonstrate sub-second eavesdropper asynchronization under a worst- case adversarial model with no channel advantage, no secret assumptions, complete protocol knowledge, and an arbitrarily stronger eavesdropper.

</details>


### [268] [Fixed-time stabilization of systems with nilpotent matrices in the presence of large unknown delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24595)
*Kang-Kang Zhang,Emilia Fridman,Pengfei Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在未知大常数输入/测量时滞（已知界）情况下，具有幂零矩阵的连续时间受扰线性系统的状态反馈固定时间镇定问题。通过引入额外人工时滞，所设计的反馈能在精确已知的幂零线性系统中，在大于未知时滞界的固定时间内将状态驱动至零，并在存在加性扰动、小系统矩阵摄动及非线性时实现具有大指数衰减率的输入-状态稳定性。对于受扰单积分器，在具有已知界的快变未知测量时滞下实现固定时间镇定。结果扩展至离散时间系统。数值仿真验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有固定时间控制理论大多假设系统状态完全已知且无时滞，或仅能处理已知/小规模时滞。实际系统中常存在未知但有界的输入/测量时滞，这对固定时间镇定构成根本性挑战。本文旨在解决具有幂零矩阵结构的受扰线性系统在未知大常数时滞下的固定时间镇定问题。

Method: 采用人工引入额外时滞的反馈设计方法，利用幂零矩阵的有限步零化特性，构造依赖于时滞上界的预测型状态反馈律，通过Lyapunov-Krasovskii泛函分析固定时间收敛性与输入-状态稳定性。

Result: (1) 对于精确已知的幂零线性系统，所设计反馈能在大于未知时滞界的固定时间内将状态驱动至零；(2) 在加性扰动、系统矩阵小摄动和非线性存在时，系统具有输入-状态稳定性且指数衰减率可任意大；(3) 对单积分器系统，在快变未知测量时滞下仍能实现固定时间镇定；(4) 结果成功推广至离散时间系统。

Conclusion: 本文首次为具有未知大常数时滞的幂零线性系统提供了固定时间镇定方案，理论结果兼具鲁棒性（对扰动和摄动）与普适性（覆盖连续和离散时间、快变时滞情形），数值仿真验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: In this paper, we study state-feedback fixed-time stabilization of continuous-time perturbed linear systems with nilpotent matrices in the presence of unknown large constant input/measurement delays with known bounds. By using an additional artificial delay, we design a feedback that for a precisely known linear system with a nilpotent matrix drives the state to zero in fixed time (which is larger than the bound of the unknown delay) and achieves input-to-state stability with a large exponential decay rate in the presence of additive disturbances and small system matrix perturbations and nonlinearities. For perturbed single integrators, fixed-time stabilization is achieved in the case of fast-varying unknown measurement delays with known bounds. The results are extended to discrete-time systems. Numerical simulations illustrate the efficiency of the results.

</details>


### [269] [Long-time Stability and Convergence of Particle Swarm Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24696)
*Giacomo Borghi,Hui Huang,Dohyeon Kim*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文连接了经典PSO停滞假设下的稳定性分析与平均场方法，通过研究带噪声底限的无记忆正则化PSO模型，分析了离散时间算法的稳定性、收敛性及平均场逼近的误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PSO作为全局优化算法，其理论分析因动力学的高阶、随机和高度非线性特性而受限，需要更严格的理论基础。

Method: 引入正则化无记忆PSO模型，添加非退化噪声底限；通过线性化动力学的Schur稳定性分析、Laplace原理分析非线性平均场系统的收敛性，以及建立N^{-1/2}阶的平均场逼近误差界。

Result: 识别出动力学稳定并收敛到全局极小点邻域的定量条件；证明了平均场逼近误差为O(N^{-1/2})。

Conclusion: 所提出的正则化PSO模型在理论上可实现稳定收敛，平均场近似有效且误差可控。

Abstract: Particle Swarm Optimization (PSO) is a global optimization algorithm defined by an interacting set of particles evolving over the search space. Heuristically motivated, its theoretical analysis remains limited due to the second-order, stochastic, and highly nonlinear nature of the dynamics. In this paper, we connect classical PSO stability analysis under the stagnation assumption with more recent mean-field methods, providing new quantitative estimates for the time-discrete algorithm. We study in particular a regularized PSO model without memory, with non-degenerate noise by adding a noise floor to the original model. Studying such a surrogate model allows us to identify quantitative conditions under which the dynamics is stable and converges toward a small neighborhood of a global minimizer. We do so by first studying the Schur stability of the linearized dynamics, then analyzing the convergence properties of a nonlinear mean-field system via a Laplace principle, and finally establishing a quantitative error bound for the mean-field approximation of order $N^{-1/2}$.

</details>
